package com.remisoft.comm.queue.annotation;

import com.remisoft.comm.queue.config.QueueConfiguration;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Import;

import java.lang.annotation.Documented;
import java.lang.annotation.ElementType;
import java.lang.annotation.Retention;
import java.lang.annotation.RetentionPolicy;
import java.lang.annotation.Target;

/**
 * 鍚敤鐟炵背娑堟伅闃熷垪妯″潡
 * <p>鍦⊿pring Boot搴旂敤涓荤被涓婃坊鍔犳娉ㄨВ锛屽惎鐢ㄥ娑堟伅涓棿浠堕€傞厤鑳藉姏
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 * @see QueueConfiguration
 */
@Target(ElementType.TYPE)
@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
@Documented
@Import(QueueConfiguration.class)
public @interface EnableRemiQueue {
}

package com.remisoft.comm.queue.config;

import com.remisoft.comm.queue.manager.QueueManager;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.queue.IMessageQueueProvider;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.queue.MessageQueueFactory;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.scheduler.DeadLetterRetryScheduler;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.DeadLetterQueueService;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.impl.DeadLetterQueueServiceImpl;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.impl.NoOpDeadLetterQueueService;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.trace.DefaultMessageTraceRecorder;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.trace.MessageTraceAspect;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.trace.MessageTraceRecorder;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.trace.RedisMessageTraceRecorder;
import com.remisoft.comm.redis.service.RedisService;
import jakarta.annotation.PostConstruct;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.ObjectProvider;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.AutoConfiguration;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnMissingBean;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnProperty;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.EnableConfigurationProperties;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisTemplate;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.EnableScheduling;
import org.springframework.scheduling.concurrent.ThreadPoolTaskExecutor;

import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.ThreadPoolExecutor;
/**
 * 娑堟伅闃熷垪鑷姩閰嶇疆绫? *
 * <p>鎻愪緵娑堟伅闃熷垪妯″潡鐨?Spring Boot 鑷姩閰嶇疆鑳藉姏銆? * 褰撻厤缃」 {@code remi.queue.enabled=true} 鏃惰嚜鍔ㄥ惎鐢ㄣ€? *
 * <p><b>Redis 杩炴帴澶嶇敤锛?/b>
 * 浼樺厛浣跨敤 remi-comm-redis 鐨?{@link RedisService} 澶嶇敤 Redis 杩炴帴锛? * 褰?RedisService 涓嶅彲鐢ㄦ椂鍥為€€鍒?QueueProperties 涓殑杩炴帴閰嶇疆鑷缓 JedisPool銆? *
 * <p><b>閰嶇疆绀轰緥锛?/b>
 * <pre>{@code
 * # application.yml
 * spring:
 *   data:
 *     redis:
 *       host: 127.0.0.1
 *       port: 6379
 * remi:
 *   queue:
 *     enabled: true
 *     stream-group: remi-group
 * }</pre>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
@AutoConfiguration
@EnableConfigurationProperties(QueueProperties.class)
@EnableScheduling
@ConditionalOnProperty(prefix = "remi.queue", name = "enabled", havingValue = "true", matchIfMissing = true)
public class QueueConfiguration {

    private final QueueProperties queueProperties;
    private final ObjectProvider<RedisService> redisServiceProvider;

    public QueueConfiguration(QueueProperties queueProperties,
                               ObjectProvider<RedisService> redisServiceProvider) {
        this.queueProperties = queueProperties;
        this.redisServiceProvider = redisServiceProvider;
    }

    /**
     * 鍒濆鍖栭獙璇?     * <p>鍦?Spring 瀹瑰櫒鍒濆鍖栧畬鎴愬悗楠岃瘉閰嶇疆鐨勫熀鏈湁鏁堟€?     */
    @PostConstruct
    public void init() {
        RedisService redisService = redisServiceProvider.getIfAvailable();
        if (redisService != null) {
            log.info("[Queue] 娑堟伅闃熷垪閰嶇疆鍒濆鍖栵紝Redis 杩炴帴锛氬鐢?remi-comm-redis");
        } else {
            log.info("[Queue] 娑堟伅闃熷垪閰嶇疆鍒濆鍖栵紝Redis 杩炴帴锛氳嚜寤?JedisPool锛坽}:{}锛?,
                    queueProperties.resolvedHost(),
                    queueProperties.resolvedPort());
            log.info("[Queue] 鎻愮ず锛氭帹鑽愬紩鍏?remi-comm-redis 妯″潡浠ュ鐢?Redis 杩炴帴锛? +
                    "閫氳繃 spring.data.redis.* 閰嶇疆杩炴帴淇℃伅");
        }
        log.debug("[Queue] 闃熷垪閰嶇疆璇︽儏锛歿}", queueProperties);
    }

    /**
     * 鍒涘缓娑堟伅闃熷垪绠＄悊鍣?     *
     * <p>鎻愪緵缁熶竴鐨勯槦鍒楃敓鍛藉懆鏈熺鐞嗗拰鐩戞帶鎸囨爣鏀堕泦銆?     * 璐熻矗娉ㄥ唽銆佹煡璇€佺Щ闄ら槦鍒楀疄渚嬪強鍏跺搴旂殑鐩戞帶鎸囨爣銆?     *
     * @return QueueManager 瀹炰緥
     */
    @Bean
    @ConditionalOnMissingBean(QueueManager.class)
    public QueueManager queueManager() {
        log.info("[Queue] 鍒涘缓娑堟伅闃熷垪绠＄悊鍣?);
        return new QueueManager();
    }

    /**
     * 鍒涘缓娑堟伅闃熷垪寮傛娑堣垂鑰呯嚎绋嬫睜锛圫pring 绠＄悊锛屾敮鎸佷紭闆呭仠鏈猴級
     *
     * <p>缁熶竴鎵樼鎵€鏈夋秷鎭槦鍒楀紓姝ユ秷璐硅€呯殑鎵ц绾跨▼锛岄伩鍏嶄笟鍔′唬鐮佺洿鎺?new Thread銆?     *
     * @param queueProperties 闃熷垪閰嶇疆灞炴€?     * @return 娑堣垂鑰呯嚎绋嬫睜
     */
    @Bean("queueConsumerExecutor")
    @ConditionalOnMissingBean(name = "queueConsumerExecutor")
    public ExecutorService queueConsumerExecutor(QueueProperties queueProperties) {
        QueueProperties.ExecutorConfig cfg = queueProperties.resolvedConsumerExecutor();
        ThreadPoolTaskExecutor executor = new ThreadPoolTaskExecutor();
        executor.setCorePoolSize(cfg.getCoreSize());
        executor.setMaxPoolSize(cfg.getMaxSize());
        executor.setQueueCapacity(cfg.getQueueCapacity());
        executor.setThreadNamePrefix(cfg.getThreadNamePrefix());
        executor.setRejectedExecutionHandler(new ThreadPoolExecutor.CallerRunsPolicy());
        executor.setWaitForTasksToCompleteOnShutdown(true);
        executor.setAwaitTerminationSeconds(cfg.getAwaitTerminationSeconds());
        executor.initialize();
        log.info("[Queue] 鍒涘缓寮傛娑堣垂鑰呯嚎绋嬫睜锛宑ore={}, max={}, queue={}",
                cfg.getCoreSize(), cfg.getMaxSize(), cfg.getQueueCapacity());
        return executor.getThreadPoolExecutor();
    }

    /**
     * 鍒涘缓娑堟伅闃熷垪鎻愪緵鑰?     *
     * <p>娑堟伅闃熷垪鎻愪緵鑰呰礋璐ｆ牴鎹?QueueType 鍒涘缓瀵瑰簲鐨勯槦鍒楀疄渚嬨€?     * 浼樺厛娉ㄥ叆 remi-comm-redis 鐨?{@link RedisService} 澶嶇敤杩炴帴锛?     * 褰?RedisService 涓嶅彲鐢ㄦ椂鍥為€€鍒拌嚜寤?JedisPool 杩炴帴銆?     *
     * @param consumerExecutor 娑堣垂鑰呯嚎绋嬫睜
     * @return 娑堟伅闃熷垪鎻愪緵鑰呭疄渚?     */
    @Bean
    @ConditionalOnMissingBean(IMessageQueueProvider.class)
    public IMessageQueueProvider messageQueueProvider(ExecutorService consumerExecutor) {
        RedisService redisService = redisServiceProvider.getIfAvailable();
        if (redisService != null) {
            log.info("[Queue] 鍒涘缓娑堟伅闃熷垪鎻愪緵鑰咃紙澶嶇敤 remi-comm-redis 杩炴帴锛?);
            return new MessageQueueFactory(queueProperties, redisService, consumerExecutor);
        }
        log.info("[Queue] 鍒涘缓娑堟伅闃熷垪鎻愪緵鑰咃紙鑷缓 JedisPool 杩炴帴锛?);
        return new MessageQueueFactory(queueProperties, null, consumerExecutor);
    }

    /**
     * 鍒涘缓姝讳俊闃熷垪鏈嶅姟
     *
     * <p>褰?RedisService 鍙敤鏃讹紝鍒涘缓鍩轰簬 Redis 鐨勬淇￠槦鍒楁湇鍔″疄渚嬨€?     *
     * @param messageQueueProvider 娑堟伅闃熷垪鎻愪緵鑰?     * @return 姝讳俊闃熷垪鏈嶅姟瀹炰緥
     */
    @Bean
    @ConditionalOnMissingBean(DeadLetterQueueService.class)
    public DeadLetterQueueService deadLetterQueueService(IMessageQueueProvider messageQueueProvider) {
        RedisService redisService = redisServiceProvider.getIfAvailable();
        if (redisService != null) {
            log.info("[Queue] 鍒涘缓姝讳俊闃熷垪鏈嶅姟锛堝鐢?remi-comm-redis 杩炴帴锛?);
            RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate = redisService.getRedisTemplate();
            return new DeadLetterQueueServiceImpl(redisTemplate, messageQueueProvider, queueProperties);
        }
        log.warn("[Queue] RedisService 涓嶅彲鐢紝杩斿洖绌烘搷浣滄淇￠槦鍒楁湇鍔?);
        return new NoOpDeadLetterQueueService();
    }

    /**
     * 鍒涘缓姝讳俊闃熷垪鑷姩閲嶈瘯璋冨害鍣?     *
     * <p>褰撴淇￠槦鍒楁湇鍔″彲鐢ㄤ笖鍚敤浜嗚嚜鍔ㄩ噸璇曟椂锛屽垱寤哄畾鏃惰皟搴﹀櫒瀹炰緥銆?     *
     * @param deadLetterQueueService 姝讳俊闃熷垪鏈嶅姟
     * @return 姝讳俊闃熷垪閲嶈瘯璋冨害鍣ㄥ疄渚?     */
    @Bean
    @ConditionalOnMissingBean(DeadLetterRetryScheduler.class)
    public DeadLetterRetryScheduler deadLetterRetryScheduler(DeadLetterQueueService deadLetterQueueService) {
        if (deadLetterQueueService != null
                && !(deadLetterQueueService instanceof NoOpDeadLetterQueueService)
                && queueProperties.resolvedDeadLetterRetryEnabled()) {
            log.info("[Queue] 鍒涘缓姝讳俊闃熷垪鑷姩閲嶈瘯璋冨害鍣紝闂撮殧: {}ms",
                    queueProperties.resolvedDeadLetterRetryInterval());
            return new DeadLetterRetryScheduler(deadLetterQueueService, queueProperties);
        }
        log.info("[Queue] 姝讳俊闃熷垪鑷姩閲嶈瘯宸茬鐢紝璺宠繃璋冨害鍣ㄥ垱寤?);
        return null;
    }

    // ==================== 娑堟伅杞ㄨ抗鐩稿叧閰嶇疆 ====================

    /**
     * 鍒涘缓娑堟伅杞ㄨ抗璁板綍鍣?     *
     * <p>鏍规嵁閰嶇疆鐨勫悗绔被鍨嬪垱寤哄搴旂殑璁板綍鍣ㄥ疄渚嬶細
     * <ul>
     *   <li>memory: 鍩轰簬鍐呭瓨鐨?LRU 缂撳瓨瀹炵幇</li>
     *   <li>redis: 鍩轰簬 Redis Hash 鐨勬寔涔呭寲瀹炵幇</li>
     * </ul>
     *
     * @return 娑堟伅杞ㄨ抗璁板綍鍣ㄥ疄渚?     */
    @Bean
    @ConditionalOnMissingBean(MessageTraceRecorder.class)
    @ConditionalOnProperty(prefix = "remi.queue.trace", name = "enabled", havingValue = "true")
    public MessageTraceRecorder messageTraceRecorder() {
        String backend = queueProperties.getTrace().resolvedBackend();
        int ttlMinutes = queueProperties.getTrace().getTtlMinutes();

        if ("redis".equalsIgnoreCase(backend)) {
            RedisService redisService = redisServiceProvider.getIfAvailable();
            if (redisService != null) {
                log.info("[Queue] 鍒涘缓 Redis 娑堟伅杞ㄨ抗璁板綍鍣紝TTL: {} 鍒嗛挓", ttlMinutes);
                return new RedisMessageTraceRecorder(redisService, ttlMinutes);
            }
            log.warn("[Queue] Redis 娑堟伅杞ㄨ抗鍚庣宸查厤缃絾 RedisService 涓嶅彲鐢紝鍥為€€鍒板唴瀛樻ā寮?);
        }

        int maxCapacity = queueProperties.getTrace().getMaxCapacity();
        log.info("[Queue] 鍒涘缓鍐呭瓨娑堟伅杞ㄨ抗璁板綍鍣紝瀹归噺: {}, TTL: {} 鍒嗛挓", maxCapacity, ttlMinutes);
        return new DefaultMessageTraceRecorder(maxCapacity, ttlMinutes);
    }

    /**
     * 鍒涘缓娑堟伅杞ㄨ抗 AOP 鍒囬潰
     *
     * <p>鎷︽埅 IMessagePublisher.publish() 鏂规硶锛岃嚜鍔ㄨ褰曟秷鎭彂閫佽建杩广€?     *
     * @param traceRecorder 娑堟伅杞ㄨ抗璁板綍鍣?     * @return AOP 鍒囬潰瀹炰緥
     */
    @Bean
    @ConditionalOnProperty(prefix = "remi.queue.trace", name = "enabled", havingValue = "true")
    public MessageTraceAspect messageTraceAspect(MessageTraceRecorder traceRecorder) {
        log.info("[Queue] 娉ㄥ唽娑堟伅杞ㄨ抗 AOP 鍒囬潰");
        return new MessageTraceAspect(traceRecorder);
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\config\QueueProperties.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.config;

import com.remisoft.comm.queue.enums.QueueType;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.queue.MessageQueueFactory;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.queue.IMessageQueueProvider;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.rate.ConsumerRateLimiter;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Min;
import lombok.Data;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.ConfigurationProperties;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 娑堟伅闃熷垪閰嶇疆绫?
 *
 * <p>鏀寔閫氳繃 Nacos 杩涜鍔ㄦ€侀厤缃紝閰嶇疆鍓嶇紑涓?{@code remi.queue}銆?
 * 鏀寔閫氳繃 {@link MessageQueueFactory} 鍒涘缓娑堟伅闃熷垪瀹炰緥锛屽鐢?remi-comm-redis 杩炴帴銆?
 *
 * <p><b>杩炴帴澶嶇敤锛?/b>
 * <p>褰撴彁渚?{@link com.remisoft.comm.redis.service.RedisService} 鏃讹紝Redis 闃熷垪
 * 浼樺厛澶嶇敤 remi-comm-redis 鐨勮繛鎺ワ紝閬垮厤閲嶅鍒涘缓 JedisPool銆?
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
@Data
@ConfigurationProperties(prefix = "remi.queue")
public class QueueProperties {

    /**
     * 闃熷垪鏄惁鍚敤
     */
    private boolean enabled = true;

    /**
     * 闃熷垪绫诲瀷锛堟敮鎸侀€氳繃鏋氫妇鍚嶇О鎴?value 鑷姩璇嗗埆锛?
     * <p>鏀寔鐨勫€硷細LIST, PUBSUB, STREAM, KAFKA, ROCKET, RABBIT, ACTIVE
     */
    private QueueType type;

    /**
     * 闃熷垪绫诲瀷瀛楃涓诧紙鐢ㄤ簬鍏煎鏃ч厤缃級
     * <p>璇ュ瓧娈典粎鐢ㄤ簬閰嶇疆瑙ｆ瀽锛屽疄闄呬娇鐢ㄥ簲閫氳繃 getType() 鑾峰彇鏋氫妇鍊笺€?
     */
    private String typeStr;

    /**
     * Redis 鏈嶅姟鍣ㄥ湴鍧€锛堜篃鐢ㄤ簬闈?Redis 闃熷垪鐨勯€氱敤涓绘満閰嶇疆锛?
     */
    private String host = "127.0.0.1";

    /**
     * Redis 鏈嶅姟鍣ㄧ鍙ｏ紙涔熺敤浜庨潪 Redis 闃熷垪鐨勯€氱敤绔彛閰嶇疆锛?
     */
    private int port = 6379;

    /**
     * Redis 瀵嗙爜锛堜篃鐢ㄤ簬闈?Redis 闃熷垪鐨勯€氱敤瀵嗙爜閰嶇疆锛?
     */
    private String password;

    /**
     * Redis 鐢ㄦ埛鍚嶏紙鐢ㄤ簬闈?Redis 闃熷垪鐨勯€氱敤鐢ㄦ埛鍚嶉厤缃級
     */
    private String username;

    /**
     * 瓒呮椂鏃堕棿锛堟绉掞級
     */
    @Min(1)
    private int timeout = 3000;

    /**
     * List 闃熷垪闃诲瓒呮椂鏃堕棿锛堢锛?
     */
    private long listBlockTimeoutSeconds = 5;

    /**
     * Stream 闃熷垪娑堣垂鑰呯粍
     */
    private String streamGroup = "group-1";

    /**
     * Stream 闃熷垪娑堣垂鑰呭悕绉?
     */
    private String streamConsumer = "consumer-1";

    /**
     * Stream 闃熷垪閲嶈瘯鏈€澶ф鏁?
     */
    private int streamRetryMax = 3;

    /**
     * Stream 闃熷垪闃诲鏃堕棿锛堟绉掞級
     */
    private long streamBlockMillis = 2000;

    /**
     * Stream 闃熷垪鎵归噺鎷夊彇澶у皬
     */
    private int streamBatchSize = 10;

    /**
     * Stream 闃熷垪姝讳俊闃熷垪鍚庣紑
     */
    private String streamDeadLetterSuffix = ":dlq";

    /**
     * 娑堣垂鑰呴檺娴侀€熺巼锛堟瘡绉掑厑璁稿鐞嗙殑娑堟伅鏁帮紝榛樿 0 琛ㄧず涓嶉檺娴侊級
     */
    private int consumerRateLimitPerSecond = 0;

    /**
     * 鏄惁鍚敤姝讳俊闃熷垪鑷姩閲嶈瘯锛堥粯璁rue锛?
     */
    private boolean deadLetterRetryEnabled = true;

    /**
     * 姝讳俊闃熷垪鏈€澶ч噸璇曟鏁帮紙榛樿3锛?
     */
    @Min(1)
    private int deadLetterMaxRetries = 3;

    /**
     * 姝讳俊闃熷垪閲嶈瘯闂撮殧姣锛堥粯璁?0000锛?
     */
    private long deadLetterRetryInterval = 60000;

    /**
     * 瑙ｆ瀽鍚庣殑闃熷垪绫诲瀷
     */
    public QueueType resolvedType() {
        if (type != null) {
            return type;
        }
        if (typeStr != null && !typeStr.trim().isEmpty()) {
            type = QueueType.fromValue(typeStr);
            return type;
        }
        throw new IllegalStateException("闃熷垪绫诲瀷涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
    }

    /**
     * 瑙ｆ瀽鍚庣殑涓绘満鍦板潃
     */
    public String resolvedHost() {
        return host != null ? host : "127.0.0.1";
    }

    /**
     * 瑙ｆ瀽鍚庣殑绔彛
     */
    public int resolvedPort() {
        return port > 0 ? port : 6379;
    }

    /**
     * 瑙ｆ瀽鍚庣殑瓒呮椂鏃堕棿
     */
    public int resolvedTimeout() {
        return timeout > 0 ? timeout : 3000;
    }

    /**

        private int awaitTerminationSeconds = 30;

    @Data

        private String backend = "memory";

        private int ttlMinutes = 30;

        private int maxCapacity = 1000;

            return backend != null && !backend.isEmpty() ? backend : "memory";
        }
    }

    /**
     * 瑙ｆ瀽闃熷垪閰嶇疆椤?
     *
     * @param configStr 鏍煎紡: "KAFKA:host:9092:topicName" 鎴?"STREAM:127.0.0.1:6379:myGroup"
     * @return 瑙ｆ瀽鍚庣殑閰嶇疆瀵硅薄
     */
    public static QueueProperties parse(String configStr) {
        QueueProperties properties = new QueueProperties();
        properties.setEnabled(true);

        if (configStr == null || configStr.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("闃熷垪閰嶇疆瀛楃涓蹭笉鑳戒负绌?);
        }

        String[] parts = configStr.split(":", 4);
        if (parts.length < 2) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("闃熷垪閰嶇疆鏍煎紡閿欒锛屽簲涓? TYPE:HOST:PORT:...");
        }

        properties.setType(QueueType.valueOf(parts[0].toUpperCase()));
        if (parts.length >= 2) {
            properties.setHost(parts[1]);
        }
        if (parts.length >= 3) {
            properties.setPort(Integer.parseInt(parts[2]));
        }
        if (parts.length >= 4) {
            Map<String, String> args = parseKeyValueArgs(parts[3]);
            applyArgs(properties, args);
        }

        return properties;
    }

    private static Map<String, String> parseKeyValueArgs(String argsStr) {
        return java.util.Arrays.stream(argsStr.split("&"))
                .map(s -> s.split("="))
                .filter(a -> a.length == 2)
                .collect(java.util.stream.Collectors.toMap(
                        a -> a[0].trim(),
                        a -> a[1].trim()
                ));
    }

    private static void applyArgs(QueueProperties properties, Map<String, String> args) {
        if (args.containsKey("group")) {
            properties.setStreamGroup(args.get("group"));
        }
        if (args.containsKey("consumer")) {
            properties.setStreamConsumer(args.get("consumer"));
        }
        if (args.containsKey("retryMax")) {
            properties.setStreamRetryMax(Integer.parseInt(args.get("retryMax")));
        }
        if (args.containsKey("blockMillis")) {
            properties.setStreamBlockMillis(Long.parseLong(args.get("blockMillis")));
        }
        if (args.containsKey("batchSize")) {
            properties.setStreamBatchSize(Integer.parseInt(args.get("batchSize")));
        }
        if (args.containsKey("password")) {
            properties.setPassword(args.get("password"));
        }
        if (args.containsKey("timeout")) {
            properties.setTimeout(Integer.parseInt(args.get("timeout")));
        }
    }

    /**
     * 鏋勫缓娑堟伅闃熷垪宸ュ巶瀹炰緥锛堝鐢?remi-comm-redis 杩炴帴锛屾帹鑽愶級
     *
     * @param redisService     Redis 鏈嶅姟瀹炰緥
     * @param consumerExecutor 寮傛娑堣垂鑰呯嚎绋嬫睜
     * @return 娑堟伅闃熷垪宸ュ巶
     */
    public IMessageQueueProvider buildFactory(com.remisoft.comm.redis.service.RedisService redisService,
                                              java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService consumerExecutor) {
        log.info("鏋勫缓娑堟伅闃熷垪宸ュ巶锛堝鐢?remi-comm-redis 杩炴帴锛?);
        return new MessageQueueFactory(this, redisService, consumerExecutor);
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        String typeStr = type != null ? type.getValue() : "null";
        return "QueueProperties{type=" + typeStr +
                ", host='" + resolvedHost() + '\'' +
                ", port=" + resolvedPort() +
                ", timeout=" + resolvedTimeout() +
                (username != null ? ", username='" + username + '\'' : "") +
                (password != null ? ", password=***" : "") +
                "}";
    }

    /**
     * 鎵撳嵃澶氶槦鍒楅厤缃皟璇曚俊鎭?
     *
     * @param multiTypes 闃熷垪绫诲瀷鍒楄〃
     * @param multiConfigs 闃熷垪閰嶇疆鏄犲皠
     */
    public void printDebugInfo(List<QueueType> multiTypes,
                               Map<String, QueueProperties> multiConfigs) {
        log.debug("=== QueueProperties 璋冭瘯淇℃伅 ===");
        log.debug("褰撳墠瀹炰緥: {}", this);
        log.debug("澶氶槦鍒楃被鍨? {}", multiTypes);
        log.debug("澶氶槦鍒楅厤缃? {}", multiConfigs);
        if (multiConfigs != null) {
            multiConfigs.forEach((key, config) ->
                    log.debug("閰嶇疆[{}]: {}", key, config)
            );
        }
        log.debug("=== 璋冭瘯淇℃伅缁撴潫 ===");
    }

    /**
     * 浠?YAML 閰嶇疆鏋勫缓 QueueProperties 鍒楄〃
     *
     * @param yamlConfigs 閰嶇疆椤瑰垪琛?
     * @return QueueProperties 鍒楄〃
     */
    public static List<QueueProperties> fromYamlConfigs(List<?> yamlConfigs) {
        if (yamlConfigs == null || yamlConfigs.isEmpty()) {
            return List.of();
        }

        return yamlConfigs.stream()
                .map(obj -> {
                    if (obj instanceof Map) {
                        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
                        Map<String, Object> map = (Map<String, Object>) obj;
                        return fromMap(map);
                    }
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException("閰嶇疆椤瑰繀椤绘槸 Map 绫诲瀷: " + obj);
                })
                .toList();
    }

    private static QueueProperties fromMap(Map<String, Object> map) {
        QueueProperties properties = new QueueProperties();
        properties.setEnabled(true);

        Object typeObj = map.get("type");
        if (typeObj != null) {
            properties.setType(QueueType.valueOf(String.valueOf(typeObj).toUpperCase()));
        }

        Object host = map.get("host");
        if (host != null) {
            properties.setHost(String.valueOf(host));
        }

        Object port = map.get("port");
        if (port != null) {
            properties.setPort(Integer.parseInt(String.valueOf(port)));
        }

        Object group = map.get("group");
        if (group != null) {
            properties.setStreamGroup(String.valueOf(group));
        }

        Object consumer = map.get("consumer");
        if (consumer != null) {
            properties.setStreamConsumer(String.valueOf(consumer));
        }

        Object password = map.get("password");
        if (password != null) {
            properties.setPassword(String.valueOf(password));
        }

        Object timeout = map.get("timeout");
        if (timeout != null) {
            properties.setTimeout(Integer.parseInt(String.valueOf(timeout)));
        }

        Object batch = map.get("batchSize");
        if (batch != null) {
            properties.setStreamBatchSize(Integer.parseInt(String.valueOf(batch)));
        }

        Object block = map.get("blockMillis");
        if (block != null) {
            properties.setStreamBlockMillis(Long.parseLong(String.valueOf(block)));
        }

        return properties;
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\dedup\DedupCleanupScheduler.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.dedup;

import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;

/**
 * 鍘婚噸 Key 瀹氭椂娓呯悊璋冨害鍣?
 *
 * <p>瀹氭湡娓呯悊鍐呭瓨鍘婚噸鍣ㄤ腑鐨勮繃鏈熻褰曪紝閬垮厤鍐呭瓨娉勬紡銆?
 * Redis 鍘婚噸鍣ㄥ凡浣跨敤 TTL 鑷姩杩囨湡锛屾棤闇€棰濆娓呯悊銆?
 *
 * <p><b>娓呯悊绛栫暐锛?/b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>姣?10 鍒嗛挓鎵ц涓€娆℃竻鐞?/li>
 *   <li>娓呯悊 {@link MessageDeduplicator} 涓秴鏃剁殑鍘婚噸璁板綍</li>
 *   <li>璁板綍娓呯悊鍓嶅悗鐨勮褰曟暟閲忓彉鍖?/li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>娉ㄦ剰浜嬮」锛?/b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>浠呭鍐呭瓨鍘婚噸鍣ㄦ湁鏁堬紝Redis 鍘婚噸鍣ㄤ緷璧?TTL 鑷姩杩囨湡</li>
 *   <li>娓呯悊闂撮殧鍙€氳繃 spring.task.scheduling 閰嶇疆璋冩暣</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
public class DedupCleanupScheduler {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(DedupCleanupScheduler.class);

    private final MessageDeduplicator memoryDeduplicator;

    public DedupCleanupScheduler(MessageDeduplicator memoryDeduplicator) {
        this.memoryDeduplicator = memoryDeduplicator;
    }

    /**
     * 瀹氭椂娓呯悊杩囨湡鍘婚噸 Key
     * <p>姣?10 鍒嗛挓鎵ц涓€娆★紝娓呯悊鍐呭瓨涓秴鏃剁殑鍘婚噸璁板綍
     */
    @Scheduled(fixedDelay = 600_000, initialDelay = 600_000)
    public void cleanupExpiredKeys() {
        if (memoryDeduplicator == null) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            int beforeCount = memoryDeduplicator.getRecordCount();
            if (beforeCount == 0) {
                return;
            }
            memoryDeduplicator.cleanupExpired();
            int afterCount = memoryDeduplicator.getRecordCount();
            int cleaned = beforeCount - afterCount;
            if (cleaned > 0) {
                log.debug("[DedupCleanupScheduler] 宸叉竻鐞?{} 鏉¤繃鏈熷幓閲嶈褰曪紙{} -> {}锛?,
                        cleaned, beforeCount, afterCount);
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.warn("[DedupCleanupScheduler] 娓呯悊杩囨湡鍘婚噸璁板綍澶辫触: {}", e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\dedup\MessageDeduplicator.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.dedup;

import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

/**
 * 娑堟伅骞傜瓑鎬у幓閲嶅櫒
 *
 * <p>鎻愪緵娑堟伅娑堣垂鐨勫幓閲嶆満鍒讹紝闃叉娑堟伅琚噸澶嶅鐞嗐€?
 * 鍩轰簬娑堟伅 traceId 杩涜鍘婚噸鍒ゆ柇锛屼娇鐢ㄥ唴瀛樼紦瀛樺瓨鍌ㄥ凡澶勭悊鐨勬秷鎭褰曘€?
 *
 * <p><b>娉ㄦ剰锛氬綋鍓嶅疄鐜板熀浜庡唴瀛樺瓨鍌紝浠呴€傜敤浜庡崟瀹炰緥鍦烘櫙銆?/b>
 * 鍒嗗竷寮忓満鏅簲浣跨敤 {@link RedisMessageDeduplicator} 鎴栬嚜琛屽疄鐜板熀浜?Redis 鐨勫幓閲嶃€?
 *
 * <p><b>鍘婚噸绛栫暐锛?/b></p>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>璁板綍姣忎釜 traceId 鐨勫鐞嗘椂闂存埑</li>
 *   <li>瀹氭湡娓呯悊杩囨湡璁板綍锛堥粯璁?1 灏忔椂锛?/li>
 *   <li>鏀寔鑷畾涔夊幓閲嶇獥鍙ｅぇ灏?/li>
 *   <li>浣跨敤 ConcurrentHashMap 淇濊瘉绾跨▼瀹夊叏锛岄伩鍏?ReadWriteLock 璇婚攣鍗囩骇鍐欓攣姝婚攣椋庨櫓</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>浣跨敤绀轰緥锛?/b></p>
 * <pre>{@code
 * MessageDeduplicator deduplicator = new MessageDeduplicator(3600); // 1灏忔椂鍘婚噸绐楀彛
 *
 * // 鍦ㄦ秷璐规秷鎭墠妫€鏌?
 * QueueMessage message = subscriber.subscribeMessage();
 * if (deduplicator.isDuplicate(message.getTraceId())) {
 *     log.info("閲嶅娑堟伅锛岃烦杩囧鐞? {}", message.getTraceId());
 *     return;
 * }
 *
 * // 澶勭悊娑堟伅
 * processMessage(message);
 *
 * // 鏍囪涓哄凡澶勭悊
 * deduplicator.markProcessed(message.getTraceId());
 * }</pre>
 *
 * <p><b>娉ㄦ剰浜嬮」锛?/b></p>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>鍐呭瓨鍘婚噸锛屽簲鐢ㄩ噸鍚悗鍘婚噸璁板綍浼氫涪澶?/li>
 *   <li>鍒嗗竷寮忓満鏅缓璁娇鐢?{@link RedisMessageDeduplicator} 瀹炵幇鍏ㄥ眬鍘婚噸</li>
 *   <li>鍘婚噸绐楀彛搴旀牴鎹笟鍔″満鏅悎鐞嗚缃?/li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 * @see RedisMessageDeduplicator
 */
public class MessageDeduplicator {

    private final ConcurrentHashMap<String, Long> processedRecords;
    private final long expireWindowMs;
    @SuppressWarnings("unused")
    private final int maxCapacity;
    private volatile long lastCleanupTime;

    private static final int DEFAULT_MAX_CAPACITY = 100000;

    public MessageDeduplicator() {
        this(TimeUnit.HOURS.toMillis(1));
    }

    public MessageDeduplicator(long expireWindowMs) {
        this(expireWindowMs, DEFAULT_MAX_CAPACITY);
    }

    public MessageDeduplicator(long expireWindowMs, int maxCapacity) {
        if (expireWindowMs <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("鍘婚噸绐楀彛蹇呴』澶т簬 0");
        }
        if (maxCapacity <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("鏈€澶у閲忓繀椤诲ぇ浜?0");
        }
        this.expireWindowMs = expireWindowMs;
        this.maxCapacity = maxCapacity;
        this.lastCleanupTime = System.currentTimeMillis();

        this.processedRecords = new ConcurrentHashMap<>(Math.min(maxCapacity, 1024));
    }

    /**
     * 璁剧疆瀹归噺涓婇檺
     *
     * @param capacity 鏂扮殑瀹归噺涓婇檺
     */
    public synchronized void setCapacity(int capacity) {
        if (capacity <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("瀹归噺蹇呴』澶т簬 0");
        }
        // ConcurrentHashMap 涓嶆敮鎸?LRU 娣樻卑锛岃繖閲屼粎鍋氬閲忔鏌ユ彁绀?
        // 濡傞渶涓ユ牸鐨?LRU 娣樻卑锛屽缓璁娇鐢ㄨ嚜瀹氫箟瀹炵幇
        cleanupExpired();
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ユ秷鎭槸鍚︿负閲嶅娑堟伅
     *
     * @param traceId 娑堟伅 traceId
     * @return true 琛ㄧず閲嶅锛宖alse 琛ㄧず鏈鐞嗚繃
     */
    public boolean isDuplicate(String traceId) {
        if (traceId == null || traceId.isEmpty()) {
            return false;
        }

        cleanupIfNecessary();

        Long processTime = processedRecords.get(traceId);
        if (processTime == null) {
            return false;
        }

        if (System.currentTimeMillis() - processTime > expireWindowMs) {
            processedRecords.remove(traceId);
            return false;
        }

        return true;
    }

    /**
     * 鏍囪娑堟伅涓哄凡澶勭悊
     *
     * @param traceId 娑堟伅 traceId
     */
    public void markProcessed(String traceId) {
        if (traceId == null || traceId.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }

        cleanupIfNecessary();

        processedRecords.put(traceId, System.currentTimeMillis());
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ュ苟鏍囪锛堝師瀛愭搷浣滐級
     *
     * <p>濡傛灉娑堟伅鏈鐞嗚繃锛屽垯鏍囪涓哄凡澶勭悊骞惰繑鍥?false銆?
     * 濡傛灉娑堟伅宸插鐞嗚繃锛屽垯杩斿洖 true锛堣〃绀洪噸澶嶏級銆?
     *
     * @param traceId 娑堟伅 traceId
     * @return true 琛ㄧず閲嶅锛宖alse 琛ㄧず棣栨澶勭悊
     */
    public boolean checkAndMark(String traceId) {
        if (traceId == null || traceId.isEmpty()) {
            return false;
        }

        cleanupIfNecessary();

        Long existing = processedRecords.putIfAbsent(traceId, System.currentTimeMillis());
        if (existing == null) {
            return false;
        }

        return System.currentTimeMillis() - existing <= expireWindowMs;
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇宸插幓閲嶈褰曟暟閲?
     *
     * @return 璁板綍鏁伴噺
     */
    public int getRecordCount() {
        cleanupIfNecessary();
        return processedRecords.size();
    }

    /**
     * 娓呯┖鎵€鏈夊幓閲嶈褰?
     */
    public void clear() {
        processedRecords.clear();
        lastCleanupTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
    }

    /**
     * 娓呯悊杩囨湡璁板綍
     */
    public void cleanupExpired() {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        processedRecords.entrySet().removeIf(
                entry -> now - entry.getValue() > expireWindowMs
        );
        lastCleanupTime = now;
    }

    private void cleanupIfNecessary() {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (now - lastCleanupTime > TimeUnit.MINUTES.toMillis(5)) {
            synchronized (this) {
                if (now - lastCleanupTime > TimeUnit.MINUTES.toMillis(5)) {
                    cleanupExpired();
                }
            }
        }
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\dedup\RedisMessageDeduplicator.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.dedup;

import com.remisoft.comm.redis.service.ops.RedisStringOps;

/**
 * 鍩轰簬 Redis 鐨勬秷鎭幓閲嶅櫒
 * <p>閫傜敤浜庡垎甯冨紡鍦烘櫙锛屽彲璺ㄥ疄渚嬪幓閲嶃€?
 * <p>浣跨敤 Redis 鐨?SETNX 鍛戒护瀹炵幇鍘熷瓙鎬у幓閲嶅垽鏂紝閰嶅悎 TTL 鑷姩杩囨湡娓呯悊銆?
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 * @see MessageDeduplicator
 */
public class RedisMessageDeduplicator {

    private static final String KEY_PREFIX = "queue:dedup:";

    private final RedisStringOps redisStringOps;
    private final long ttlSeconds;

    /**
     * 鏋勯€犲嚱鏁?
     *
     * @param redisStringOps Redis String 鎿嶄綔缁勪欢
     * @param ttlMillis      鍘婚噸绐楀彛鏃堕棿锛堟绉掞級
     */
    public RedisMessageDeduplicator(RedisStringOps redisStringOps, long ttlMillis) {
        if (redisStringOps == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("redisStringOps 涓嶈兘涓?null");
        }
        if (ttlMillis <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("鍘婚噸绐楀彛蹇呴』澶т簬 0");
        }
        this.redisStringOps = redisStringOps;
        this.ttlSeconds = Math.max(1, ttlMillis / 1000);
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ユ秷鎭槸鍚︿负閲嶅娑堟伅
     *
     * @param messageId 娑堟伅 ID
     * @return true 琛ㄧず閲嶅锛宖alse 琛ㄧず鏈鐞嗚繃
     */
    public boolean isDuplicate(String messageId) {
        if (messageId == null || messageId.isEmpty()) {
            return false;
        }
        String key = KEY_PREFIX + messageId;
        Boolean success = redisStringOps.setIfAbsent(key, "1", ttlSeconds);
        return !Boolean.TRUE.equals(success);
    }
}

/**

 *
 * <p>灏佽娑堟伅闃熷垪涓殑娑堟伅鍐呭锛屽寘鍚秷鎭綋銆佸ご閮ㄤ俊鎭€佽拷韪狪D銆侀噸璇曟鏁扮瓑鏍囧噯瀛楁銆?

public class QueueMessage implements Serializable {

    private Integer retryCount;

    private String timestamp;

    private Long expireMillis;

    private Long sequenceNumber;

    /**

    private String messageGroupKey;

    /**

        message.setTraceId(generateTraceId());
        message.setRetryCount(0);
        message.setTimestamp(formatNow());
        message.setPriority(Integer.MAX_VALUE);
        message.setExpireMillis(0L);
        return message;
    }

     * @param priority 浼樺厛绾э紙鏁板€艰秺灏忎紭鍏堢骇瓒婇珮锛?

     * @param expireTime  杩囨湡鏃堕棿鏁板€?
     * @param timeUnit    鏃堕棿鍗曚綅
     * @return 鏋勫缓濂界殑 QueueMessage 瀹炰緥

    public static QueueMessage of(String body, long expireTime, TimeUnit timeUnit) {

     * @param headers    娑堟伅澶翠俊鎭?
     * @param traceId    杩借釜ID
     * @param retryCount 閲嶈瘯娆℃暟
     * @return 鏋勫缓濂界殑 QueueMessage 瀹炰緥
     */
    public static QueueMessage of(String body, Map<String, String> headers, String traceId, Integer retryCount) {

        message.setHeaders(headers == null ? new HashMap<>(4) : new HashMap<>(headers));
        message.setTraceId(StringUtils.isNotBlank(traceId) ? traceId : generateTraceId());
        message.setRetryCount(retryCount == null ? 0 : retryCount);
        message.setTimestamp(formatNow());
        message.setPriority(Integer.MAX_VALUE);
        message.setExpireMillis(0L);
        return message;
    }

     * @return JSON 瀛楃涓诧紝濡傛灉杈撳叆涓?null 鍒欒繑鍥?null
     */
    public static String toPayload(QueueMessage message) {
        if (message == null) {
            return null;
        }
        if (message.getHeaders() == null) {
            message.setHeaders(new HashMap<>(4));
        }
        if (message.getRetryCount() == null) {
            message.setRetryCount(0);
        }
        if (StringUtils.isBlank(message.getTraceId())) {
            message.setTraceId(generateTraceId());
        }
        if (StringUtils.isBlank(message.getTimestamp())) {
            message.setTimestamp(formatNow());
        }
        if (message.getPriority() == null) {
            message.setPriority(Integer.MAX_VALUE);
        }
        if (message.getExpireMillis() == null) {
            message.setExpireMillis(0L);
        }
        return JsonUtils.toJson(message);
    }

    /**
     * 灏?JSON 瀛楃涓插弽搴忓垪鍖栦负娑堟伅瀵硅薄
     *
     * <p>濡傛灉搴忓垪鍖栧け璐ユ垨瑙ｆ瀽寮傚父锛屼細灏濊瘯灏嗗師濮嬪瓧绗︿覆浣滀负娑堟伅浣撳垱寤烘柊娑堟伅銆?
     * 杩欑‘淇濅簡鍗充娇鏍煎紡涓嶆爣鍑嗙殑娑堟伅涔熻兘琚鐞嗐€?
     *
     * @param payload JSON 瀛楃涓?
     * @return 娑堟伅瀵硅薄锛屽鏋滆緭鍏ヤ负绌哄垯杩斿洖 null
     */
    public static QueueMessage fromPayload(String payload) {
        if (StringUtils.isBlank(payload)) {
            return null;
        }
        try {
            QueueMessage message = JsonUtils.fromJson(payload, QueueMessage.class);
            if (message == null) {
                return QueueMessage.of(payload);
            }
            if (message.getHeaders() == null) {
                message.setHeaders(new HashMap<>(4));
            }
            if (message.getRetryCount() == null) {
                message.setRetryCount(0);
            }
            if (StringUtils.isBlank(message.getTraceId())) {
                message.setTraceId(generateTraceId());
            }
            if (StringUtils.isBlank(message.getTimestamp())) {
                message.setTimestamp(formatNow());
            }
            if (message.getPriority() == null) {
                message.setPriority(Integer.MAX_VALUE);
            }
            if (message.getExpireMillis() == null) {
                message.setExpireMillis(0L);
            }
            return message;
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            return QueueMessage.of(payload);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 娣诲姞娑堟伅澶?
     *
     * @param key   澶翠俊鎭殑閿?
     * @param value 澶翠俊鎭殑鍊?
     * @return 褰撳墠娑堟伅瀹炰緥锛屾敮鎸侀摼寮忚皟鐢?
     */
    public QueueMessage addHeader(String key, String value) {
        if (this.headers == null) {
            this.headers = new HashMap<>(4);
        }
        this.headers.put(key, value);
        return this;
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇娑堟伅澶?
     *
     * @param key 澶翠俊鎭殑閿?
     * @return 澶翠俊鎭殑鍊硷紝濡傛灉涓嶅瓨鍦ㄨ繑鍥?null
     */
    public String getHeader(String key) {
        if (this.headers == null) {
            return null;
        }
        return this.headers.get(key);
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ユ秷鎭槸鍚﹀凡杩囨湡
     *
     * @return true 濡傛灉宸茶繃鏈燂紝false 濡傛灉鏈繃鏈熸垨娌℃湁璁剧疆杩囨湡鏃堕棿
     */
    public boolean isExpired() {
        if (this.expireMillis == null || this.expireMillis <= 0) {
            return false;
        }
        if (StringUtils.isBlank(this.timestamp)) {
            return false;
        }
        try {
            LocalDateTime createTime = LocalDateTime.parse(this.timestamp, TIMESTAMP_FORMATTER);
            LocalDateTime expireTime = createTime.plusNanos(this.expireMillis * 1_000_000);
            return LocalDateTime.now().isAfter(expireTime);
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ユ秷鎭槸鍚︿负楂樹紭鍏堢骇
     *
     * @param threshold 浼樺厛绾ч槇鍊硷紝榛樿 Integer.MAX_VALUE
     * @return true 濡傛灉浼樺厛绾ч珮浜庨槇鍊?
     */
    public boolean isHighPriority(int threshold) {
        return this.priority != null && this.priority < threshold;
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ユ秷鎭槸鍚︿负楂樹紭鍏堢骇锛堜娇鐢ㄩ粯璁ら槇鍊硷級
     *
     * @return true 濡傛灉浼樺厛绾ч珮浜?1000
     */
    public boolean isHighPriority() {
        return isHighPriority(1000);
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ユ秷鎭槸鍚︿负椤哄簭娑堟伅
     *
     * @return true 濡傛灉璁剧疆浜嗘秷鎭垎缁勯敭
     */
    public boolean isSequential() {
        return messageGroupKey != null && !messageGroupKey.isEmpty();
    }

    /**
     * 璁剧疆椤哄簭娑堟伅灞炴€?
     *
     * @param messageGroupKey 娑堟伅鍒嗙粍閿紝鐩稿悓鍒嗙粍鐨勬秷鎭繚璇侀『搴?
     * @param sequenceNumber  娑堟伅搴忓彿锛岀敤浜庣粍鍐呮帓搴?
     * @return 褰撳墠娑堟伅瀹炰緥锛屾敮鎸侀摼寮忚皟鐢?
     */
    public QueueMessage setSequential(String messageGroupKey, Long sequenceNumber) {
        this.messageGroupKey = messageGroupKey;
        this.sequenceNumber = sequenceNumber;
        return this;
    }

    /**
     * 澧炲姞閲嶈瘯娆℃暟
     *
     * @return 褰撳墠閲嶈瘯娆℃暟
     */
    public int incrementRetryCount() {
        if (this.retryCount == null) {
            this.retryCount = 0;
        }
        return ++this.retryCount;
    }

    /**
     * 閲嶇疆娑堟伅鐘舵€?
     * <p>娓呴櫎閲嶈瘯璁℃暟锛屾洿鏂版椂闂存埑鍜岃拷韪狪D
     *
     * @return 褰撳墠娑堟伅瀹炰緥锛屾敮鎸侀摼寮忚皟鐢?
     */
    public QueueMessage reset() {
        this.retryCount = 0;
        this.traceId = generateTraceId();
        this.timestamp = formatNow();
        return this;
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇娑堟伅鎽樿淇℃伅
     *
     * <p>鐢ㄤ簬鏃ュ織璁板綍鍜岄棶棰樻帓鏌?
     *
     * @return 鏍煎紡鍖栫殑鎽樿瀛楃涓?
     */
    public String getSummary() {
        return String.format("[traceId=%s, retry=%d, priority=%d, bodyLen=%d]",
                this.traceId,
                this.retryCount,
                this.priority,
                this.body == null ? 0 : this.body.length());
    }

    /**
     * 鐢熸垚鏂扮殑杩借釜ID
     *
     * @return 鏍煎紡鍖栫殑杩借釜ID瀛楃涓?
     */
    private static String generateTraceId() {
        return TracerUtils.generateTraceId();
    }

    /**
     * 鏍煎紡鍖栧綋鍓嶆椂闂?
     *
     * @return 鏍煎紡鍖栫殑鏃堕棿瀛楃涓?
     */
    private static String formatNow() {
        return LocalDateTime.now().format(TIMESTAMP_FORMATTER);
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return "QueueMessage{" +
                "body='" + (body != null && body.length() > 100 ? body.substring(0, 100) + "..." : body) + '\'' +
                ", headers=" + (headers != null ? headers.size() + " entries" : "null") +
                ", traceId='" + traceId + '\'' +
                ", retryCount=" + retryCount +
                ", timestamp='" + timestamp + '\'' +
                ", priority=" + priority +
                ", expireMillis=" + expireMillis +
                '}';
    }
}

package com.remisoft.comm.queue.enums;

/**
 * 娑堟伅闃熷垪绫诲瀷鏋氫妇
 *
 * <p>瀹氫箟绯荤粺鏀寔鐨勬秷鎭槦鍒楀疄鐜扮被鍨嬶紝鍖呮嫭 Redis 绯诲垪锛圠ist銆丼tream銆丳ubSub锛?
 * 鍜屼富娴佷腑闂翠欢锛圓ctiveMQ銆丷abbitMQ銆丷ocketMQ銆並afka锛夈€?
 *
 * <p><b>绫诲瀷璇存槑锛?/b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>{@link #LIST}锛歊edis List锛岃交閲忕骇闃熷垪锛屾敮鎸?FIFO</li>
 *   <li>{@link #STREAM}锛歊edis Stream锛屾敮鎸佹秷璐圭粍銆佹秷鎭‘璁ょ瓑楂樼骇鐗规€?/li>
 *   <li>{@link #PUBSUB}锛歊edis PubSub锛屾敮鎸佸彂甯?璁㈤槄妯″紡</li>
 *   <li>{@link #ACTIVE}锛欰ctiveMQ锛岄潰鍚戞秷鎭殑涓棿浠?/li>
 *   <li>{@link #RABBIT}锛歊abbitMQ锛孉MQP 鍗忚瀹炵幇</li>
 *   <li>{@link #ROCKET}锛歊ocketMQ锛岄樋閲屽反宸村紑婧愮殑鍒嗗竷寮忔秷鎭腑闂翠欢</li>
 *   <li>{@link #KAFKA}锛欿afka锛岄珮鍚炲悙閲忓垎甯冨紡娑堟伅绯荤粺</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>閫夊瀷寤鸿锛?/b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>绠€鍗曢槦鍒楀満鏅細Redis List</li>
 *   <li>闇€瑕佹秷鎭‘璁わ細Redis Stream 鎴?RabbitMQ</li>
 *   <li>楂樺悶鍚愰噺鍦烘櫙锛欿afka 鎴?RocketMQ</li>
 *   <li>浜嬪姟娑堟伅锛歊ocketMQ</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
public enum QueueType {

    /**
     * Redis List 闃熷垪
     * <p>杞婚噺绾ч槦鍒楋紝鍩轰簬 Redis 鐨?LPUSH/BRPOP 鍛戒护瀹炵幇 FIFO
     */
    LIST("list"),

    /**
     * Redis Stream 闃熷垪
     * <p>鏀寔娑堣垂缁勩€佹秷鎭‘璁ゃ€佹寔涔呭寲绛夐珮绾х壒鎬э紝鏄?List 鐨勫崌绾х増
     */
    STREAM("stream"),

    /**
     * Redis PubSub 鍙戝竷/璁㈤槄
     * <p>鏀寔澶氳闃呰€呮ā寮忥紝浣嗘秷鎭笉鎸佷箙鍖栵紝閫傚悎瀹炴椂閫氱煡鍦烘櫙
     */
    PUBSUB("pubsub"),

    /**
     * ActiveMQ
     * <p>Apache 鏃椾笅鐨勯潰鍚戞秷鎭殑涓棿浠讹紝鏀寔澶氱鍗忚
     */
    ACTIVE("active"),

    /**
     * RabbitMQ
     * <p>瀹炵幇浜?AMQP 鍗忚鐨勬秷鎭槦鍒楋紝鏀寔涓板瘜鐨勮矾鐢卞姛鑳?
     */
    RABBIT("rabbit"),

    /**
     * RocketMQ
     * <p>闃块噷宸村反寮€婧愮殑鍒嗗竷寮忔秷鎭腑闂翠欢锛屾敮鎸佷簨鍔℃秷鎭拰椤哄簭娑堟伅
     */
    ROCKET("rocket"),

    /**
     * Kafka
     * <p>楂樺悶鍚愰噺鍒嗗竷寮忔秷鎭郴缁燂紝閫傚悎鏃ュ織鏀堕泦鍜屽疄鏃舵祦澶勭悊
     */
    KAFKA("kafka");

    private final String value;

    QueueType(String value) {
        this.value = value;
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇闃熷垪绫诲瀷鐨勫瓧绗︿覆琛ㄧず
     *
     * @return 闃熷垪绫诲瀷鍊?
     */
    public String getValue() {
        return value;
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return value;
    }

    /**
     * 鏍规嵁瀛楃涓插€煎弽搴忓垪鍖栦负鏋氫妇
     *
     * @param value 闃熷垪绫诲瀷瀛楃涓?
     * @return 瀵瑰簲鐨勬灇涓惧€?
     */
    public static QueueType fromValue(String value) {
        if (value == null) {
            return null;
        }
        for (QueueType type : values()) {
            if (type.value.equalsIgnoreCase(value)) {
                return type;
            }
        }
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("鏈煡鐨勯槦鍒楃被鍨? " + value);
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\health\QueueHealthIndicator.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.health;

import com.remisoft.comm.queue.config.QueueProperties;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.enums.QueueType;
import com.remisoft.comm.redis.service.RedisService;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.boot.actuate.health.Health;
import org.springframework.boot.actuate.health.HealthIndicator;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnClass;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnProperty;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;

import java.net.InetSocketAddress;
import java.net.Socket;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 娑堟伅闃熷垪鍋ュ悍妫€鏌?
 *
 * <p>鏍规嵁瀹為檯闃熷垪绫诲瀷锛圞afka/RabbitMQ/RocketMQ/Redis锛夋鏌ュ搴斾腑闂翠欢杩為€氭€с€?
 * Redis 绫诲瀷澶嶇敤 remi-comm-redis 杩炴帴杩涜妫€鏌ワ紝闈?Redis 绫诲瀷閫氳繃 TCP 绔彛杩為€氭€ф鏌ャ€?
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
@Configuration
@ConditionalOnClass(name = "org.springframework.boot.actuate.health.HealthIndicator")
@ConditionalOnProperty(prefix = "remi.queue", name = "enabled", havingValue = "true", matchIfMissing = false)
public class QueueHealthIndicator implements HealthIndicator {

    private static final int HEALTH_CHECK_TIMEOUT_MS = 2000;

    private final RedisService redisService;
    private final QueueProperties queueProperties;

    public QueueHealthIndicator(QueueProperties queueProperties,
                                org.springframework.beans.factory.ObjectProvider<RedisService> redisServiceProvider) {
        this.queueProperties = queueProperties;
        this.redisService = redisServiceProvider.getIfAvailable();
    }

    @Override
    public Health health() {
        QueueType type = resolveQueueType();
        if (type == null) {
            return Health.unknown()
                    .withDetail("error", "闃熷垪绫诲瀷鏈厤缃?)
                    .build();
        }

        try {
            Health.Builder builder;
            if (isRedisType(type)) {
                builder = checkRedisHealth();
            } else {
                builder = checkMqConnectivity(type);
            }
            return builder.build();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("娑堟伅闃熷垪鍋ュ悍妫€鏌ュけ璐? type={}", type, e);
            return Health.down()
                    .withDetail("mqType", type.getValue())
                    .withDetail("host", queueProperties.resolvedHost())
                    .withDetail("port", resolvePort(type))
                    .withDetail("error", e.getMessage())
                    .build();
        }
    }

    /**
     * 瑙ｆ瀽闃熷垪绫诲瀷
     */
    private QueueType resolveQueueType() {
        try {
            return queueProperties.resolvedType();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌?Redis 闃熷垪鍋ュ悍鐘舵€?
     */
    private Health.Builder checkRedisHealth() {
        if (redisService == null) {
            return Health.unknown()
                    .withDetail("mqType", "redis")
                    .withDetail("detail", "RedisService 鏈彁渚涳紝鏃犳硶鎵ц鍋ュ悍妫€鏌?);
        }

        long startTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        redisService.hasKey("__health_check__");
        long responseTime = System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime;

        return Health.up()
                .withDetail("mqType", "redis")
                .withDetail("host", queueProperties.resolvedHost())
                .withDetail("port", queueProperties.resolvedPort())
                .withDetail("responseTimeMs", responseTime);
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ラ潪 Redis 涓棿浠剁殑 TCP 杩為€氭€?
     */
    private Health.Builder checkMqConnectivity(QueueType type) {
        String host = queueProperties.resolvedHost();
        int port = resolvePort(type);

        long startTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        boolean connected = checkTcpConnection(host, port);
        long responseTime = System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime;

        Map<String, Object> details = new HashMap<>();
        details.put("mqType", type.getValue());
        details.put("host", host);
        details.put("port", port);
        details.put("responseTimeMs", responseTime);
        details.put("connected", connected);

        if (connected) {
            return Health.up().withDetails(details);
        } else {
            return Health.down()
                    .withDetails(details)
                    .withDetail("error", "鏃犳硶杩炴帴鍒?" + type.getValue() + " 鏈嶅姟 " + host + ":" + port);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 鏍规嵁闃熷垪绫诲瀷瑙ｆ瀽瀵瑰簲鐨勭鍙?
     */
    private int resolvePort(QueueType type) {
        if (type == QueueType.KAFKA) {
            return queueProperties.resolvedPort() != 6379 ? queueProperties.resolvedPort() : 9092;
        } else if (type == QueueType.RABBIT) {
            return queueProperties.resolvedPort() != 6379 ? queueProperties.resolvedPort() : 5672;
        } else if (type == QueueType.ROCKET) {
            return queueProperties.resolvedPort() != 6379 ? queueProperties.resolvedPort() : 9876;
        } else if (type == QueueType.ACTIVE) {
            return queueProperties.resolvedPort() != 6379 ? queueProperties.resolvedPort() : 61616;
        }
        return queueProperties.resolvedPort();
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌?TCP 绔彛杩為€氭€?
     */
    private boolean checkTcpConnection(String host, int port) {
        try (Socket socket = new Socket()) {
            socket.connect(new InetSocketAddress(host, port), HEALTH_CHECK_TIMEOUT_MS);
            return true;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.debug("TCP 杩為€氭€ф鏌ュけ璐? host={}, port={}", host, port, e);
            return false;
        }
    }

    private boolean isRedisType(QueueType type) {
        return type == QueueType.LIST || type == QueueType.PUBSUB || type == QueueType.STREAM;
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\manager\QueueManager.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.manager;

import com.remisoft.comm.queue.metrics.MessageMetrics;

import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * 娑堟伅闃熷垪绠＄悊鍣?
 *
 * <p>鎻愪緵缁熶竴鐨勯槦鍒楃敓鍛藉懆鏈熺鐞嗗拰鐩戞帶鎸囨爣鏀堕泦銆?
 * 璐熻矗娉ㄥ唽銆佹煡璇€佺Щ闄ら槦鍒楀疄渚嬪強鍏跺搴旂殑鐩戞帶鎸囨爣銆?
 *
 * <p><b>鍔熻兘锛?/b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>闃熷垪瀹炰緥娉ㄥ唽涓庣鐞?/li>
 *   <li>鐩戞帶鎸囨爣鏀堕泦涓庢煡璇?/li>
 *   <li>鍏ㄥ眬鍋ュ悍妫€鏌?/li>
 *   <li>浼橀泤鍋滄満鏀寔</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>浣跨敤绀轰緥锛?/b>
 * <pre>{@code
 * QueueManager manager = new QueueManager();
 *
 * // 娉ㄥ唽闃熷垪
 * manager.register("order-queue", "redis", queue, metrics);
 *
 * // 鏌ヨ鐩戞帶
 * MessageMetrics metrics = manager.getMetrics("order-queue");
 * log.info("Metrics: {}", metrics.getSummary());
 *
 * // 浼橀泤鍋滄満
 * manager.shutdown();
 * }</pre>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
public class QueueManager {

    private final Map<String, QueueEntry> queueRegistry = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<String, MessageMetrics> metricsRegistry = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    /**
     * 娉ㄥ唽闃熷垪鍙婂叾鐩戞帶鎸囨爣
     *
     * @param queueName 闃熷垪鍚嶇О
     * @param queueType 闃熷垪绫诲瀷
     * @param queue 闃熷垪瀹炰緥锛堝彲涓?null锛屼粎鐢ㄤ簬绠＄悊鐢ㄩ€旓級
     * @param metrics 鐩戞帶鎸囨爣瀹炰緥
     */
    public void register(String queueName, String queueType, AutoCloseable queue, MessageMetrics metrics) {
        if (queueName == null || queueName.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("闃熷垪鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        if (metrics == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("鐩戞帶鎸囨爣涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        QueueEntry entry = new QueueEntry(queueName, queueType, queue);
        queueRegistry.put(queueName, entry);
        metricsRegistry.put(queueName, metrics);
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇闃熷垪鐩戞帶鎸囨爣
     *
     * @param queueName 闃熷垪鍚嶇О
     * @return 鐩戞帶鎸囨爣锛屾湭娉ㄥ唽鏃惰繑鍥?null
     */
    public MessageMetrics getMetrics(String queueName) {
        return metricsRegistry.get(queueName);
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇鎵€鏈夐槦鍒楀悕绉?
     *
     * @return 闃熷垪鍚嶇О闆嗗悎锛堜笉鍙彉锛?
     */
    public Collection<String> getAllQueueNames() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableCollection(queueRegistry.keySet());
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇鎵€鏈夌洃鎺ф寚鏍?
     *
     * @return 鐩戞帶鎸囨爣闆嗗悎锛堜笉鍙彉锛?
     */
    public Collection<MessageMetrics> getAllMetrics() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableCollection(metricsRegistry.values());
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇闃熷垪瀹炰緥
     *
     * @param queueName 闃熷垪鍚嶇О
     * @return 闃熷垪瀹炰緥锛屾湭娉ㄥ唽鏃惰繑鍥?null
     */
    public AutoCloseable getQueue(String queueName) {
        QueueEntry entry = queueRegistry.get(queueName);
        return entry != null ? entry.getQueue() : null;
    }

    /**
     * 绉婚櫎闃熷垪娉ㄥ唽
     *
     * @param queueName 闃熷垪鍚嶇О
     * @return 鏄惁鎴愬姛绉婚櫎
     */
    public boolean unregister(String queueName) {
        queueRegistry.remove(queueName);
        metricsRegistry.remove(queueName);
        return true;
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ラ槦鍒楁槸鍚﹀凡娉ㄥ唽
     *
     * @param queueName 闃熷垪鍚嶇О
     * @return 鏄惁宸叉敞鍐?
     */
    public boolean isRegistered(String queueName) {
        return queueRegistry.containsKey(queueName);
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇宸叉敞鍐岄槦鍒楁暟閲?
     *
     * @return 闃熷垪鏁伴噺
     */
    public int getQueueCount() {
        return queueRegistry.size();
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇鍏ㄥ眬鎸囨爣鎽樿
     *
     * @return 鍏ㄥ眬鎽樿瀛楃涓?
     */
    public String getGlobalSummary() {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        sb.append("=== QueueManager Global Summary ===\n");
        sb.append("Total queues: ").append(queueRegistry.size()).append("\n\n");

        for (Map.Entry<String, MessageMetrics> entry : metricsRegistry.entrySet()) {
            sb.append(entry.getValue().getSummary()).append("\n");
        }

        return sb.toString();
    }

    /**
     * 浼橀泤鍋滄満鎵€鏈夐槦鍒?
     *
     * <p>渚濇璋冪敤姣忎釜闃熷垪鐨?close() 鏂规硶銆?
     * 鎸夋敞鍐岄『搴忓叧闂紝纭繚璧勬簮姝ｇ‘閲婃斁銆?
     */
    public void shutdown() {
        for (QueueEntry entry : queueRegistry.values()) {
            try {
                AutoCloseable queue = entry.getQueue();
                if (queue != null) {
                    invokeClose(queue);
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                // 蹇界暐鍏抽棴寮傚父锛岀户缁叧闂笅涓€涓?
            }
        }
        queueRegistry.clear();
        metricsRegistry.clear();
    }

    /**
     * 闃熷垪娉ㄥ唽琛ㄦ潯鐩?
     */
    private static class QueueEntry {
        @SuppressWarnings("unused")
        private final String queueName;
        @SuppressWarnings("unused")
        private final String queueType;
        private final AutoCloseable queue;

        QueueEntry(String queueName, String queueType, AutoCloseable queue) {
            this.queueName = queueName;
            this.queueType = queueType;
            this.queue = queue;
        }

        public AutoCloseable getQueue() {
            return queue;
        }
    }

    private void invokeClose(AutoCloseable closeable) {
        try {
            closeable.close();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            // 鍏抽棴寮傚父蹇界暐锛岀户缁叧闂笅涓€涓?
        }
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\metrics\MessageMetrics.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.metrics;

import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.LongAdder;

/**
 * 娑堟伅闃熷垪鐩戞帶鎸囨爣
 *
 * <p>鎻愪緵娑堟伅鍙戝竷鍜屾秷璐圭殑瀹炴椂鐩戞帶鎸囨爣锛屽寘鎷細
 * <ul>
 *   <li>鍙戝竷 QPS / 娑堣垂 QPS</li>
 *   <li>鍙戝竷寤惰繜 / 娑堣垂寤惰繜</li>
 *   <li>娑堟伅绉帇閲?/li>
 *   <li>鎴愬姛/澶辫触娆℃暟</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>浣跨敤绀轰緥锛?/b>
 * <pre>{@code
 * MessageMetrics metrics = new MessageMetrics("my-queue");
 *
 * // 鍦ㄥ彂甯冩椂璁板綍
 * metrics.recordPublish(success, latencyMs);
 *
 * // 鍦ㄦ秷璐规椂璁板綍
 * metrics.recordConsume(success, latencyMs);
 *
 * // 鑾峰彇鐩戞帶鏁版嵁
 * log.info("Publish QPS: {}", metrics.getAvgPublishQps());
 * log.info("Consume QPS: {}", metrics.getAvgConsumeQps());
 * }</pre>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
public class MessageMetrics {

    private final String queueName;
    private final String queueType;

    // 鍙戝竷鎸囨爣
    private final LongAdder publishSuccessCount = new LongAdder();
    private final LongAdder publishFailCount = new LongAdder();
    private final AtomicLong publishLatencySum = new AtomicLong(0);
    private final AtomicLong publishMaxLatency = new AtomicLong(0);

    // 娑堣垂鎸囨爣
    private final LongAdder consumeSuccessCount = new LongAdder();
    private final LongAdder consumeFailCount = new LongAdder();
    private final AtomicLong consumeLatencySum = new AtomicLong(0);
    private final AtomicLong consumeMaxLatency = new AtomicLong(0);

    // 绉帇閲?
    private volatile long backlogCount = 0;

    // 鍚姩鏃堕棿
    private final long startTime = System.currentTimeMillis();

    /**
     * 鏋勯€犲嚱鏁?
     *
     * @param queueName 闃熷垪鍚嶇О
     * @param queueType 闃熷垪绫诲瀷锛堝 redis, kafka 绛夛級
     */
    public MessageMetrics(String queueName, String queueType) {
        this.queueName = queueName;
        this.queueType = queueType;
    }

    /**
     * 璁板綍涓€娆℃秷鎭彂甯?
     *
     * @param success 鏄惁鍙戝竷鎴愬姛
     * @param latencyMs 鍙戝竷寤惰繜锛堟绉掞級
     */
    public void recordPublish(boolean success, long latencyMs) {
        if (success) {
            publishSuccessCount.increment();
        } else {
            publishFailCount.increment();
        }
        publishLatencySum.addAndGet(latencyMs);
        updateMaxLatency(publishMaxLatency, latencyMs);
    }

    /**
     * 璁板綍涓€娆℃秷鎭秷璐?
     *
     * @param success 鏄惁娑堣垂鎴愬姛
     * @param latencyMs 娑堣垂寤惰繜锛堟绉掞級
     */
    public void recordConsume(boolean success, long latencyMs) {
        if (success) {
            consumeSuccessCount.increment();
        } else {
            consumeFailCount.increment();
        }
        consumeLatencySum.addAndGet(latencyMs);
        updateMaxLatency(consumeMaxLatency, latencyMs);
    }

    /**
     * 鏇存柊娑堟伅绉帇閲?
     *
     * @param count 褰撳墠绉帇鏁伴噺
     */
    public void updateBacklog(long count) {
        this.backlogCount = count;
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇鍙戝竷鎴愬姛鎬绘暟
     *
     * @return 鍙戝竷鎴愬姛鏁伴噺
     */
    public long getPublishSuccessCount() {
        return publishSuccessCount.sum();
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇鍙戝竷澶辫触鎬绘暟
     *
     * @return 鍙戝竷澶辫触鏁伴噺
     */
    public long getPublishFailCount() {
        return publishFailCount.sum();
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇娑堣垂鎴愬姛鎬绘暟
     *
     * @return 娑堣垂鎴愬姛鏁伴噺
     */
    public long getConsumeSuccessCount() {
        return consumeSuccessCount.sum();
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇娑堣垂澶辫触鎬绘暟
     *
     * @return 娑堣垂澶辫触鏁伴噺
     */
    public long getConsumeFailCount() {
        return consumeFailCount.sum();
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇骞冲潎鍙戝竷寤惰繜锛堟绉掞級
     *
     * @return 骞冲潎寤惰繜
     */
    public double getAvgPublishLatency() {
        long total = publishSuccessCount.sum() + publishFailCount.sum();
        return total == 0 ? 0.0 : (double) publishLatencySum.get() / total;
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇鏈€澶у彂甯冨欢杩燂紙姣锛?
     *
     * @return 鏈€澶у欢杩?
     */
    public long getMaxPublishLatency() {
        return publishMaxLatency.get();
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇骞冲潎娑堣垂寤惰繜锛堟绉掞級
     *
     * @return 骞冲潎寤惰繜
     */
    public double getAvgConsumeLatency() {
        long total = consumeSuccessCount.sum() + consumeFailCount.sum();
        return total == 0 ? 0.0 : (double) consumeLatencySum.get() / total;
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇鏈€澶ф秷璐瑰欢杩燂紙姣锛?
     *
     * @return 鏈€澶у欢杩?
     */
    public long getMaxConsumeLatency() {
        return consumeMaxLatency.get();
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇娑堟伅绉帇閲?
     *
     * @return 绉帇鏁伴噺
     */
    public long getBacklogCount() {
        return backlogCount;
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇骞冲潎鍙戝竷 QPS
     *
     * @return QPS
     */
    public double getAvgPublishQps() {
        long elapsedSeconds = getElapsedSeconds();
        return elapsedSeconds == 0 ? 0.0 : (double) publishSuccessCount.sum() / elapsedSeconds;
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇骞冲潎娑堣垂 QPS
     *
     * @return QPS
     */
    public double getAvgConsumeQps() {
        long elapsedSeconds = getElapsedSeconds();
        return elapsedSeconds == 0 ? 0.0 : (double) consumeSuccessCount.sum() / elapsedSeconds;
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇闃熷垪鍚嶇О
     *
     * @return 闃熷垪鍚嶇О
     */
    public String getQueueName() {
        return queueName;
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇闃熷垪绫诲瀷
     *
     * @return 闃熷垪绫诲瀷
     */
    public String getQueueType() {
        return queueType;
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇杩愯鏃堕棿锛堢锛?
     *
     * @return 杩愯绉掓暟
     */
    public long getElapsedSeconds() {
        return (System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime) / 1000;
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇瀹屾暣鐨勬寚鏍囨憳瑕?
     *
     * @return 鎸囨爣鎽樿瀛楃涓?
     */
    public String getSummary() {
        return String.format(
                "QueueMetrics[%s:%s] pub(success=%d,fail=%d,qps=%.1f,latencyAvg=%.1fms,latencyMax=%dms) " +
                        "cons(success=%d,fail=%d,qps=%.1f,latencyAvg=%.1fms,latencyMax=%dms) backlog=%d uptime=%ds",
                queueName, queueType,
                publishSuccessCount.sum(), publishFailCount.sum(), getAvgPublishQps(),
                getAvgPublishLatency(), getMaxPublishLatency(),
                consumeSuccessCount.sum(), consumeFailCount.sum(), getAvgConsumeQps(),
                getAvgConsumeLatency(), getMaxConsumeLatency(),
                backlogCount, getElapsedSeconds()
        );
    }

    /**
     * 閲嶇疆鎵€鏈夋寚鏍?
     */
    public void reset() {
        publishSuccessCount.reset();
        publishFailCount.reset();
        publishLatencySum.set(0);
        publishMaxLatency.set(0);
        consumeSuccessCount.reset();
        consumeFailCount.reset();
        consumeLatencySum.set(0);
        consumeMaxLatency.set(0);
        backlogCount = 0;
    }

    private void updateMaxLatency(AtomicLong maxLatency, long currentLatency) {
        long current;
        do {
            current = maxLatency.get();
            if (currentLatency <= current) {
                return;
            }
        } while (!maxLatency.compareAndSet(current, currentLatency));
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\mq\active\ActiveMQ.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.mq.active;

import com.remisoft.comm.exception.custom.BusinessException;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.queue.AbstractMessageQueue;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessagePublisher;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessageSubscriber;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.apache.activemq.ActiveMQConnectionFactory;

import jakarta.jms.Connection;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;

/**
 * ActiveMQ 娑堟伅闃熷垪
 *
 * <p>ActiveMQ 鏄?Apache 鏃椾笅鐨勫紑婧愭秷鎭腑闂翠欢锛屾敮鎸?JMS 瑙勮寖銆?
 * 鎻愪緵楂樺彲闈犳€с€佸绉嶆秷鎭被鍨嬬殑鏀寔銆?
 *
 * <p><b>鎶€鏈壒鐐癸細</b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>JMS 鏍囧噯锛氬畬鍏ㄩ伒寰?Java Message Service 瑙勮寖</li>
 *   <li>澶氱鍗忚锛氭敮鎸?OpenWire銆丄MQP銆丮QTT銆丼TOMP 绛夊崗璁?/li>
 *   <li>楂樺彲闈狅細鏀寔娑堟伅鎸佷箙鍖栧拰浜嬪姟</li>
 *   <li>鐏垫椿璺敱锛氭敮鎸佸绉嶇洰鐨勫湴绫诲瀷</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>涓ょ妯″紡锛?/b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>ActiveMQ Classic锛氫紶缁熺殑娑堟伅浠ｇ悊</li>
 *   <li>ActiveMQ Artemis锛氭柊涓€浠ｉ珮鎬ц兘娑堟伅浠ｇ悊锛堟帹鑽愶級</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>閫傜敤鍦烘櫙锛?/b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>浼佷笟搴旂敤闆嗘垚</li>
 *   <li>寮傛娑堟伅澶勭悊</li>
 *   <li>浜嬩欢椹卞姩鏋舵瀯</li>
 *   <li>寰湇鍔￠€氫俊</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>浣跨敤绀轰緥锛?/b>
 * <pre>{@code
 * IMessageQueue queue = messageQueueProvider.createMessageQueue(QueueType.active);
 * IMessagePublisher publisher = queue.createPublisher("my-queue");
 * IMessageSubscriber subscriber = queue.createSubscriber("my-queue");
 *
 * publisher.publish("Hello ActiveMQ");
 *
 * subscriber.subscribeAsync(message -> {
 *     log.info("Received: {}", message.getBody());
 * });
 * }</pre>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
public class ActiveMQ extends AbstractMessageQueue {

    private final ActiveMQProperties properties;
    private final ExecutorService consumerExecutor;

    public ActiveMQ(ActiveMQProperties properties, ExecutorService consumerExecutor) {
        super("ActiveMQ");
        if (properties == null) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("ActiveMQ 閰嶇疆涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        this.properties = properties;
        this.consumerExecutor = consumerExecutor;
        validateConnection();
        log.info("[ActiveMQ] 鍒濆鍖栨垚鍔燂紝brokerUrl={}", properties.resolvedBrokerUrl());
    }

    @Override
    public IMessagePublisher createPublisher(String channel) {
        checkNotClosed();
        if (channel == null || channel.isEmpty()) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("闃熷垪鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        return new ActiveMQPublisher(properties, channel);
    }

    @Override
    public IMessageSubscriber createSubscriber(String channel) {
        checkNotClosed();
        if (channel == null || channel.isEmpty()) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("闃熷垪鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        return new ActiveMQSubscriber(properties, channel, consumerExecutor);
    }

    @Override
    protected void doClose() {
    }

    private void validateConnection() {
        try {
            ActiveMQConnectionFactory factory = new ActiveMQConnectionFactory(
                    properties.resolvedUsername(),
                    properties.resolvedPassword(),
                    properties.resolvedBrokerUrl()
            );
            try (Connection conn = factory.createConnection()) {
                conn.start();
                log.debug("[ActiveMQ] 杩炴帴楠岃瘉鎴愬姛");
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[ActiveMQ] 杩炴帴楠岃瘉澶辫触锛宐rokerUrl={}", properties.resolvedBrokerUrl(), e);
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("ActiveMQ 杩炴帴澶辫触锛岃妫€鏌ラ厤缃細" + e.getMessage()).build();
        }
    }
}

import com.remisoft.comm.queue.config.QueueProperties;
import lombok.Data;
import lombok.EqualsAndHashCode;

/**
 * ActiveMQ 娑堟伅闃熷垪閰嶇疆灞炴€?
 *
 * <p>灏佽 ActiveMQ 娑堟伅闃熷垪鐨勮繛鎺ュ拰琛屼负閰嶇疆鍙傛暟銆?
 * 鏀寔 ActiveMQ Classic 鍜?ActiveMQ Artemis锛堟帹鑽愶級涓ょ妯″紡銆?
 *
 * <p><b>閰嶇疆绀轰緥锛?/b>
 * <pre>{@code
 * remi:
 *   queue:
 *     activemq:
 *       broker-url: tcp://localhost:61616
 *       username: admin
 *       password: admin
 *       queue-name: remi-activemq-queue
 *       concurrent-consumers: 5
 *       max-concurrent-consumers: 10
 * }</pre>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Data
@EqualsAndHashCode(callSuper = true)
public class ActiveMQProperties extends QueueProperties {

    /**
     * ActiveMQ Broker 鍦板潃
     */
    private String brokerUrl = "tcp://localhost:61616";

    /**
     * 鐢ㄦ埛鍚?

     * <p>榛樿鍊间负 "admin"锛屼粎鐢ㄤ簬鏈湴寮€鍙戠幆澧冦€傜敓浜х幆澧冨姟蹇呴€氳繃閰嶇疆瑕嗙洊銆?
     */
    private String username = "admin";

     * 瀵嗙爜
     *
     * <p>榛樿鍊间负 "admin"锛屼粎鐢ㄤ簬鏈湴寮€鍙戠幆澧冦€傜敓浜х幆澧冨姟蹇呴€氳繃閰嶇疆瑕嗙洊锛?
     * 鍚﹀垯杩炴帴灏嗕娇鐢ㄥ急瀵嗙爜锛屽瓨鍦ㄥ畨鍏ㄩ闄┿€?
     */
    private String password = "admin";

    /**
     * 榛樿闃熷垪鍚嶇О
     */
    private String queueName = "remi-activemq-queue";

    /**
     * 鏄惁浣跨敤 Artemis锛堟帹鑽愶級

    private boolean artemis = true;

    /**

    private int concurrentConsumers = 5;

    private int maxMessagesPerTask = -1;

    /**
     * 鏄惁鎸佷箙鍖栨秷鎭?
     */
    private boolean persistent = true;

    /**
     * 瑙ｆ瀽鑾峰彇 brokerUrl
     */
    public String resolvedBrokerUrl() {
        return isNotBlank(brokerUrl) ? brokerUrl : "tcp://localhost:61616";
    }

    /**
     * 瑙ｆ瀽鑾峰彇 username
     */
    public String resolvedUsername() {
        return isNotBlank(username) ? username : "admin";
    }

     */
    public String resolvedPassword() {
        return password;
    }

     * 瑙ｆ瀽鑾峰彇 queueName
     */
    public String resolvedQueueName() {
        return isNotBlank(queueName) ? queueName : "remi-activemq-queue";
    }

    private boolean isNotBlank(String str) {
        return str != null && !str.trim().isEmpty();
    }

=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\mq\active\ActiveMQPublisher.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.mq.active;

import com.remisoft.comm.exception.custom.InfrastructureException;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.domain.QueueMessage;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessagePublisher;
import jakarta.jms.*;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.apache.activemq.ActiveMQConnectionFactory;

/**
 * ActiveMQ 娑堟伅鍙戝竷鑰?
 *
 * <p>浣跨敤 ActiveMQ Artemis JMS API 瀹炵幇娑堟伅鍙戝竷鍔熻兘銆?
 * 鏀寔 ActiveMQ Classic 鍜?ActiveMQ Artemis 涓ょ妯″紡銆?
 *
 * <p><b>鎶€鏈壒鐐癸細</b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>JMS 鏍囧噯锛氶伒寰?Java Message Service 瑙勮寖</li>
 *   <li>澶氱妯″紡锛氭敮鎸佺偣瀵圭偣鍜屽彂甯?璁㈤槄妯″紡</li>
 *   <li>娑堟伅鎸佷箙鍖栵細鏀寔娑堟伅鎸佷箙鍖栦繚璇佸彲闈犳€?/li>
 *   <li>浜嬪姟鏀寔锛氭敮鎸?JMS 浜嬪姟</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>浣跨敤绀轰緥锛?/b>
 * <pre>{@code
 * ActiveMQPublisher publisher = new ActiveMQPublisher(properties, "my-queue");
 * publisher.publish("Hello ActiveMQ");
 * publisher.publish(QueueMessage.of("Hello"));
 * publisher.close();
 * }</pre>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
public class ActiveMQPublisher implements IMessagePublisher {

    private final ActiveMQConnectionFactory connectionFactory;
    private final Connection connection;
    private final Session session;
    private final MessageProducer producer;
    private final String queueName;
    private volatile boolean closed = false;

    public ActiveMQPublisher(ActiveMQProperties properties, String queueName) {
        if (properties == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("ActiveMQ 閰嶇疆涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        if (queueName == null || queueName.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("闃熷垪鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        this.queueName = queueName;
        try {
            this.connectionFactory = createConnectionFactory(properties);
            this.connection = connectionFactory.createConnection();
            this.session = connection.createSession(false, Session.CLIENT_ACKNOWLEDGE);
            Destination destination = session.createQueue(queueName);
            this.producer = session.createProducer(destination);
            if (properties.isPersistent()) {
                producer.setDeliveryMode(DeliveryMode.PERSISTENT);
            }
            connection.start();
            log.info("[ActiveMQ] 鍙戝竷鑰呭垵濮嬪寲瀹屾垚锛宷ueue={}, broker={}",
                    queueName, properties.resolvedBrokerUrl());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[ActiveMQ] 鍒濆鍖栧彂甯冭€呭け璐ワ紝queue={}", queueName, e);
            throw new InfrastructureException("ActiveMQ 鍙戝竷鑰呭垵濮嬪寲澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void publish(String message) {
        if (message == null || closed) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            QueueMessage queueMessage = QueueMessage.fromPayload(message);
            if (queueMessage == null) {
                queueMessage = QueueMessage.of(message);
            }
            publish(queueMessage);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[ActiveMQ] 娑堟伅鍙戝竷澶辫触锛宷ueue={}", queueName, e);
            throw new InfrastructureException("ActiveMQ 娑堟伅鍙戝竷澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void publish(QueueMessage message) {
        if (message == null || closed) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            String payload = QueueMessage.toPayload(message);
            TextMessage textMessage = session.createTextMessage(payload);
            textMessage.setJMSMessageID(message.getTraceId());
            producer.send(textMessage);
            if (log.isDebugEnabled()) {
                log.debug("[ActiveMQ] 娑堟伅宸插彂閫侊紝queue={}, traceId={}", queueName, message.getTraceId());
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[ActiveMQ] 娑堟伅鍙戝竷澶辫触锛宷ueue={}, traceId={}", queueName, message.getTraceId(), e);
            throw new InfrastructureException("ActiveMQ 娑堟伅鍙戝竷澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public String getChannel() {
        return queueName;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isActive() {
        return !closed && connection != null;
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        if (closed) {
            return;
        }
        synchronized (this) {
            if (closed) {
                return;
            }
            closed = true;
            try {
                if (producer != null) {
                    producer.close();
                }
                if (session != null) {
                    session.close();
                }
                if (connection != null) {
                    connection.close();
                }
                log.info("[ActiveMQ] 鍙戝竷鑰呭凡鍏抽棴锛宷ueue={}", queueName);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.warn("[ActiveMQ] 鍏抽棴鍙戝竷鑰呮椂鍙戠敓寮傚父", e);
            }
        }
    }

    private ActiveMQConnectionFactory createConnectionFactory(ActiveMQProperties properties) {
        ActiveMQConnectionFactory factory = new ActiveMQConnectionFactory();
        factory.setBrokerURL(properties.resolvedBrokerUrl());
        factory.setUserName(properties.resolvedUsername());
        factory.setPassword(properties.resolvedPassword());
        return factory;
    }
}

=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\mq\active\ActiveMQSubscriber.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.mq.active;

import com.remisoft.comm.exception.custom.InfrastructureException;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.domain.QueueMessage;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.rate.ConsumerRateLimiter;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.recovery.ConsumerThreadGuard;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessageHandler;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessageSubscriber;
import jakarta.jms.*;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.apache.activemq.ActiveMQConnectionFactory;

import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference;

/**
 * ActiveMQ 娑堟伅璁㈤槄鑰?
 *
 * <p>浣跨敤 ActiveMQ Artemis JMS API 瀹炵幇娑堟伅娑堣垂鍔熻兘銆?
 * 鏀寔鍚屾鎷夊彇鍜屽紓姝ユ寔缁秷璐逛袱绉嶆ā寮忋€?
 *
 * <p><b>鎶€鏈壒鐐癸細</b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>JMS 鏍囧噯锛氶伒寰?Java Message Service 瑙勮寖</li>
 *   <li>鍚屾/寮傛锛氭敮鎸佸悓姝ユ媺鍙栧拰寮傛鐩戝惉</li>
 *   <li>娑堟伅杩囨护锛氭敮鎸佹秷鎭€夋嫨鍣ㄨ繃婊?/li>
 *   <li>娑堣垂闄愭祦锛氭敮鎸?prefetch 闄愭祦</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>娑堣垂妯″紡锛?/b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>鍚屾鎷夊彇锛氳皟鐢?subscribe() 闃诲绛夊緟娑堟伅</li>
 *   <li>寮傛鐩戝惉锛氬疄鐜?MessageListener 鎸佺画鎺ユ敹娑堟伅</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
public class ActiveMQSubscriber implements IMessageSubscriber {

    private final ActiveMQConnectionFactory connectionFactory;
    private final Connection connection;
    private final Session session;
    private final MessageConsumer consumer;
    private final String queueName;

    private final AtomicBoolean running;
    private final AtomicLong consumedCount;
    private final AtomicReference<Throwable> lastError;
    private final ConsumerRateLimiter rateLimiter;
    private final ExecutorService consumerExecutor;

    private volatile Thread consumerThread;
    private volatile ConsumerThreadGuard threadGuard;

    public ActiveMQSubscriber(ActiveMQProperties properties, String queueName, ExecutorService consumerExecutor) {
        if (properties == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("ActiveMQ 閰嶇疆涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        if (queueName == null || queueName.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("闃熷垪鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        this.queueName = queueName;
        this.running = new AtomicBoolean(false);
        this.consumedCount = new AtomicLong(0);
        this.lastError = new AtomicReference<>();
        this.rateLimiter = new ConsumerRateLimiter(properties.resolvedConsumerRateLimitPerSecond());
        this.consumerExecutor = consumerExecutor;

        try {
            this.connectionFactory = createConnectionFactory(properties);
            this.connection = connectionFactory.createConnection();
            this.session = connection.createSession(false, Session.CLIENT_ACKNOWLEDGE);
            Destination destination = session.createQueue(queueName);
            this.consumer = session.createConsumer(destination);
            connection.start();
            log.info("[ActiveMQ] 璁㈤槄鑰呭垵濮嬪寲瀹屾垚锛宷ueue={}, broker={}",
                    queueName, properties.resolvedBrokerUrl());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[ActiveMQ] 鍒濆鍖栬闃呰€呭け璐ワ紝queue={}", queueName, e);
            throw new InfrastructureException("ActiveMQ 璁㈤槄鑰呭垵濮嬪寲澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public String subscribe() {
        try {
            Message message = consumer.receive(1000);
            if (message == null) {
                return null;
            }
            String body = extractMessageBody(message);
            if (body != null) {
                message.acknowledge();
                consumedCount.incrementAndGet();
            }
            return body;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            lastError.set(e);
            log.error("[ActiveMQ] 鎷夊彇娑堟伅寮傚父锛宷ueue={}", queueName, e);
            return null;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public String subscribeAsync(IMessageHandler handler) {
        if (!running.compareAndSet(false, true)) {
            log.warn("[ActiveMQ] 璁㈤槄鑰呭凡鍦ㄨ繍琛屼腑锛宷ueue={}", queueName);
            return queueName;
        }
        if (consumerExecutor != null) {
            threadGuard = new ConsumerThreadGuard("activemq-" + queueName, 10, consumerExecutor);
            threadGuard.start(() -> consumeLoop(handler));
            log.info("[ActiveMQ] 寮傛娑堣垂宸插惎鍔紙绾跨▼姹犳墭绠★級锛宷ueue={}", queueName);
        } else {
            consumerThread = new Thread(() -> consumeLoop(handler), "remi-queue-activemq-" + queueName);
            consumerThread.setDaemon(true);
            consumerThread.start();
            log.warn("[ActiveMQ] 寮傛娑堣垂宸插惎鍔紙瑁哥嚎绋嬶紝涓嶆帹鑽愶級锛宷ueue={}", queueName);
        }
        return queueName;
    }

    @Override
    public void stop() {
        if (!running.compareAndSet(true, false)) {
            return;
        }
        log.info("[ActiveMQ] 鏀跺埌鍋滄淇″彿锛宷ueue={}", queueName);
        if (threadGuard != null) {
            threadGuard.stop();
        }
        if (consumerThread != null) {
            consumerThread.interrupt();
        }
        try {
            if (consumer != null) {
                consumer.close();
            }
            if (session != null) {
                session.close();
            }
            if (connection != null) {
                connection.close();
            }
            log.info("[ActiveMQ] 璁㈤槄鑰呭凡鍋滄锛宷ueue={}", queueName);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.warn("[ActiveMQ] 鍋滄璁㈤槄鑰呮椂鍙戠敓寮傚父", e);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isRunning() {
        return running.get();
    }

    @Override
    public Object getChannel() {
        return queueName;
    }

    @Override
    public String getConsumerId() {
        return queueName;
    }

    @Override
    public int getConsumedCount() {
        return (int) consumedCount.get();
    }

    @Override
    public Throwable getLastError() {
        return lastError.get();
    }

    private void consumeLoop(IMessageHandler handler) {
        try {
            while (running.get()) {
                try {
                    Message message = consumer.receive(1000);
                    if (message == null) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    rateLimiter.acquire();
                    processMessage(message, handler);
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    lastError.set(e);
                    log.error("[ActiveMQ] 娑堣垂寰幆寮傚父锛宷ueue={}", queueName, e);
                    sleepQuietly(1000);
                }
            }
        } finally {
            running.set(false);
            log.info("[ActiveMQ] 娑堣垂寰幆宸查€€鍑猴紝queue={}", queueName);
        }
    }

    private String processMessageBody(Message message) {
        try {
            return message.getJMSMessageID();
        } catch (JMSException e) {
            return "unknown";
        }
    }

    private void processMessage(Message message, IMessageHandler handler) {
        if (message == null) {
            return;
        }
        String messageId = processMessageBody(message);
        try {
            String body = extractMessageBody(message);
            if (body == null) {
                return;
            }
            QueueMessage queueMessage = QueueMessage.fromPayload(body);
            if (queueMessage == null) {
                queueMessage = QueueMessage.of(body);
            }
            queueMessage.setTraceId(messageId);

            if (handler != null) {
                handler.onMessage(queueMessage);
            }
            message.acknowledge();
            consumedCount.incrementAndGet();
            lastError.set(null);
            log.debug("[ActiveMQ] 娑堟伅澶勭悊鎴愬姛锛宷ueue={}, messageId={}", queueName, messageId);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            lastError.set(e);
            log.error("[ActiveMQ] 娑堟伅澶勭悊寮傚父锛宷ueue={}, messageId={}", queueName, messageId, e);
        }
    }

    private String extractMessageBody(Message message) {
        try {
            if (message instanceof TextMessage) {
                return ((TextMessage) message).getText();
            }
            return message.toString();
        } catch (JMSException e) {
            log.warn("[ActiveMQ] 鎻愬彇娑堟伅浣撳け璐?, e);
            return null;
        }
    }

    private ActiveMQConnectionFactory createConnectionFactory(ActiveMQProperties properties) {
        ActiveMQConnectionFactory factory = new ActiveMQConnectionFactory();
        factory.setBrokerURL(properties.resolvedBrokerUrl());
        factory.setUserName(properties.resolvedUsername());
        factory.setPassword(properties.resolvedPassword());
        return factory;
    }

    private void sleepQuietly(long millis) {
        try {
            Thread.sleep(millis);
        } catch (InterruptedException ignored) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
    }
}
