package com.remisoft.comm.queue.mq.kafka;

import com.remisoft.comm.exception.custom.InfrastructureException;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.domain.QueueMessage;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessagePublisher;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.producer.Callback;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.producer.KafkaProducer;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.producer.ProducerConfig;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.producer.ProducerRecord;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.producer.RecordMetadata;
import org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.StringSerializer;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.Properties;

/**
 * Kafka 娑堟伅鍙戝竷鑰?
 *
 * <p>浣跨敤 Kafka Producer API 瀹炵幇娑堟伅鍙戝竷鍔熻兘銆?
 * 鏀寔鍚屾鍜屽紓姝ュ彂閫侊紝鑷姩澶勭悊娑堟伅搴忓垪鍖栥€?
 *
 * <p><b>鎶€鏈壒鐐癸細</b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>楂樺悶鍚愰噺锛氶€傚悎澶ц妯℃秷鎭紶閫掑満鏅?/li>
 *   <li>寮傛鍙戦€侊細鏀寔寮傛鍙戦€佹彁楂樻€ц兘</li>
 *   <li>鍒嗗尯鏀寔锛氬彲鏍规嵁娑堟伅閿疄鐜版秷鎭垎鍖?/li>
 *   <li>鎵归噺鍙戦€侊細鏀寔鎵归噺鍙戦€佸噺灏戠綉缁滃紑閿€</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>浣跨敤绀轰緥锛?/b>
 * <pre>{@code
 * KafkaMessagePublisher publisher = new KafkaMessagePublisher(properties, "my-topic");
 * publisher.publish("Hello Kafka");
 * publisher.publish(QueueMessage.of("Hello"));
 * publisher.close();
 * }</pre>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
public class KafkaMessagePublisher implements IMessagePublisher {

    private final KafkaProducer<String, String> producer;
    private final String topic;
    private volatile boolean closed = false;

    public KafkaMessagePublisher(KafkaQueueProperties properties, String topic) {
        if (properties == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Kafka 閰嶇疆涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        if (topic == null || topic.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("涓婚鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        this.topic = topic;
        this.producer = createProducer(properties);
        log.info("[Kafka] 鍙戝竷鑰呭垵濮嬪寲瀹屾垚锛宼opic={}, bootstrapServers={}",
                topic, properties.resolvedBootstrapServers());
    }

    @Override
    public void publish(String message) {
        if (message == null || closed) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            QueueMessage queueMessage = QueueMessage.fromPayload(message);
            if (queueMessage == null) {
                queueMessage = QueueMessage.of(message);
            }
            publish(queueMessage);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[Kafka] 娑堟伅鍙戝竷澶辫触锛宼opic={}", topic, e);
            throw new InfrastructureException("Kafka 娑堟伅鍙戝竷澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void publish(QueueMessage message) {
        if (message == null || closed) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            String payload = QueueMessage.toPayload(message);
            String key = message.getTraceId();
            ProducerRecord<String, String> record = new ProducerRecord<>(topic, key, payload);
            producer.send(record, new Callback() {
                @Override
                public void onCompletion(RecordMetadata metadata, Exception exception) {
                    if (exception != null) {
                        log.error("[Kafka] 娑堟伅鍙戦€佸け璐ュ洖璋冿紝topic={}, traceId={}, partition={}, error={}",
                                topic, key,
                                metadata != null ? metadata.partition() : -1,
                                exception.getMessage());
                    } else if (log.isDebugEnabled()) {
                        log.debug("[Kafka] 娑堟伅鍙戦€佹垚鍔燂紝topic={}, traceId={}, partition={}, offset={}",
                                topic, key, metadata.partition(), metadata.offset());
                    }
                }
            });
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[Kafka] 娑堟伅鍙戝竷澶辫触锛宼opic={}, traceId={}", topic, message.getTraceId(), e);
            throw new InfrastructureException("Kafka 娑堟伅鍙戝竷澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void publishDelayed(QueueMessage message, long delayMillis) {
        log.warn("[Kafka] 寤惰繜娑堟伅鏆備笉鏀寔锛宼opic={}", topic);
        publish(message);
    }

    @Override
    public String getChannel() {
        return topic;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isActive() {
        return !closed && producer != null;
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        if (closed) {
            return;
        }
        synchronized (this) {
            if (closed) {
                return;
            }
            closed = true;
            try {
                if (producer != null) {
                    producer.flush();
                    producer.close(Duration.ofSeconds(5));
                    log.info("[Kafka] 鍙戝竷鑰呭凡鍏抽棴锛宼opic={}", topic);
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.warn("[Kafka] 鍏抽棴鍙戝竷鑰呮椂鍙戠敓寮傚父", e);
            }
        }
    }

    private KafkaProducer<String, String> createProducer(KafkaQueueProperties properties) {
        Properties props = new Properties();
        props.put(ProducerConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, properties.resolvedBootstrapServers());
        props.put(ProducerConfig.KEY_SERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, StringSerializer.class.getName());
        props.put(ProducerConfig.VALUE_SERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, StringSerializer.class.getName());
        props.put(ProducerConfig.ACKS_CONFIG, "all");
        props.put(ProducerConfig.RETRIES_CONFIG, 3);
        props.put(ProducerConfig.BATCH_SIZE_CONFIG, 16384);
        props.put(ProducerConfig.LINGER_MS_CONFIG, 5);
        props.put(ProducerConfig.BUFFER_MEMORY_CONFIG, 33554432);
        props.put(ProducerConfig.MAX_BLOCK_MS_CONFIG, 5000);
        return new KafkaProducer<>(props);
    }
}

package com.remisoft.comm.queue.mq.kafka;

import com.remisoft.comm.queue.domain.QueueMessage;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.rate.ConsumerRateLimiter;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.recovery.ConsumerThreadGuard;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessageHandler;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessageSubscriber;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.consumer.ConsumerConfig;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.consumer.ConsumerRecord;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.consumer.ConsumerRecords;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.consumer.KafkaConsumer;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.consumer.OffsetAndMetadata;
import org.apache.kafka.common.TopicPartition;
import org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.StringDeserializer;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.Properties;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference;

/**
 * Kafka 娑堟伅璁㈤槄鑰?
 *
 * <p>浣跨敤 Kafka Consumer API 瀹炵幇娑堟伅娑堣垂鍔熻兘銆?
 * 鏀寔鍚屾鎷夊彇鍜屽紓姝ユ寔缁秷璐逛袱绉嶆ā寮忋€?
 *
 * <p><b>鎶€鏈壒鐐癸細</b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>娑堣垂缁勬敮鎸侊細澶氫釜娑堣垂鑰呭崗鍚屾秷璐瑰疄鐜拌礋杞藉潎琛?/li>
 *   <li>鍋忕Щ閲忕鐞嗭細鏀寔鎵嬪姩鎻愪氦鍋忕Щ閲忓疄鐜扮簿纭帶鍒?/li>
 *   <li>娑堟伅鍥炴函锛氫粠earliest鎴杔atest浣嶇疆寮€濮嬫秷璐?/li>
 *   <li>鍒嗗尯绛栫暐锛氬悓涓€鍒嗗尯娑堟伅鏈夊簭</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>娑堣垂妯″紡锛?/b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>鍚屾鎷夊彇锛氳皟鐢?subscribe() 闃诲绛夊緟鍗曟潯娑堟伅</li>
 *   <li>寮傛鎸佺画娑堣垂锛氳皟鐢?subscribeAsync(handler) 鍚庡彴绾跨▼鎸佺画娑堣垂</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>鍋忕Щ閲忔彁浜わ細</b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>enableAutoCommit=false锛氭墜鍔ㄦ彁浜ゅ亸绉婚噺</li>
 *   <li>娑堟伅澶勭悊鎴愬姛鍚庤嚜鍔ㄦ彁浜?/li>
 *   <li>淇濊瘉鑷冲皯涓€娆℃秷璐硅涔?/li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
public class KafkaMessageSubscriber implements IMessageSubscriber {

    private final KafkaConsumer<String, String> consumer;
    private final String topic;
    private final String groupId;

    private final AtomicBoolean running;
    private final AtomicLong consumedCount;
    private final AtomicReference<Throwable> lastError;

    private final ArrayDeque<ConsumerRecord<String, String>> recordBuffer = new ArrayDeque<>();
    private final ConsumerRateLimiter rateLimiter;
    private final ExecutorService consumerExecutor;

    private volatile Thread consumerThread;
    private volatile ConsumerThreadGuard threadGuard;

    public KafkaMessageSubscriber(KafkaQueueProperties properties, String topic, ExecutorService consumerExecutor) {
        if (properties == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Kafka 閰嶇疆涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        if (topic == null || topic.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("涓婚鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        this.topic = topic;
        this.groupId = properties.resolvedGroupId();
        this.consumer = createConsumer(properties);
        this.running = new AtomicBoolean(false);
        this.consumedCount = new AtomicLong(0);
        this.lastError = new AtomicReference<>();
        this.rateLimiter = new ConsumerRateLimiter(properties.resolvedConsumerRateLimitPerSecond());
        this.consumerExecutor = consumerExecutor;
        log.info("[Kafka] 璁㈤槄鑰呭垵濮嬪寲瀹屾垚锛宼opic={}, groupId={}", topic, groupId);
    }

    @Override
    public String subscribe() {
        try {
            if (recordBuffer.isEmpty()) {
                ConsumerRecords<String, String> records = consumer.poll(Duration.ofMillis(1000));
                if (records != null) {
                    for (ConsumerRecord<String, String> record : records) {
                        if (record != null && record.value() != null) {
                            recordBuffer.addLast(record);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            if (recordBuffer.isEmpty()) {
                return null;
            }
            ConsumerRecord<String, String> record = recordBuffer.removeFirst();
            consumedCount.incrementAndGet();
            consumer.commitSync(Collections.singletonMap(
                    new TopicPartition(record.topic(), record.partition()),
                    new OffsetAndMetadata(record.offset() + 1)
            ));
            return record.value();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            lastError.set(e);
            log.error("[Kafka] 鎷夊彇娑堟伅寮傚父锛宼opic={}, groupId={}", topic, groupId, e);
            return null;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public String subscribeAsync(IMessageHandler handler) {
        if (!running.compareAndSet(false, true)) {
            log.warn("[Kafka] 璁㈤槄鑰呭凡鍦ㄨ繍琛屼腑锛宼opic={}, groupId={}", topic, groupId);
            return groupId;
        }
        if (consumerExecutor != null) {
            threadGuard = new ConsumerThreadGuard("kafka-" + groupId, 10, consumerExecutor);
            threadGuard.start(() -> consumeLoop(handler));
            log.info("[Kafka] 寮傛娑堣垂宸插惎鍔紙绾跨▼姹犳墭绠★級锛宼opic={}, groupId={}", topic, groupId);
        } else {
            consumerThread = new Thread(() -> consumeLoop(handler), "remi-queue-kafka-" + groupId);
            consumerThread.setDaemon(true);
            consumerThread.start();
            log.warn("[Kafka] 寮傛娑堣垂宸插惎鍔紙瑁哥嚎绋嬶紝涓嶆帹鑽愶級锛宼opic={}, groupId={}", topic, groupId);
        }
        return groupId;
    }

    @Override
    public void stop() {
        if (!running.compareAndSet(true, false)) {
            return;
        }
        log.info("[Kafka] 鏀跺埌鍋滄淇″彿锛宼opic={}, groupId={}", topic, groupId);
        if (threadGuard != null) {
            threadGuard.stop();
        }
        if (consumerThread != null) {
            consumerThread.interrupt();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isRunning() {
        return running.get();
    }

    @Override
    public Object getChannel() {
        return topic;
    }

    @Override
    public String getConsumerId() {
        return groupId;
    }

    @Override
    public int getConsumedCount() {
        return (int) consumedCount.get();
    }

    @Override
    public Throwable getLastError() {
        return lastError.get();
    }

    private void consumeLoop(IMessageHandler handler) {
        try {
            consumer.subscribe(Collections.singletonList(topic));
            log.debug("[Kafka] 宸茶闃呬富棰橈紝topic={}", topic);

            while (running.get()) {
                try {
                    ConsumerRecords<String, String> records = consumer.poll(Duration.ofMillis(1000));
                    if (records == null || records.isEmpty()) {
                        continue;
                    }

                    for (ConsumerRecord<String, String> record : records) {
                        if (!running.get()) {
                            break;
                        }
                        rateLimiter.acquire();
                        processRecord(record, handler);
                    }
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    lastError.set(e);
                    log.error("[Kafka] 娑堣垂寰幆寮傚父锛宼opic={}, groupId={}", topic, groupId, e);
                    sleepQuietly(1000);
                }
            }
        } finally {
            running.set(false);
            log.info("[Kafka] 娑堣垂寰幆宸查€€鍑猴紝topic={}, groupId={}", topic, groupId);
        }
    }

    private void processRecord(ConsumerRecord<String, String> record, IMessageHandler handler) {
        if (record == null || record.value() == null) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            QueueMessage message = QueueMessage.fromPayload(record.value());
            if (message == null) {
                message = QueueMessage.of(record.value());
            }
            if (handler != null) {
                handler.onMessage(message);
            }
            consumer.commitSync();
            consumedCount.incrementAndGet();
            lastError.set(null);
            log.debug("[Kafka] 娑堟伅澶勭悊鎴愬姛锛宼opic={}, partition={}, offset={}, traceId={}",
                    record.topic(), record.partition(), record.offset(), message.getTraceId());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            lastError.set(e);
            log.error("[Kafka] 娑堟伅澶勭悊寮傚父锛宼opic={}, partition={}, offset={}",
                    record.topic(), record.partition(), record.offset(), e);
        }
    }

    private KafkaConsumer<String, String> createConsumer(KafkaQueueProperties properties) {
        Properties props = new Properties();
        props.put(ConsumerConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, properties.resolvedBootstrapServers());
        props.put(ConsumerConfig.GROUP_ID_CONFIG, properties.resolvedGroupId());
        props.put(ConsumerConfig.KEY_DESERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, StringDeserializer.class.getName());
        props.put(ConsumerConfig.VALUE_DESERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, StringDeserializer.class.getName());
        props.put(ConsumerConfig.ENABLE_AUTO_COMMIT_CONFIG, properties.isEnableAutoCommit());
        props.put(ConsumerConfig.AUTO_OFFSET_RESET_CONFIG, properties.getAutoOffsetReset());
        props.put(ConsumerConfig.MAX_POLL_RECORDS_CONFIG, properties.getMaxPollRecords());
        props.put(ConsumerConfig.SESSION_TIMEOUT_MS_CONFIG, properties.getSessionTimeoutMs());
        props.put(ConsumerConfig.HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS_CONFIG, properties.getHeartbeatIntervalMs());
        return new KafkaConsumer<>(props);
    }

    private void sleepQuietly(long millis) {
        try {
            Thread.sleep(millis);
        } catch (InterruptedException ignored) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
    }
}

 * @since 1.0.0

@Slf4j
public class KafkaMQ extends AbstractMessageQueue {

    private final KafkaQueueProperties properties;
    private final ExecutorService consumerExecutor;

    public KafkaMQ(KafkaQueueProperties properties, ExecutorService consumerExecutor) {
        super("Kafka");
        if (properties == null) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("Kafka 閰嶇疆涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        this.properties = properties;
        this.consumerExecutor = consumerExecutor;
        validateConnection();
        log.info("[KafkaMQ] 鍒濆鍖栨垚鍔燂紝bootstrapServers={}", properties.resolvedBootstrapServers());
    }

    @Override
    public IMessagePublisher createPublisher(String channel) {
        checkNotClosed();
        if (channel == null || channel.isEmpty()) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("涓婚鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        return new KafkaMessagePublisher(properties, channel);
    }

    @Override
    public IMessageSubscriber createSubscriber(String channel) {
        checkNotClosed();
        if (channel == null || channel.isEmpty()) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("涓婚鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        return new KafkaMessageSubscriber(properties, channel, consumerExecutor);
    }

    @Override
    protected void doClose() {
    }

    private void validateConnection() {
        try {
            Properties props = new Properties();
            props.put(AdminClientConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, properties.resolvedBootstrapServers());
            props.put(ProducerConfig.KEY_SERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, StringSerializer.class.getName());
            props.put(ProducerConfig.VALUE_SERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, StringSerializer.class.getName());
            try (AdminClient adminClient = AdminClient.create(props)) {
                ListTopicsResult result = adminClient.listTopics(new ListTopicsOptions().timeoutMs(5000));
                result.names().get(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
            }
            log.debug("[KafkaMQ] 杩炴帴楠岃瘉鎴愬姛");
        } catch (TimeoutException | ExecutionException e) {
            log.error("[KafkaMQ] 杩炴帴楠岃瘉澶辫触锛宐ootstrapServers={}", properties.resolvedBootstrapServers(), e);
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("Kafka 杩炴帴澶辫触锛岃妫€鏌ラ厤缃細" + e.getMessage()).build();
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("Kafka 杩炴帴楠岃瘉琚腑鏂?).build();
        }
    }
}

@EqualsAndHashCode(callSuper = true)
public class KafkaQueueProperties extends QueueProperties {

    /**
     * Kafka 鏈嶅姟鍦板潃
     */

    /**
     * 娑堣垂鑰呯粍ID
     */
    private String groupId = "remi-consumer-group";

    /**
     * 榛樿涓婚
     */

    /**
     * 姣忔鏈€澶ф媺鍙栨秷鎭暟
     */
    private int maxPollRecords = 10;

    /**
     * 娑堣垂鑰呬細璇濊秴鏃舵椂闂达紙姣锛?
     */
    private int sessionTimeoutMs = 30000;

    /**
     * 娑堣垂鑰呭績璺抽棿闅旀椂闂达紙姣锛?
     */
    private int heartbeatIntervalMs = 10000;

    /**
     * 娑堟伅閿簭鍒楀寲鍣?
     */
    private String keySerializer = "org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.StringSerializer";

    /**
     * 娑堟伅鍊煎簭鍒楀寲鍣?
     */
    private String valueSerializer = "org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.StringSerializer";

    /**
     * 娑堟伅閿弽搴忓垪鍖栧櫒
     */
    private String keyDeserializer = "org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.StringDeserializer";

    /**
     * 娑堟伅鍊煎弽搴忓垪鍖栧櫒
     */
    private String valueDeserializer = "org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.StringDeserializer";

    /**
     * 瑙ｆ瀽鑾峰彇 bootstrap-servers
     */
    public String resolvedBootstrapServers() {
        return isNotBlank(bootstrapServers) ? bootstrapServers : "localhost:9092";
    }

    /**
     * 瑙ｆ瀽鑾峰彇 group-id
     */
    public String resolvedGroupId() {
        return isNotBlank(groupId) ? groupId : "remi-consumer-group";
    }

    /**
     * 瑙ｆ瀽鑾峰彇 topic
     */
    public String resolvedTopic() {
        return isNotBlank(topic) ? topic : "remi-kafka-topic";
    }

    private boolean isNotBlank(String str) {
        return str != null && !str.trim().isEmpty();
    }

 * RabbitMQ 娑堟伅闃熷垪
 *
 * <p>RabbitMQ 鏄熀浜?AMQP 鍗忚鐨勬秷鎭腑闂翠欢锛屽叿鏈夐珮鍙潬鎬с€佺伒娲昏矾鐢辩瓑鐗圭偣銆?

 * <p><b>鎶€鏈壒鐐癸細</b>

 *   <li>澶氱浜ゆ崲鏈猴細Direct銆丗anout銆乀opic銆丠eaders</li>
 *   <li>娑堟伅纭锛氭敮鎸?Publisher Confirms 鍜?Consumer ACK</li>

 * <ul>
 *   <li>Direct锛氬畬鍏ㄥ尮閰嶈矾鐢遍敭 routing-key</li>
 *   <li>Fanout锛氬箍鎾埌鎵€鏈夌粦瀹氱殑闃熷垪</li>
 *   <li>Topic锛氭敮鎸侀€氶厤绗﹀尮閰嶏紙* 鍜?#锛?/li>
 *   <li>Headers锛氬熀浜庢秷鎭ご鐨勫尮閰?/li>

 *   <li>寰湇鍔￠棿閫氫俊</li>
 *   <li>浜嬩欢椹卞姩鏋舵瀯</li>
 *   <li>娑堟伅璺敱鍜岃繃婊?/li>

 * <p><b>浣跨敤绀轰緥锛?/b>
 * <pre>{@code
 * IMessageQueue queue = messageQueueProvider.createMessageQueue(QueueType.rabbit);
 * IMessagePublisher publisher = queue.createPublisher("my-queue");
 * IMessageSubscriber subscriber = queue.createSubscriber("my-queue");
 *
 * publisher.publish("Hello RabbitMQ");
 *
 * subscriber.subscribeAsync(message -> {
 *     log.info("Received: {}", message.getBody());
 * });
 * }</pre>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
public class RabbitMQ extends AbstractMessageQueue {

    private final RabbitMQProperties properties;

    public RabbitMQ(RabbitMQProperties properties) {
        super("RabbitMQ");
        if (properties == null) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("RabbitMQ 閰嶇疆涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        this.properties = properties;
        validateConnection();
        log.info("[RabbitMQ] 鍒濆鍖栨垚鍔燂紝host={}:{}", properties.resolvedHost(), properties.resolvedPort());
    }

    @Override
    public IMessagePublisher createPublisher(String channel) {
        checkNotClosed();
        if (channel == null || channel.isEmpty()) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("闃熷垪鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        return new RabbitMQPublisher(properties, channel);
    }

    @Override
    public IMessageSubscriber createSubscriber(String channel) {
        checkNotClosed();
        if (channel == null || channel.isEmpty()) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("闃熷垪鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        return new RabbitMQSubscriber(properties, channel);
    }

    @Override
    protected void doClose() {
    }

    private void validateConnection() {
        try {
            ConnectionFactory factory = new ConnectionFactory();
            factory.setHost(properties.resolvedHost());
            factory.setPort(properties.resolvedPort());
            factory.setUsername(properties.resolvedUsername());
            factory.setPassword(properties.resolvedPassword());
            factory.setVirtualHost(properties.resolvedVirtualHost());
            try (Connection conn = factory.newConnection()) {
                if (conn.isOpen()) {
                    log.debug("[RabbitMQ] 杩炴帴楠岃瘉鎴愬姛");
                }
            }
        } catch (IOException | TimeoutException e) {
            log.error("[RabbitMQ] 杩炴帴楠岃瘉澶辫触锛宧ost={}:{}", properties.resolvedHost(), properties.resolvedPort(), e);
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("RabbitMQ 杩炴帴澶辫触锛岃妫€鏌ラ厤缃細" + e.getMessage()).build();
        }
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\mq\rabbit\RabbitMQProperties.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.mq.rabbit;

import com.remisoft.comm.queue.config.QueueProperties;
import lombok.Data;
import lombok.EqualsAndHashCode;

/**
 * RabbitMQ 娑堟伅闃熷垪閰嶇疆灞炴€?
 *
 * <p>灏佽 RabbitMQ 娑堟伅闃熷垪鐨勮繛鎺ュ拰琛屼负閰嶇疆鍙傛暟銆?
 *
 * <p><b>閰嶇疆绀轰緥锛?/b>
 * <pre>{@code
 * remi:
 *   queue:
 *     rabbitmq:
 *       host: localhost
 *       port: 5672
 *       username: guest
 *       password: guest
 *       virtual-host: /
 *       queue-name: remi-queue
 *       exchange-name: remi-exchange
 *       routing-key: remi.routing.key
 * }</pre>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Data
@EqualsAndHashCode(callSuper = true)
public class RabbitMQProperties extends QueueProperties {

    /**

    private String host = "localhost";

     * 鍚﹀垯杩炴帴灏嗕娇鐢ㄥ急瀵嗙爜锛屽瓨鍦ㄥ畨鍏ㄩ闄┿€?

    private String password = "guest";

        return isNotBlank(host) ? host : "localhost";

    public String resolvedPassword() {

    }

    private boolean isNotBlank(String str) {
        return str != null && !str.trim().isEmpty();
    }
}

=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\mq\rabbit\RabbitMQPublisher.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.mq.rabbit;

import com.remisoft.comm.exception.custom.InfrastructureException;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.domain.QueueMessage;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessagePublisher;
import com.rabbitmq.client.AMQP;
import com.rabbitmq.client.Channel;
import com.rabbitmq.client.Connection;
import com.rabbitmq.client.ConnectionFactory;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeoutException;

/**
 * RabbitMQ 娑堟伅鍙戝竷鑰?
 *
 * <p>浣跨敤鍘熺敓 amqp-client 瀹炵幇 RabbitMQ 娑堟伅鍙戝竷鍔熻兘銆?
 * 鏀寔浜ゆ崲鏈恒€佽矾鐢遍敭绛?RabbitMQ 鐗规湁姒傚康銆?
 *
 * <p><b>鎶€鏈壒鐐癸細</b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>澶氱浜ゆ崲鏈猴細鏀寔 Direct銆丗anout銆乀opic 绛変氦鎹㈡満绫诲瀷</li>
 *   <li>娑堟伅纭锛氭敮鎸佹秷鎭‘璁ゆ満鍒朵繚璇佸彲闈犳姇閫?/li>
 *   <li>娑堟伅鎸佷箙鍖栵細鏀寔娑堟伅鍜岄槦鍒楃殑鎸佷箙鍖?/li>
 *   <li>鐏垫椿璺敱锛氬熀浜庤矾鐢遍敭鐨勬秷鎭矾鐢?/li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>浣跨敤绀轰緥锛?/b>
 * <pre>{@code
 * RabbitMQPublisher publisher = new RabbitMQPublisher(properties, "my-queue");
 * publisher.publish("Hello RabbitMQ");
 * publisher.publish(QueueMessage.of("Hello"));
 * publisher.close();
 * }</pre>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
public class RabbitMQPublisher implements IMessagePublisher {

    private final Connection connection;
    private final Channel channel;
    private final String routingKey;
    private final String exchangeName;
    private volatile boolean closed = false;

    public RabbitMQPublisher(RabbitMQProperties properties, String queueName) {
        this(properties, queueName, queueName);
    }

    public RabbitMQPublisher(RabbitMQProperties properties, String queueName, String routingKey) {
        if (properties == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("RabbitMQ 閰嶇疆涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        if (queueName == null || queueName.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("闃熷垪鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        this.routingKey = routingKey != null ? routingKey : queueName;
        this.exchangeName = properties.getExchangeName();
        ConnectionFactory factory = new ConnectionFactory();
        factory.setHost(properties.resolvedHost());
        factory.setPort(properties.resolvedPort());
        factory.setUsername(properties.resolvedUsername());
        factory.setPassword(properties.resolvedPassword());
        factory.setVirtualHost(properties.resolvedVirtualHost());
        try {
            this.connection = factory.newConnection();
            this.channel = connection.createChannel();
            channel.exchangeDeclare(exchangeName, "direct", true);
            channel.queueDeclare(queueName, true, false, false, null);
            channel.queueBind(queueName, exchangeName, this.routingKey);
            log.info("[RabbitMQ] 鍙戝竷鑰呭垵濮嬪寲瀹屾垚锛宷ueue={}, exchange={}, routingKey={}",
                    queueName, exchangeName, this.routingKey);
        } catch (IOException | TimeoutException e) {
            log.error("[RabbitMQ] 鍒濆鍖栧彂甯冭€呭け璐ワ紝queue={}", queueName, e);
            throw new InfrastructureException("RabbitMQ 鍙戝竷鑰呭垵濮嬪寲澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void publish(String message) {
        if (message == null || closed) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            QueueMessage queueMessage = QueueMessage.fromPayload(message);
            if (queueMessage == null) {
                queueMessage = QueueMessage.of(message);
            }
            publish(queueMessage);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[RabbitMQ] 娑堟伅鍙戝竷澶辫触锛宺outingKey={}", routingKey, e);
            throw new InfrastructureException("RabbitMQ 娑堟伅鍙戝竷澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void publish(QueueMessage message) {
        if (message == null || closed) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            String payload = QueueMessage.toPayload(message);
            AMQP.BasicProperties props = new AMQP.BasicProperties.Builder()
                    .contentType("application/json")
                    .messageId(message.getTraceId())
                    .deliveryMode(2)
                    .build();
            channel.basicPublish(exchangeName, routingKey, props, payload.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            if (log.isDebugEnabled()) {
                log.debug("[RabbitMQ] 娑堟伅宸插彂閫侊紝routingKey={}, traceId={}", routingKey, message.getTraceId());
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[RabbitMQ] 娑堟伅鍙戝竷澶辫触锛宺outingKey={}, traceId={}", routingKey, message.getTraceId(), e);
            throw new InfrastructureException("RabbitMQ 娑堟伅鍙戝竷澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public String getChannel() {
        return routingKey;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isActive() {
        return !closed && connection != null && connection.isOpen();
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        if (closed) {
            return;
        }
        synchronized (this) {
            if (closed) {
                return;
            }
            closed = true;
            try {
                if (channel != null && channel.isOpen()) {
                    channel.close();
                }
                if (connection != null && connection.isOpen()) {
                    connection.close();
                }
                log.info("[RabbitMQ] 鍙戝竷鑰呭凡鍏抽棴锛宺outingKey={}", routingKey);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.warn("[RabbitMQ] 鍏抽棴鍙戝竷鑰呮椂鍙戠敓寮傚父", e);
            }
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\mq\rabbit\RabbitMQSubscriber.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.mq.rabbit;

import com.remisoft.comm.exception.custom.InfrastructureException;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.domain.QueueMessage;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.rate.ConsumerRateLimiter;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessageHandler;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessageSubscriber;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.trace.MessageTracer;
import com.rabbitmq.client.AMQP;
import com.rabbitmq.client.Channel;
import com.rabbitmq.client.Connection;
import com.rabbitmq.client.ConnectionFactory;
import com.rabbitmq.client.DefaultConsumer;
import com.rabbitmq.client.Envelope;
import com.rabbitmq.client.GetResponse;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeoutException;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference;

/**
 * RabbitMQ 娑堟伅璁㈤槄鑰?
 *
 * <p>浣跨敤鍘熺敓 amqp-client 瀹炵幇 RabbitMQ 娑堟伅娑堣垂鍔熻兘銆?
 * 鏀寔鎵嬪姩 ACK銆佹秷鎭‘璁ょ瓑楂樼骇鐗规€с€?
 *
 * <p><b>鎶€鏈壒鐐癸細</b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>澶氱浜ゆ崲鏈猴細鏀寔 Direct銆丗anout銆乀opic 绛?/li>
 *   <li>鎵嬪姩 ACK锛氭敮鎸佹墜鍔ㄦ秷鎭‘璁?/li>
 *   <li>娑堣垂闄愭祦锛氭敮鎸?prefetchCount 闄愭祦</li>
 *   <li>骞跺彂娑堣垂锛氭敮鎸佸绾跨▼骞跺彂娑堣垂</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>娑堟伅纭鏈哄埗锛?/b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>ACK锛氱‘璁ゆ秷鎭凡鎴愬姛澶勭悊</li>
 *   <li>NACK锛氭嫆缁濇秷鎭紝鍙€夋嫨鏄惁閲嶆柊鍏ラ槦</li>
 *   <li>REJECT锛氭嫆缁濇秷鎭紝涓嶉噸鏂板叆闃?/li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
public class RabbitMQSubscriber implements IMessageSubscriber {

    private static final String HEADER_RETRY_COUNT = "x-retry-count";
    private static final int DEFAULT_MAX_RETRY_COUNT = 3;

    private final Connection connection;
    private final Channel channel;
    private final String queueName;
    private final int maxRetryCount;

    private final AtomicBoolean running;
    private final AtomicLong consumedCount;
    private final AtomicReference<Throwable> lastError;
    private final ConsumerRateLimiter rateLimiter;

    public RabbitMQSubscriber(RabbitMQProperties properties, String queueName) {
        if (properties == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("RabbitMQ 閰嶇疆涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        if (queueName == null || queueName.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("闃熷垪鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        this.queueName = queueName;
        this.maxRetryCount = DEFAULT_MAX_RETRY_COUNT;
        this.running = new AtomicBoolean(false);
        this.consumedCount = new AtomicLong(0);
        this.lastError = new AtomicReference<>();
        this.rateLimiter = new ConsumerRateLimiter(properties.resolvedConsumerRateLimitPerSecond());

        ConnectionFactory factory = new ConnectionFactory();
        factory.setHost(properties.resolvedHost());
        factory.setPort(properties.resolvedPort());
        factory.setUsername(properties.resolvedUsername());
        factory.setPassword(properties.resolvedPassword());
        factory.setVirtualHost(properties.resolvedVirtualHost());

        try {
            this.connection = factory.newConnection();
            this.channel = connection.createChannel();
            channel.basicQos(properties.getPrefetchCount());
            log.info("[RabbitMQ] 璁㈤槄鑰呭垵濮嬪寲瀹屾垚锛宷ueue={}", queueName);
        } catch (IOException | TimeoutException e) {
            log.error("[RabbitMQ] 鍒濆鍖栬闃呰€呭け璐ワ紝queue={}", queueName, e);
            throw new InfrastructureException("RabbitMQ 璁㈤槄鑰呭垵濮嬪寲澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public String subscribe() {
        try {
            AMQP.Queue.DeclareOk result = channel.queueDeclarePassive(queueName);
            if (result == null || result.getMessageCount() == 0) {
                return null;
            }
            GetResponse response = channel.basicGet(queueName, true);
            if (response == null) {
                return null;
            }
            return new String(response.getBody(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            lastError.set(e);
            log.error("[RabbitMQ] 鎷夊彇娑堟伅寮傚父锛宷ueue={}", queueName, e);
            return null;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public String subscribeAsync(IMessageHandler handler) {
        if (!running.compareAndSet(false, true)) {
            log.warn("[RabbitMQ] 璁㈤槄鑰呭凡鍦ㄨ繍琛屼腑锛宷ueue={}", queueName);
            return queueName;
        }
        try {
            channel.basicConsume(queueName, false, new DefaultConsumer(channel) {
                @Override
                public void handleDelivery(String consumerTag, Envelope envelope,
                                          AMQP.BasicProperties properties, byte[] body) {
                    rateLimiter.acquire();
                    processMessage(body, handler, envelope, properties);
                }
            });
            log.info("[RabbitMQ] 寮傛娑堣垂宸插惎鍔紝queue={}", queueName);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            running.set(false);
            lastError.set(e);
            log.error("[RabbitMQ] 鍚姩娑堣垂鑰呭け璐ワ紝queue={}", queueName, e);
            throw new InfrastructureException("RabbitMQ 娑堣垂鑰呭惎鍔ㄥけ璐ワ細" + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
        return queueName;
    }

    private void processMessage(byte[] body, IMessageHandler handler, Envelope envelope,
                                AMQP.BasicProperties properties) {
        if (body == null || body.length == 0) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            String bodyStr = new String(body, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
            QueueMessage message = QueueMessage.fromPayload(bodyStr);
            if (message == null) {
                message = QueueMessage.of(bodyStr);
            }
            MessageTracer.injectTraceId(message.getTraceId());
            if (handler != null) {
                handler.onMessage(message);
            }
            channel.basicAck(envelope.getDeliveryTag(), false);
            consumedCount.incrementAndGet();
            lastError.set(null);
            log.debug("[RabbitMQ] 娑堟伅澶勭悊鎴愬姛锛宷ueue={}, deliveryTag={}, traceId={}",
                    queueName, envelope.getDeliveryTag(), message.getTraceId());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            lastError.set(e);
            int retryCount = getRetryCount(properties);
            log.error("[RabbitMQ] 娑堟伅澶勭悊寮傚父锛宷ueue={}, deliveryTag={}, retryCount={}/{}",
                    queueName, envelope.getDeliveryTag(), retryCount, maxRetryCount, e);
            handleFailedMessage(body, properties, envelope, retryCount);
        } finally {
            MessageTracer.clearTraceId();
        }
    }

    private void handleFailedMessage(byte[] body, AMQP.BasicProperties properties,
                                     Envelope envelope, int retryCount) {
        if (retryCount >= maxRetryCount) {
            safeNack(envelope.getDeliveryTag());
            log.error("[RabbitMQ] 娑堟伅宸茶揪鏈€澶ч噸璇曟鏁帮紝鎷掔粷骞朵笉閲嶆柊鍏ラ槦锛宷ueue={}, deliveryTag={}, retryCount={}",
                    queueName, envelope.getDeliveryTag(), retryCount);
            return;
        }
        try {
            republishWithRetryCount(body, properties, retryCount + 1);
            channel.basicAck(envelope.getDeliveryTag(), false);
            log.warn("[RabbitMQ] 娑堟伅灏嗛噸璇曪紝queue={}, deliveryTag={}, retryCount={}/{}",
                    queueName, envelope.getDeliveryTag(), retryCount + 1, maxRetryCount);
        } catch (IOException ex) {
            log.error("[RabbitMQ] 閲嶅彂娑堟伅澶辫触锛屾嫆缁濆苟涓嶉噸鏂板叆闃燂紝queue={}, deliveryTag={}",
                    queueName, envelope.getDeliveryTag(), ex);
            safeNack(envelope.getDeliveryTag());
        }
    }

    private void safeNack(long deliveryTag) {
        try {
            channel.basicNack(deliveryTag, false, false);
        } catch (IOException ex) {
            log.error("[RabbitMQ] NACK澶辫触锛宷ueue={}, deliveryTag={}", queueName, deliveryTag, ex);
        }
    }

    private int getRetryCount(AMQP.BasicProperties properties) {
        if (properties == null || properties.getHeaders() == null) {
            return 0;
        }
        Object value = properties.getHeaders().get(HEADER_RETRY_COUNT);
        if (value instanceof Number) {
            return ((Number) value).intValue();
        }
        return 0;
    }

    private void republishWithRetryCount(byte[] body, AMQP.BasicProperties originalProperties,
                                         int newRetryCount) throws IOException {
        Map<String, Object> headers = originalProperties != null && originalProperties.getHeaders() != null
                ? new HashMap<>(originalProperties.getHeaders())
                : new HashMap<>(4);
        headers.put(HEADER_RETRY_COUNT, newRetryCount);

        AMQP.BasicProperties newProperties = new AMQP.BasicProperties.Builder()
                .headers(headers)
                .deliveryMode(originalProperties != null ? originalProperties.getDeliveryMode() : 2)
                .build();

        channel.basicPublish("", queueName, newProperties, body);
    }

    @Override
    public void stop() {
        if (!running.compareAndSet(true, false)) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            if (channel != null && channel.isOpen()) {
                channel.close();
            }
            log.info("[RabbitMQ] 璁㈤槄鑰呭凡鍋滄锛宷ueue={}", queueName);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.warn("[RabbitMQ] 鍋滄璁㈤槄鑰呮椂鍙戠敓寮傚父", e);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isRunning() {
        return running.get();
    }

    @Override
    public Object getChannel() {
        return queueName;
    }

    @Override
    public String getConsumerId() {
        return queueName;
    }

    @Override
    public int getConsumedCount() {
        return (int) consumedCount.get();
    }

    @Override
    public Throwable getLastError() {
        return lastError.get();
    }
}

=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\mq\rocket\RocketMQ.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.mq.rocket;

import com.remisoft.comm.exception.custom.BusinessException;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.queue.AbstractMessageQueue;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessagePublisher;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessageSubscriber;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.apache.rocketmq.client.producer.DefaultMQProducer;

/**
 * RocketMQ 娑堟伅闃熷垪
 *
 * <p>RocketMQ 鏄樋閲屽反宸村紑婧愮殑鍒嗗竷寮忔秷鎭腑闂翠欢锛屽叿鏈夐珮鍙敤銆侀珮鍙潬绛夌壒鐐广€?
 * 鏀寔浜嬪姟娑堟伅銆侀『搴忔秷鎭€佸欢杩熸秷鎭瓑楂樼骇鐗规€с€?
 *
 * <p><b>鎶€鏈壒鐐癸細</b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>浜嬪姟娑堟伅锛氭敮鎸佸崐娑堟伅鏈哄埗锛屽疄鐜板垎甯冨紡浜嬪姟</li>
 *   <li>寤惰繜娑堟伅锛氭敮鎸佹寚瀹氬欢杩熸椂闂村彂閫佹秷鎭?/li>
 *   <li>椤哄簭娑堟伅锛氫繚璇佸悓涓€鍒嗗尯娑堟伅鐨勬秷璐归『搴?/li>
 *   <li>娑堟伅鍥炴函锛氭敮鎸佷粠鎸囧畾鏃堕棿閲嶆柊娑堣垂</li>
 *   <li>娑堣垂缁勶細鏀寔澶氫釜娑堣垂鑰呯粍鎴愭秷璐圭粍鍗忓悓娑堣垂</li>

 * <p><b>閫傜敤鍦烘櫙锛?/b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>鍒嗗竷寮忎簨鍔★細濡傝鍗曞鐞嗐€佹敮浠樻祦绋?/li>

 *   <li>绯荤粺瑙ｈ€︼細濡傚簱瀛樺悓姝ャ€佺墿娴侀€氱煡</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>涓?Kafka 瀵规瘮锛?/b>
 * <table border="1">
 *   <tr><th>鐗规€?/th><th>RocketMQ</th><th>Kafka</th></tr>
 *   <tr><td>浜嬪姟娑堟伅</td><td>鏀寔</td><td>涓嶆敮鎸?/td></tr>
 *   <tr><td>寤惰繜娑堟伅</td><td>鏀寔</td><td>涓嶆敮鎸?/td></tr>
 *   <tr><td>椤哄簭娑堟伅</td><td>鏀寔</td><td>鏀寔</td></tr>
 *   <tr><td>娑堟伅鍥炴函</td><td>鏀寔</td><td>鏀寔</td></tr>
 *   <tr><td>鍚炲悙閲?/td><td>楂?/td><td>鏋侀珮</td></tr>
 * </table>
 *
 * <p><b>浣跨敤绀轰緥锛?/b>
 * <pre>{@code
 * IMessageQueue queue = messageQueueProvider.createMessageQueue(QueueType.rocket);
 * IMessagePublisher publisher = queue.createPublisher("my-topic");
 * IMessageSubscriber subscriber = queue.createSubscriber("my-topic");
 *
 * publisher.publish("Hello RocketMQ");
 *
 * subscriber.subscribeAsync(message -> {
 *     log.info("Received: {}", message.getBody());
 * });
 * }</pre>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
public class RocketMQ extends AbstractMessageQueue {

    private final RocketMQProperties properties;

    public RocketMQ(RocketMQProperties properties) {
        super("RocketMQ");
        if (properties == null) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("RocketMQ 閰嶇疆涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        this.properties = properties;
        validateConnection();
        log.info("[RocketMQ] 鍒濆鍖栨垚鍔燂紝namesrvAddr={}", properties.resolvedNamesrvAddr());
    }

    @Override
    public IMessagePublisher createPublisher(String channel) {
        checkNotClosed();
        if (channel == null || channel.isEmpty()) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("涓婚鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        return new RocketMQPublisher(properties, channel);
    }

    @Override
    public IMessageSubscriber createSubscriber(String channel) {
        checkNotClosed();
        if (channel == null || channel.isEmpty()) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("涓婚鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        return new RocketMQSubscriber(properties, channel);
    }

    @Override
    protected void doClose() {
    }

    private void validateConnection() {
        try {
            DefaultMQProducer probe = new DefaultMQProducer("rocketmq-connectivity-check");
            probe.setNamesrvAddr(properties.resolvedNamesrvAddr());
            probe.setSendMsgTimeout(3000);
            probe.start();
            probe.shutdown();
            log.debug("[RocketMQ] 杩炴帴楠岃瘉鎴愬姛");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[RocketMQ] 杩炴帴楠岃瘉澶辫触锛宯amesrvAddr={}", properties.resolvedNamesrvAddr(), e);
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("RocketMQ 杩炴帴澶辫触锛岃妫€鏌ラ厤缃細" + e.getMessage()).build();
        }
    }
}

package com.remisoft.comm.queue.mq.rocket;

import com.remisoft.comm.queue.config.QueueProperties;
import lombok.Data;
import lombok.EqualsAndHashCode;

/**
 * RocketMQ 娑堟伅闃熷垪閰嶇疆灞炴€?
 *
 * <p>灏佽 RocketMQ 娑堟伅闃熷垪鐨勮繛鎺ュ拰琛屼负閰嶇疆鍙傛暟銆?
 *
 * <p><b>閰嶇疆绀轰緥锛?/b>
 * <pre>{@code
 * remi:
 *   queue:
 *     rocketmq:
 *       namesrv-addr: localhost:9876
 *       group-id: remi-consumer-group
 *       topic: remi-topic
 *       access-key: your-access-key
 *       secret-key: your-secret-key
 * }</pre>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Data
@EqualsAndHashCode(callSuper = true)
public class RocketMQProperties extends QueueProperties {

    /**
     * NameServer 鍦板潃
     */
    private String namesrvAddr = "localhost:9876";

    /**
     * 娑堣垂鑰呯粍ID
     */
    private String groupId = "remi-consumer-group";

    /**
     * 榛樿涓婚
     */
    private String topic = "remi-rocketmq-topic";

    /**
     * 娑堟伅鏍囩
     */
    private String tag = "*";

    /**
     * 鎺ュ叆瀵嗛挜锛堥樋閲屼簯 MQ 浣跨敤锛?
     */
    private String accessKey;

    /**
     * 瀵嗛挜锛堥樋閲屼簯 MQ 浣跨敤锛?
     */
    private String secretKey;

    /**
     * 鏄惁鍚敤椤哄簭娑堟伅
     */
    private boolean orderly = false;

    /**
     * 娑堣垂绾跨▼鏁?
     */
    private int consumeThreadMin = 10;
    private int consumeThreadMax = 20;

    /**
     * 鎵归噺娑堣垂澶у皬
     */
    private int consumeMessageBatchMaxSize = 1;

    /**
     * 鏈€澶ч噸璇曟鏁?
     */
    private int maxRetryCount = 3;

    /**
     * 瑙ｆ瀽鑾峰彇 namesrvAddr
     */
    public String resolvedNamesrvAddr() {
        return isNotBlank(namesrvAddr) ? namesrvAddr : "localhost:9876";
    }

    /**
     * 瑙ｆ瀽鑾峰彇 groupId
     */
    public String resolvedGroupId() {
        return isNotBlank(groupId) ? groupId : "remi-consumer-group";
    }

    /**
     * 瑙ｆ瀽鑾峰彇 topic
     */
    public String resolvedTopic() {
        return isNotBlank(topic) ? topic : "remi-rocketmq-topic";
    }

    private boolean isNotBlank(String str) {
        return str != null && !str.trim().isEmpty();
    }
}

=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\mq\rocket\RocketMQPublisher.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.mq.rocket;

import com.remisoft.comm.exception.custom.BusinessException;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.domain.QueueMessage;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessagePublisher;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.apache.rocketmq.client.producer.DefaultMQProducer;
import org.apache.rocketmq.client.producer.SendCallback;
import org.apache.rocketmq.client.producer.SendResult;
import org.apache.rocketmq.client.producer.SendStatus;
import org.apache.rocketmq.common.message.Message;

import java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture;

/**
 * RocketMQ 娑堟伅鍙戝竷鑰?
 *
 * <p>浣跨敤 RocketMQ Producer API 瀹炵幇娑堟伅鍙戝竷鍔熻兘銆?
 * 鏀寔鍚屾銆佸紓姝ュ拰寤惰繜鍙戦€侊紝鎻愪緵瀹屽杽鐨勫け璐ラ噸璇曞拰鐘舵€佹牎楠屾満鍒躲€?
 *
 * <p><b>鎶€鏈壒鐐癸細</b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>鍚屾鍙戦€侊細甯﹂噸璇曟満鍒讹紝澶辫触鏃舵姏鍑?BusinessException</li>
 *   <li>寮傛鍙戦€侊細鍩轰簬 CompletableFuture锛屽洖璋冧腑鏍￠獙 SendStatus</li>
 *   <li>寤惰繜娑堟伅锛氭敮鎸佹寚瀹氬欢杩熺骇鍒殑娑堟伅</li>
 *   <li>楂樺彲闈狅細鍙戦€佸け璐ヨ嚜鍔ㄩ噸璇?3 娆★紝SendStatus 闈?SEND_OK 鏃舵姏寮傚父</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>浣跨敤绀轰緥锛?/b>
 * <pre>{@code
 * RocketMQPublisher publisher = new RocketMQPublisher(properties, "my-topic");
 * publisher.publish("Hello RocketMQ");
 * publisher.publish(QueueMessage.of("Hello"));
 * publisher.publishAsync(QueueMessage.of("Async"));
 * publisher.publishDelayed(QueueMessage.of("Delay"), 60000);
 * publisher.close();
 * }</pre>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
public class RocketMQPublisher implements IMessagePublisher {

    private static final int DEFAULT_MAX_RETRY = 3;
    private static final int DEFAULT_SEND_TIMEOUT = 5000;

    private final DefaultMQProducer producer;
    private final String topic;
    private final String tag;
    private final int maxRetryTimes;
    private final int sendTimeout;
    private volatile boolean closed = false;

    public RocketMQPublisher(RocketMQProperties properties, String topic) {
        this(properties, topic, "*");
    }

    public RocketMQPublisher(RocketMQProperties properties, String topic, String tag) {
        if (properties == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("RocketMQ 閰嶇疆涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        if (topic == null || topic.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("涓婚鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        this.topic = topic;
        this.tag = tag != null ? tag : "*";
        this.maxRetryTimes = properties.getMaxRetryCount() > 0 ? properties.getMaxRetryCount() : DEFAULT_MAX_RETRY;
        this.sendTimeout = DEFAULT_SEND_TIMEOUT;
        this.producer = createProducer(properties);
        log.info("[RocketMQ] 鍙戝竷鑰呭垵濮嬪寲瀹屾垚锛宼opic={}, tag={}, namesrvAddr={}, maxRetry={}",
                topic, this.tag, properties.resolvedNamesrvAddr(), maxRetryTimes);
    }

    @Override
    public void publish(String message) {
        if (message == null) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("娑堟伅鍐呭涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        checkNotClosed();
        try {
            QueueMessage queueMessage = QueueMessage.fromPayload(message);
            if (queueMessage == null) {
                queueMessage = QueueMessage.of(message);
            }
            publish(queueMessage);
        } catch (BusinessException e) {
            throw e;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[RocketMQ] 娑堟伅鍙戝竷澶辫触锛宼opic={}", topic, e);
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("RocketMQ 娑堟伅鍙戝竷澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage()).cause(e).build();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void publish(QueueMessage message) {
        if (message == null) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("娑堟伅涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        checkNotClosed();

        Exception lastException = null;
        for (int attempt = 0; attempt <= maxRetryTimes; attempt++) {
            try {
                String payload = QueueMessage.toPayload(message);
                Message msg = new Message(topic, tag, message.getTraceId(), payload.getBytes());
                SendResult result = producer.send(msg);
                validateSendStatus(result, message.getTraceId(), topic);
                if (log.isDebugEnabled()) {
                    log.debug("[RocketMQ] 娑堟伅宸插彂閫侊紝topic={}, traceId={}, msgId={}, status={}, attempt={}",
                            topic, message.getTraceId(), result.getMsgId(), result.getSendStatus(), attempt + 1);
                }
                return;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                lastException = e;
                if (attempt < maxRetryTimes) {
                    log.warn("[RocketMQ] 娑堟伅鍙戦€佸け璐ワ紝鍑嗗閲嶈瘯锛宼opic={}, traceId={}, attempt={}/{}, error={}",
                            topic, message.getTraceId(), attempt + 1, maxRetryTimes + 1, e.getMessage());
                    sleepBeforeRetry(attempt);
                }
            }
        }

        log.error("[RocketMQ] 娑堟伅鍙戝竷澶辫触锛屽凡閲嶈瘯 {} 娆★紝topic={}, traceId={}",
                maxRetryTimes, topic, message.getTraceId(), lastException);
        String errorMsg = lastException != null ? lastException.getMessage() : "鏈煡閿欒";
        throw BusinessException.builder().key("RocketMQ 娑堟伅鍙戝竷澶辫触锛屽凡閲嶈瘯 " + maxRetryTimes + " 娆★細" + errorMsg).cause(lastException).build();
    }

    /**
     * 寮傛鍙戦€佹秷鎭?
     *
     * <p>浣跨敤 RocketMQ 寮傛鍙戦€?API锛岃繑鍥?CompletableFuture 渚涜皟鐢ㄦ柟澶勭悊缁撴灉銆?
     * 鍥炶皟涓細鏍￠獙 SendStatus锛岄潪 SEND_OK 鏃惰褰曢敊璇棩蹇楀苟鍦?Future 涓姏鍑哄紓甯搞€?
     *
     * @param message 寰呭彂甯冪殑娑堟伅
     * @return CompletableFuture 鐢ㄤ簬寮傛鑾峰彇鍙戦€佺粨鏋?
     */
    public CompletableFuture<SendResult> publishAsync(QueueMessage message) {
        if (message == null) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("娑堟伅涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        checkNotClosed();

        CompletableFuture<SendResult> future = new CompletableFuture<>();
        try {
            String payload = QueueMessage.toPayload(message);
            Message msg = new Message(topic, tag, message.getTraceId(), payload.getBytes());
            producer.send(msg, new SendCallback() {
                @Override
                public void onSuccess(SendResult result) {
                    try {
                        validateSendStatus(result, message.getTraceId(), topic);
                        if (log.isDebugEnabled()) {
                            log.debug("[RocketMQ] 寮傛娑堟伅鍙戦€佹垚鍔燂紝topic={}, traceId={}, msgId={}, status={}",
                                    topic, message.getTraceId(), result.getMsgId(), result.getSendStatus());
                        }
                        future.complete(result);
                    } catch (Exception e) {
                        log.error("[RocketMQ] 寮傛娑堟伅鍙戦€佺姸鎬佸紓甯革紝topic={}, traceId={}, status={}",
                                topic, message.getTraceId(), result.getSendStatus(), e);
                        future.completeExceptionally(BusinessException.builder()
                                .key("RocketMQ 寮傛娑堟伅鍙戦€佺姸鎬佸紓甯革細" + e.getMessage()).cause(e).build());
                    }
                }

                @Override
                public void onException(Throwable e) {
                    log.error("[RocketMQ] 寮傛娑堟伅鍙戦€佸け璐ワ紝topic={}, traceId={}",
                            topic, message.getTraceId(), e);
                    future.completeExceptionally(BusinessException.builder()
                            .key("RocketMQ 寮傛娑堟伅鍙戦€佸け璐ワ細" + e.getMessage()).cause(e).build());
                }
            });
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[RocketMQ] 寮傛娑堟伅鍙戦€佸垵濮嬪寲澶辫触锛宼opic={}, traceId={}",
                    topic, message.getTraceId(), e);
            future.completeExceptionally(BusinessException.builder()
                    .key("RocketMQ 寮傛娑堟伅鍙戦€佸垵濮嬪寲澶辫触锛? + e.getMessage()).cause(e).build());
        }
        return future;
    }

    @Override
    public void publishDelayed(QueueMessage message, long delayMillis) {
        if (message == null) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("娑堟伅涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        if (delayMillis <= 0) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("寤惰繜鏃堕棿蹇呴』澶т簬 0").build();
        }
        checkNotClosed();

        Exception lastException = null;
        for (int attempt = 0; attempt <= maxRetryTimes; attempt++) {
            try {
                String payload = QueueMessage.toPayload(message);
                int delayLevel = calculateDelayLevel(delayMillis);
                Message msg = new Message(topic, tag, message.getTraceId(), payload.getBytes());
                msg.setDelayTimeLevel(delayLevel);
                SendResult result = producer.send(msg);
                validateSendStatus(result, message.getTraceId(), topic);
                log.debug("[RocketMQ] 寤惰繜娑堟伅宸插彂閫侊紝topic={}, traceId={}, delayLevel={}, msgId={}, status={}, attempt={}",
                        topic, message.getTraceId(), delayLevel, result.getMsgId(), result.getSendStatus(), attempt + 1);
                return;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                lastException = e;
                if (attempt < maxRetryTimes) {
                    log.warn("[RocketMQ] 寤惰繜娑堟伅鍙戦€佸け璐ワ紝鍑嗗閲嶈瘯锛宼opic={}, traceId={}, attempt={}/{}, error={}",
                            topic, message.getTraceId(), attempt + 1, maxRetryTimes + 1, e.getMessage());
                    sleepBeforeRetry(attempt);
                }
            }
        }

        log.error("[RocketMQ] 寤惰繜娑堟伅鍙戝竷澶辫触锛屽凡閲嶈瘯 {} 娆★紝topic={}, traceId={}",
                maxRetryTimes, topic, message.getTraceId(), lastException);
        String errorMsg = lastException != null ? lastException.getMessage() : "鏈煡閿欒";
        throw BusinessException.builder().key("RocketMQ 寤惰繜娑堟伅鍙戝竷澶辫触锛屽凡閲嶈瘯 " + maxRetryTimes + " 娆★細" + errorMsg).cause(lastException).build();
    }

    @Override
    public String getChannel() {
        return topic;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isActive() {
        return !closed && producer != null;
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        if (closed) {
            return;
        }
        synchronized (this) {
            if (closed) {
                return;
            }
            closed = true;
            try {
                if (producer != null) {
                    producer.shutdown();
                    log.info("[RocketMQ] 鍙戝竷鑰呭凡鍏抽棴锛宼opic={}", topic);
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.warn("[RocketMQ] 鍏抽棴鍙戝竷鑰呮椂鍙戠敓寮傚父", e);
            }
        }
    }

    private DefaultMQProducer createProducer(RocketMQProperties properties) {
        try {
            DefaultMQProducer producer = new DefaultMQProducer(properties.resolvedGroupId());
            producer.setNamesrvAddr(properties.resolvedNamesrvAddr());
            producer.setRetryTimesWhenSendFailed(maxRetryTimes);
            producer.setRetryTimesWhenSendAsyncFailed(maxRetryTimes);
            producer.setSendMsgTimeout(sendTimeout);
            producer.setMaxMessageSize(1024 * 1024 * 4);
            producer.start();
            return producer;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[RocketMQ] 鍒涘缓鐢熶骇鑰呭け璐ワ紝namesrvAddr={}", properties.resolvedNamesrvAddr(), e);
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("RocketMQ 鐢熶骇鑰呭垱寤哄け璐ワ細" + e.getMessage()).cause(e).build();
        }
    }

    /**
     * 鏍￠獙鍙戦€佺粨鏋滅姸鎬?
     *
     * <p>妫€鏌?SendResult.getSendStatus() 鏄惁涓?SEND_OK銆?
     * 闈?SEND_OK 鏃舵姏鍑哄紓甯革紝闃叉娑堟伅闈欓粯涓㈠け銆?
     */
    private void validateSendStatus(SendResult result, String traceId, String topicName) {
        if (result == null) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("RocketMQ 鍙戦€佺粨鏋滀负绌猴紝topic=" + topicName + ", traceId=" + traceId).build();
        }
        SendStatus status = result.getSendStatus();
        if (status != SendStatus.SEND_OK) {
            String errorMsg = String.format("RocketMQ 鍙戦€佺姸鎬佸紓甯革紝topic=%s, traceId=%s, msgId=%s, status=%s",
                    topicName, traceId, result.getMsgId(), status);
            log.error("[RocketMQ] {}", errorMsg);
            throw BusinessException.builder().key(errorMsg).build();
        }
    }

    /**
     * 閲嶈瘯鍓嶇瓑寰咃紝浣跨敤鎸囨暟閫€閬跨瓥鐣?
     *
     * @param attempt 褰撳墠閲嶈瘯娆℃暟锛堜粠 0 寮€濮嬶級
     */
    private void sleepBeforeRetry(int attempt) {
        long backoffMs = Math.min(100L * (1L << attempt), 2000L);
        try {
            Thread.sleep(backoffMs);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            log.warn("[RocketMQ] 閲嶈瘯绛夊緟琚腑鏂?);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 璁＄畻寤惰繜绾у埆
     *
     * <p>RocketMQ 鏀寔鐨勫欢杩熺骇鍒細
     * 1=1s, 2=5s, 3=10s, 4=30s, 5=1m, 6=2m, 7=3m, 8=4m, 9=5m, 10=6m,
     * 11=7m, 12=8m, 13=9m, 14=10m, 15=20m, 16=30m, 17=1h, 18=2h
     */
    private int calculateDelayLevel(long delayMillis) {
        if (delayMillis <= 1000) {
            return 1;
        } else if (delayMillis <= 5000) {
            return 2;
        } else if (delayMillis <= 10000) {
            return 3;
        } else if (delayMillis <= 30000) {
            return 4;
        } else if (delayMillis <= 60000) {
            return 5;
        } else if (delayMillis <= 120000) {
            return 6;
        } else if (delayMillis <= 180000) {
            return 7;
        } else if (delayMillis <= 240000) {
            return 8;
        } else if (delayMillis <= 300000) {
            return 9;
        } else if (delayMillis <= 360000) {
            return 10;
        } else if (delayMillis <= 420000) {
            return 11;
        } else if (delayMillis <= 480000) {
            return 12;
        } else if (delayMillis <= 540000) {
            return 13;
        } else if (delayMillis <= 600000) {
            return 14;
        } else if (delayMillis <= 1200000) {
            return 15;
        } else if (delayMillis <= 1800000) {
            return 16;
        } else if (delayMillis <= 3600000) {
            return 17;
        } else {
            return 18;
        }
    }

    private void checkNotClosed() {
        if (closed) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("鍙戝竷鑰呭凡鍏抽棴锛屾棤娉曠户缁搷浣?).build();
        }
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\mq\rocket\RocketMQSubscriber.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.mq.rocket;

import com.remisoft.comm.exception.custom.InfrastructureException;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.domain.QueueMessage;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.rate.ConsumerRateLimiter;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessageHandler;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessageSubscriber;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.apache.rocketmq.client.consumer.DefaultMQPushConsumer;
import org.apache.rocketmq.client.consumer.listener.ConsumeConcurrentlyStatus;
import org.apache.rocketmq.client.consumer.listener.MessageListenerConcurrently;
import org.apache.rocketmq.common.message.MessageExt;

import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference;

/**
 * RocketMQ 娑堟伅璁㈤槄鑰?
 *
 * <p>浣跨敤 RocketMQ Push Consumer API 瀹炵幇娑堟伅娑堣垂鍔熻兘銆?
 * 鏀寔骞跺彂娑堣垂鍜岄『搴忔秷璐癸紝鎻愪緵娑堟伅閲嶈瘯鍜屾淇￠槦鍒楁敮鎸併€?
 *
 * <p><b>鎶€鏈壒鐐癸細</b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>Push 妯″紡锛氭湇鍔＄鎺ㄩ€佹秷鎭紝瀹㈡埛绔鍔ㄦ帴鏀?/li>
 *   <li>骞跺彂娑堣垂锛氬绾跨▼骞跺彂澶勭悊娑堟伅</li>
 *   <li>椤哄簭娑堣垂锛氭寜娑堟伅椤哄簭渚濇澶勭悊</li>
 *   <li>娑堟伅閲嶈瘯锛氭秷璐瑰け璐ヨ嚜鍔ㄩ噸璇?/li>
 *   <li>姝讳俊闃熷垪锛氶噸璇曡秴杩囬槇鍊艰繘鍏ユ淇￠槦鍒?/li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>娑堟伅閲嶈瘯鏈哄埗锛?/b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>娑堣垂澶辫触鍚庯紝娑堟伅浼氬湪涓€瀹氭椂闂撮棿闅斿悗閲嶈瘯</li>
 *   <li>榛樿閲嶈瘯 16 娆★紝瓒呰繃鍚庤繘鍏ユ淇￠槦鍒?/li>
 *   <li>鍙€氳繃閰嶇疆璋冩暣閲嶈瘯娆℃暟鍜岄棿闅?/li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
public class RocketMQSubscriber implements IMessageSubscriber {

    private final DefaultMQPushConsumer consumer;
    private final String topic;
    private final String groupId;

    private final AtomicBoolean running;
    private final AtomicLong consumedCount;
    private final AtomicReference<Throwable> lastError;
    private final ConsumerRateLimiter rateLimiter;

    public RocketMQSubscriber(RocketMQProperties properties, String topic) {
        if (properties == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("RocketMQ 閰嶇疆涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        if (topic == null || topic.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("涓婚鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        this.topic = topic;
        this.groupId = properties.resolvedGroupId();
        this.consumer = createConsumer(properties, topic);
        this.running = new AtomicBoolean(false);
        this.consumedCount = new AtomicLong(0);
        this.lastError = new AtomicReference<>();
        this.rateLimiter = new ConsumerRateLimiter(properties.resolvedConsumerRateLimitPerSecond());
        log.info("[RocketMQ] 璁㈤槄鑰呭垵濮嬪寲瀹屾垚锛宼opic={}, groupId={}", topic, groupId);
    }

    @Override
    public String subscribe() {
        log.warn("[RocketMQ] 鍚屾鎷夊彇妯″紡鏆備笉鏀寔锛岃浣跨敤 subscribeAsync()");
        return groupId;
    }

    @Override
    public String subscribeAsync(IMessageHandler handler) {
        if (!running.compareAndSet(false, true)) {
            log.warn("[RocketMQ] 璁㈤槄鑰呭凡鍦ㄨ繍琛屼腑锛宼opic={}, groupId={}", topic, groupId);
            return groupId;
        }
        try {
            consumer.registerMessageListener((MessageListenerConcurrently) (msgs, context) -> {
                for (MessageExt msgExt : msgs) {
                    rateLimiter.acquire();
                    processMessage(msgExt, handler);
                }
                return ConsumeConcurrentlyStatus.CONSUME_SUCCESS;
            });
            consumer.start();
            log.info("[RocketMQ] 寮傛娑堣垂宸插惎鍔紝topic={}, groupId={}", topic, groupId);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            running.set(false);
            lastError.set(e);
            log.error("[RocketMQ] 鍚姩娑堣垂鑰呭け璐ワ紝topic={}, groupId={}", topic, groupId, e);
            throw new InfrastructureException("RocketMQ 娑堣垂鑰呭惎鍔ㄥけ璐ワ細" + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
        return groupId;
    }

    @Override
    public void stop() {
        if (!running.compareAndSet(true, false)) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            if (consumer != null) {
                consumer.shutdown();
                log.info("[RocketMQ] 璁㈤槄鑰呭凡鍋滄锛宼opic={}, groupId={}", topic, groupId);
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.warn("[RocketMQ] 鍋滄璁㈤槄鑰呮椂鍙戠敓寮傚父", e);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isRunning() {
        return running.get();
    }

    @Override
    public Object getChannel() {
        return topic;
    }

    @Override
    public String getConsumerId() {
        return groupId;
    }

    @Override
    public int getConsumedCount() {
        return (int) consumedCount.get();
    }

    @Override
    public Throwable getLastError() {
        return lastError.get();
    }

    private void processMessage(MessageExt msgExt, IMessageHandler handler) {
        if (msgExt == null || msgExt.getBody() == null) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            String body = new String(msgExt.getBody());
            QueueMessage message = QueueMessage.fromPayload(body);
            if (message == null) {
                message = QueueMessage.of(body);
            }
            message.setTraceId(msgExt.getKeys() != null ? msgExt.getKeys() : message.getTraceId());

            if (handler != null) {
                handler.onMessage(message);
            }
            consumedCount.incrementAndGet();
            lastError.set(null);
            log.debug("[RocketMQ] 娑堟伅澶勭悊鎴愬姛锛宼opic={}, msgId={}, traceId={}",
                    msgExt.getTopic(), msgExt.getMsgId(), message.getTraceId());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            lastError.set(e);
            log.error("[RocketMQ] 娑堟伅澶勭悊寮傚父锛宼opic={}, msgId={}",
                    msgExt.getTopic(), msgExt.getMsgId(), e);
            throw new InfrastructureException("娑堟伅澶勭悊澶辫触", e);
        }
    }

    private DefaultMQPushConsumer createConsumer(RocketMQProperties properties, String topic) {
        try {
            DefaultMQPushConsumer consumer = new DefaultMQPushConsumer(properties.resolvedGroupId());
            consumer.setNamesrvAddr(properties.resolvedNamesrvAddr());
            consumer.setConsumeThreadMin(properties.getConsumeThreadMin());
            consumer.setConsumeThreadMax(properties.getConsumeThreadMax());
            consumer.setConsumeMessageBatchMaxSize(properties.getConsumeMessageBatchMaxSize());
            consumer.setMaxReconsumeTimes(properties.getMaxRetryCount());
            consumer.subscribe(topic, properties.getTag());
            return consumer;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[RocketMQ] 鍒涘缓娑堣垂鑰呭け璐ワ紝topic={}, namesrvAddr={}",
                    topic, properties.resolvedNamesrvAddr(), e);
            throw new InfrastructureException("RocketMQ 娑堣垂鑰呭垱寤哄け璐ワ細" + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }
}

package com.remisoft.comm.queue.queue;

import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessagePublisher;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessageSubscriber;

/**
 * 娑堟伅闃熷垪鎶借薄鍩虹被
 *
 * <p>鎻愪緵娑堟伅闃熷垪鐨勯€氱敤瀹炵幇,鍖呮嫭:
 * <ul>
 *   <li>闃熷垪鍏抽棴鐘舵€佺鐞?/li>
 *   <li>鍏抽棴鐘舵€佹鏌?/li>
 *   <li>閫氱敤鐨勫叧闂€昏緫妯℃澘</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p>瀛愮被闇€瑕佸疄鐜?
 * <ul>
 *   <li>{@link #createPublisher(String)} - 鍒涘缓娑堟伅鍙戝竷鑰?/li>
 *   <li>{@link #createSubscriber(String)} - 鍒涘缓娑堟伅璁㈤槄鑰?/li>
 *   <li>{@link #doClose()} - 鎵ц瀹為檯鐨勫叧闂搷浣?/li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
public abstract class AbstractMessageQueue implements IMessageQueue {

    /** 闃熷垪绫诲瀷鎻忚堪 */
    private final String queueType;
    
    /** 闃熷垪鍏抽棴鐘舵€?*/
    private volatile boolean closed = false;

    /**
     * 鏋勯€犲嚱鏁?     *
     * @param queueType 闃熷垪绫诲瀷鎻忚堪
     */
    protected AbstractMessageQueue(String queueType) {
        this.queueType = queueType;
    }

    /**
     * 鍒涘缓娑堟伅鍙戝竷鑰?     *
     * @param channel 閫氶亾鍚嶇О
     * @return 娑堟伅鍙戝竷鑰呭疄渚?     */
    @Override
    public abstract IMessagePublisher createPublisher(String channel);

    /**
     * 鍒涘缓娑堟伅璁㈤槄鑰?     *
     * @param channel 閫氶亾鍚嶇О
     * @return 娑堟伅璁㈤槄鑰呭疄渚?     */
    @Override
    public abstract IMessageSubscriber createSubscriber(String channel);

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇闃熷垪绫诲瀷鎻忚堪
     *
     * @return 闃熷垪绫诲瀷瀛楃涓?     */
    @Override
    public String getType() {
        return queueType;
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ラ槦鍒楁槸鍚﹀凡鍏抽棴
     *
     * @return true 濡傛灉宸插叧闂?false 濡傛灉浠嶅湪浣跨敤
     */
    @Override
    public boolean isClosed() {
        return closed;
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ラ槦鍒楁槸鍚﹀凡鍏抽棴,濡傛灉宸插叧闂垯鎶涘嚭寮傚父
     *
     * @throws IllegalStateException 濡傛灉闃熷垪宸插叧闂?     */
    @Override
    public void checkNotClosed() {
        if (closed) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("闃熷垪宸插叧闂?鏃犳硶缁х画鎿嶄綔");
        }
    }

    /**
     * 鍏抽棴闃熷垪
     *
     * <p>鎵ц鍏抽棴鎿嶄綔:
     * <ol>
     *   <li>妫€鏌ユ槸鍚﹀凡鍏抽棴,閬垮厤閲嶅鍏抽棴</li>
     *   <li>鏍囪涓哄凡鍏抽棴鐘舵€?/li>
     *   <li>璋冪敤瀛愮被鐨?doClose() 鎵ц瀹為檯鍏抽棴閫昏緫</li>
     * </ol>
     */
    @Override
    public final void close() {
        if (closed) {
            return;
        }
        synchronized (this) {
            if (closed) {
                return;
            }
            closed = true;
            doClose();
        }
    }

    /**
     * 鎵ц瀹為檯鐨勫叧闂搷浣?     *
     * <p>瀛愮被闇€瑕佸疄鐜版鏂规硶,鎵ц鐗瑰畾闃熷垪绫诲瀷鐨勫叧闂€昏緫,
     * 濡傚叧闂繛鎺ャ€侀噴鏀捐祫婧愮瓑銆?     */
    protected abstract void doClose();
}

import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessagePublisher;

/**
 * 娑堟伅闃熷垪鎺ュ彛
 *
 * <p>瀹氫箟娑堟伅闃熷垪鐨勬爣鍑嗘搷浣滐紝璐熻矗鍒涘缓娑堟伅鍙戝竷鑰呭拰璁㈤槄鑰呫€?
 * 璇ユ帴鍙ｆ槸闃熷垪瀹炵幇鐨勬娊璞″眰锛屾敮鎸佸绉嶆秷鎭槦鍒楁ā寮忥紙濡?Redis List銆丳ubSub銆丼tream锛夈€?
 *
 * <p><b>浣跨敤绀轰緥锛?/b>
 * <pre>{@code
 * // 鍒涘缓闃熷垪瀹炰緥
 * IMessageQueue queue = messageQueueProvider.createMessageQueue(QueueType.stream);
 *
 * // 鍒涘缓鍙戝竷鑰呭拰璁㈤槄鑰?
 * IMessagePublisher publisher = queue.createPublisher("my-channel");
 * IMessageSubscriber subscriber = queue.createSubscriber("my-channel");
 *
 * // 鍙戝竷娑堟伅

 * subscriber.subscribe(message -> {

 * });
 *

 * queue.close();
 * }</pre>
 *

 *   <li>createPublisher: 鍒涘缓娑堟伅鍙戝竷鑰咃紝鐢ㄤ簬鍙戦€佹秷鎭埌闃熷垪</li>
 *   <li>createSubscriber: 鍒涘缓娑堟伅璁㈤槄鑰咃紝鐢ㄤ簬浠庨槦鍒楁帴鏀舵秷鎭?/li>

 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com

public interface IMessageQueue extends AutoCloseable {

    /**
     * 鍒涘缓娑堟伅鍙戝竷鑰?

     * <p>鍙戝竷鑰呰礋璐ｅ皢娑堟伅鍙戦€佸埌鎸囧畾鐨勯槦鍒楅€氶亾銆?

     * @param channel 闃熷垪閫氶亾鍚嶇О锛堝涓婚鍚嶃€侀槦鍒楀悕锛?
     * @return 娑堟伅鍙戝竷鑰呭疄渚?

    IMessagePublisher createPublisher(String channel);

    /**

     * <p>璁㈤槄鑰呰礋璐ｄ粠鎸囧畾鐨勯槦鍒楅€氶亾鎺ユ敹娑堟伅銆?
     * 璁㈤槄鑰呮敮鎸佸悓姝ユ媺鍙栧拰寮傛鐩戝惉涓ょ娑堣垂妯″紡銆?
     *
     * @param channel 闃熷垪閫氶亾鍚嶇О
     * @return 娑堟伅璁㈤槄鑰呭疄渚?
     */
    IMessageSubscriber createSubscriber(String channel);

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇闃熷垪閫氶亾鍒楄〃
     *
     * <p>杩斿洖褰撳墠闃熷垪瀹炰緥绠＄悊鐨勬墍鏈夐€氶亾鍚嶇О銆?
     * 璇ユ柟娉曚负鍙€夊疄鐜帮紝鏌愪簺闃熷垪妯″紡鍙兘涓嶆敮鎸併€?
     *
     * @return 閫氶亾鍚嶇О鏁扮粍锛屽鏋滀笉鏀寔杩斿洖绌烘暟缁?
     */
    default String[] getChannels() {
        return new String[0];
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ラ槦鍒楁槸鍚﹀凡鍏抽棴
     *
     * @return true 濡傛灉闃熷垪宸插叧闂紝false 濡傛灉闃熷垪澶勪簬娲昏穬鐘舵€?

    default boolean isClosed() {
        return false;

    /**

     *
     * @return 闃熷垪绫诲瀷鐨勬弿杩板瓧绗︿覆
     */
    default String getType() {
        return this.getClass().getSimpleName();
    }

    /**
     * 鍏抽棴闃熷垪杩炴帴
     *
     * <p>閲婃斁鎵€鏈変笌闃熷垪鐩稿叧鐨勮祫婧愶紝鍖呮嫭杩炴帴姹犮€侀€氶亾绛夈€?
     * 鍏抽棴鍚庝笉搴斿啀浣跨敤璇ラ槦鍒楀疄渚嬭繘琛屼换浣曟搷浣溿€?
     */
    default void close() {
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ラ槦鍒楁槸鍚﹀凡鍏抽棴锛屽鏋滃凡鍏抽棴鍒欐姏鍑哄紓甯?
     *
     * <p>鍦ㄦ墽琛屽彂甯冦€佽闃呯瓑鎿嶄綔鍓嶈皟鐢ㄦ鏂规硶锛?
     * 闃叉鍦ㄩ槦鍒楀叧闂悗缁х画杩涜鎿嶄綔銆?
     *
     * @throws IllegalStateException 濡傛灉闃熷垪宸插叧闂?
     */
    default void checkNotClosed() {
        if (isClosed()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("闃熷垪宸插叧闂紝鏃犳硶缁х画鎿嶄綔");
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\queue\MessageQueueFactory.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.queue;

import com.remisoft.comm.exception.custom.BusinessException;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.config.QueueProperties;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.enums.QueueType;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.mq.active.ActiveMQ;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.mq.active.ActiveMQProperties;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.mq.kafka.KafkaMQ;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.mq.kafka.KafkaQueueProperties;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.mq.rabbit.RabbitMQ;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.mq.rabbit.RabbitMQProperties;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.mq.rocket.RocketMQ;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.mq.rocket.RocketMQProperties;
import com.remisoft.comm.redis.service.RedisService;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;

import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;

/**
 * 娑堟伅闃熷垪宸ュ巶绫?
 *
 * <p>鏍规嵁闃熷垪绫诲瀷鍒涘缓瀵瑰簲鐨勬秷鎭槦鍒楀疄渚嬨€?
 * 鏀寔 Redis List銆丷edis PubSub銆丷edis Stream銆並afka銆丷ocketMQ銆丷abbitMQ銆丄ctiveMQ 绛夊绉嶆秷鎭槦鍒椼€?
 *
 * <p><b>Redis 杩炴帴澶嶇敤锛?/b>
 * Redis 闃熷垪瀹炰緥澶嶇敤 remi-comm-redis 鐨勮繛鎺ワ紝鐢?{@link RedisService} 缁熶竴绠＄悊銆?
 *
 * <p><b>绾跨▼姹犲鐢細</b>
 * 寮傛娑堣垂鑰呯粺涓€浣跨敤 Spring 绠＄悊鐨?{@link ExecutorService}锛岄伩鍏嶄笟鍔′唬鐮佺洿鎺ュ垱寤鸿８绾跨▼銆?
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
public class MessageQueueFactory implements IMessageQueueProvider {

    private final QueueProperties properties;
    private final RedisService redisService;
    private final ExecutorService consumerExecutor;

    /**
     * 鏋勯€犲嚱鏁帮紙鎺ㄨ崘锛?
     *
     * @param properties       闃熷垪閰嶇疆
     * @param redisService     Redis 鏈嶅姟锛堝彲涓?null锛屼粎浣跨敤闈?Redis 闃熷垪鏃跺厑璁革級
     * @param consumerExecutor 寮傛娑堣垂鑰呯嚎绋嬫睜锛堝彲涓?null锛屽皢閫€鍖栧埌瑁哥嚎绋嬶紝涓嶆帹鑽愶級
     */
    public MessageQueueFactory(QueueProperties properties, RedisService redisService, ExecutorService consumerExecutor) {
        if (properties == null) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("闃熷垪閰嶇疆涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        this.properties = properties;
        this.redisService = redisService;
        this.consumerExecutor = consumerExecutor;
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        log.info("MessageQueueFactory 鍏抽棴");
    }

    @Override
    public IMessageQueue createMessageQueue(QueueType type, String... args) {
        if (type == null) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("闃熷垪绫诲瀷涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        switch (type) {
            case LIST:
                return createRedisListMQ();
            case PUBSUB:
                return createRedisPubSubMQ();
            case STREAM:
                return createRedisStreamMQ();
            case KAFKA:
                return createKafkaMQ();
            case ROCKET:
                return createRocketMQ();
            case RABBIT:
                return createRabbitMQ();
            case ACTIVE:
                return createActiveMQ();
            default:
                throw BusinessException.builder().key("涓嶆敮鎸佺殑娑堟伅骞冲彴: " + type).build();
        }
    }

    private IMessageQueue createRedisListMQ() {
        if (redisService == null) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("浣跨敤 Redis 闃熷垪闇€寮曞叆 remi-comm-redis 妯″潡").build();
        }
        log.info("[Factory] 鍒涘缓 Redis List 闃熷垪锛堝鐢?remi-comm-redis 杩炴帴锛?);
        return new RedisListMQ(redisService, properties, consumerExecutor);
    }

    private IMessageQueue createRedisPubSubMQ() {
        if (redisService == null) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("浣跨敤 Redis 闃熷垪闇€寮曞叆 remi-comm-redis 妯″潡").build();
        }
        log.info("[Factory] 鍒涘缓 Redis PubSub 闃熷垪锛堝鐢?remi-comm-redis 杩炴帴锛?);
        return new RedisPubSubMQ(redisService, properties);
    }

    private IMessageQueue createRedisStreamMQ() {
        if (redisService == null) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("浣跨敤 Redis 闃熷垪闇€寮曞叆 remi-comm-redis 妯″潡").build();
        }
        log.info("[Factory] 鍒涘缓 Redis Stream 闃熷垪锛堝鐢?remi-comm-redis 杩炴帴锛?);
        return new RedisStreamMQ(redisService, properties, consumerExecutor);
    }

    private IMessageQueue createKafkaMQ() {
        log.info("[Factory] 鍒涘缓 Kafka 闃熷垪");
        KafkaQueueProperties kafkaProperties = extractKafkaProperties();
        return new KafkaMQ(kafkaProperties, consumerExecutor);
    }

    private IMessageQueue createRocketMQ() {
        log.info("[Factory] 鍒涘缓 RocketMQ 闃熷垪");
        RocketMQProperties rocketProperties = extractRocketMQProperties();
        return new RocketMQ(rocketProperties);
    }

    private IMessageQueue createRabbitMQ() {
        log.info("[Factory] 鍒涘缓 RabbitMQ 闃熷垪");
        RabbitMQProperties rabbitProperties = extractRabbitMQProperties();
        return new RabbitMQ(rabbitProperties);
    }

    private IMessageQueue createActiveMQ() {
        log.info("[Factory] 鍒涘缓 ActiveMQ 闃熷垪");
        ActiveMQProperties activeProperties = extractActiveMQProperties();
        return new ActiveMQ(activeProperties, consumerExecutor);
    }

    private KafkaQueueProperties extractKafkaProperties() {
        KafkaQueueProperties kafkaProperties = new KafkaQueueProperties();
        kafkaProperties.setBootstrapServers(properties.resolvedHost() + ":" + properties.resolvedPort());
        kafkaProperties.setGroupId(properties.getStreamGroup());
        kafkaProperties.setTopic(properties.getStreamConsumer());
        kafkaProperties.setEnableAutoCommit(false);
        kafkaProperties.setAutoOffsetReset("earliest");
        kafkaProperties.setMaxPollRecords(properties.getStreamBatchSize());
        return kafkaProperties;
    }

    private RocketMQProperties extractRocketMQProperties() {
        RocketMQProperties rocketProperties = new RocketMQProperties();
        rocketProperties.setNamesrvAddr(properties.resolvedHost() + ":" + properties.resolvedPort());
        rocketProperties.setGroupId(properties.getStreamGroup());
        rocketProperties.setTopic(properties.getStreamConsumer());
        return rocketProperties;
    }

    private RabbitMQProperties extractRabbitMQProperties() {
        RabbitMQProperties rabbitProperties = new RabbitMQProperties();
        rabbitProperties.setHost(properties.resolvedHost());
        rabbitProperties.setRabbitPort(properties.resolvedPort());
        rabbitProperties.setUsername(properties.getUsername());
        rabbitProperties.setPassword(properties.resolvedPassword());
        rabbitProperties.setQueueName(properties.getStreamConsumer());
        return rabbitProperties;
    }

    private ActiveMQProperties extractActiveMQProperties() {
        ActiveMQProperties activeProperties = new ActiveMQProperties();
        activeProperties.setBrokerUrl("tcp://" + properties.resolvedHost() + ":" + properties.resolvedPort());
        activeProperties.setUsername(properties.getUsername());
        activeProperties.setPassword(properties.resolvedPassword());
        activeProperties.setQueueName(properties.getStreamConsumer());
        return activeProperties;
    }
}

package com.remisoft.comm.queue.queue;

import com.remisoft.comm.exception.custom.BusinessException;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.config.QueueProperties;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessagePublisher;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessageSubscriber;
