import com.remisoft.comm.redis.service.RedisService;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisTemplate;

import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;

/**
 * 鍩轰簬 Redis List 瀹炵幇鐨勬秷鎭槦鍒?
 *
 * <p>Redis List 闃熷垪鏄渶杞婚噺鐨勬秷鎭槦鍒楀疄鐜帮紝鍩轰簬 LPUSH/BRPOP 鍛戒护瀹炵幇 FIFO锛堝厛杩涘厛鍑猴級璇箟銆?
 * 閫傜敤浜庣畝鍗曠殑浠诲姟闃熷垪鍦烘櫙锛屼笉鏀寔娑堟伅纭銆侀噸璇曘€佹淇＄瓑楂樼骇鐗规€с€?
 *
 * <p><b>杩炴帴澶嶇敤锛?/b>閫氳繃 {@link RedisService} 澶嶇敤 remi-comm-redis 鐨勮繛鎺ャ€?
 *
 * <p><b>鐢熶骇鐜璀﹀憡锛?/b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>Redis List 涓嶅叿澶囨秷鎭寔涔呭寲纭鏈哄埗锛宐roker 瀹曟満鍙兘涓㈠け鏈秷璐规秷鎭?/li>
 *   <li>涓嶆敮鎸佹秷鎭?ACK / 閲嶈瘯 / 姝讳俊闃熷垪锛屼笉閫傚悎鍏抽敭涓氬姟鍦烘櫙</li>
 *   <li>鐢熶骇鐜鍏抽敭涓氬姟璇蜂娇鐢?Kafka / RocketMQ / RabbitMQ 绛変笓涓氭秷鎭腑闂翠欢</li>
 *   <li>浠呴€傜敤浜庤交閲忕骇浠诲姟闃熷垪銆佷复鏃剁紦鍐茬瓑闈炲叧閿満鏅?/li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
public class RedisListMQ implements IMessageQueue {

    private final QueueProperties queueProperties;
    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final ExecutorService consumerExecutor;
    private volatile boolean closed = false;

    /**
     * 鍩轰簬 RedisService 鏋勯€狅紙澶嶇敤 remi-comm-redis 杩炴帴锛屾帹鑽愶級
     *
     * @param redisService     Redis 鏈嶅姟
     * @param config           闃熷垪閰嶇疆
     * @param consumerExecutor 寮傛娑堣垂鑰呯嚎绋嬫睜锛堝彲涓?null锛屽皢閫€鍖栧埌瑁哥嚎绋嬶紝涓嶆帹鑽愶級
     */
    public RedisListMQ(RedisService redisService, QueueProperties config, ExecutorService consumerExecutor) {
        if (config == null) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("闃熷垪閰嶇疆涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        this.queueProperties = config;
        this.redisTemplate = redisService.getRedisTemplate();
        this.consumerExecutor = consumerExecutor;
        log.info("[RedisListMQ] 鍒濆鍖栨垚鍔燂紙澶嶇敤 remi-comm-redis 杩炴帴锛?);
        log.warn("[RedisListMQ] 璀﹀憡: Redis List 闃熷垪涓嶉€傚悎鐢熶骇鐜鍏抽敭涓氬姟鍦烘櫙锛? +
                "涓嶅叿澶囨秷鎭?ACK/閲嶈瘯/姝讳俊鑳藉姏锛屽叧閿笟鍔¤浣跨敤 Kafka/RocketMQ/RabbitMQ");
    }

    @Override
    public IMessagePublisher createPublisher(String channel) {
        checkNotClosed();
        if (channel == null || channel.isEmpty()) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("閫氶亾鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        return new RedisListPublisher(redisTemplate, channel);
    }

    @Override
    public IMessageSubscriber createSubscriber(String channel) {
        checkNotClosed();
        if (channel == null || channel.isEmpty()) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("閫氶亾鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        return new RedisListSubscriber(redisTemplate, channel,
                (int) queueProperties.resolvedListBlockTimeoutSeconds(), 10000, queueProperties, consumerExecutor);
    }

    @Override
    public String[] getChannels() {
        return new String[0];
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isClosed() {
        return closed;
    }

    @Override
    public String getType() {
        return "Redis-List";
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        if (closed) {
            return;
        }
        synchronized (this) {
            if (closed) {
                return;
            }
            closed = true;
            // RedisTemplate 鐢?remi-comm-redis 绠＄悊锛屾棤闇€鍏抽棴
            log.info("[Redis-List] 闃熷垪宸插叧闂?);
        }
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\queue\RedisPubSubMQ.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.queue;

import com.remisoft.comm.exception.custom.BusinessException;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.config.QueueProperties;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessagePublisher;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessageSubscriber;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.impl.RedisPubSubPublisher;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.impl.RedisPubSubSubscriber;
import com.remisoft.comm.redis.service.RedisService;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisTemplate;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentMap;

/**
 * 鍩轰簬 Redis PubSub 瀹炵幇鐨勬秷鎭槦鍒?
 *
 * <p>Redis PubSub锛堝彂甯?璁㈤槄锛夋槸涓€绉嶅箍鎾ā寮忕殑娑堟伅閫氫俊鏈哄埗銆?
 * 鍙戝竷鑰呭皢娑堟伅鍙戦€佸埌鎸囧畾棰戦亾锛屾墍鏈夎闃呰棰戦亾鐨勮闃呰€呴兘鑳芥敹鍒版秷鎭€?
 *
 * <p><b>杩炴帴澶嶇敤锛?/b>閫氳繃 {@link RedisService} 澶嶇敤 remi-comm-redis 鐨勮繛鎺ャ€?
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
public class RedisPubSubMQ implements IMessageQueue {

    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final QueueProperties config;
    private final ConcurrentMap<String, RedisPubSubSubscriber> subscribers;
    private volatile boolean closed = false;

    /**
     * 鍩轰簬 RedisService 鏋勯€狅紙澶嶇敤 remi-comm-redis 杩炴帴锛?
     *
     * @param redisService Redis 鏈嶅姟
     * @param config       闃熷垪閰嶇疆
     */
    public RedisPubSubMQ(RedisService redisService, QueueProperties config) {
        if (config == null) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("闃熷垪閰嶇疆涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        this.config = config;
        this.subscribers = new ConcurrentHashMap<>(4);
        this.redisTemplate = redisService.getRedisTemplate();
        log.info("[RedisPubSubMQ] 鍒濆鍖栨垚鍔燂紙澶嶇敤 remi-comm-redis 杩炴帴锛?);
    }

    @Override
    public IMessagePublisher createPublisher(String channel) {
        checkNotClosed();
        if (channel == null || channel.isEmpty()) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("閫氶亾鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        return new RedisPubSubPublisher(redisTemplate, channel);
    }

    @Override
    public IMessageSubscriber createSubscriber(String channel) {
        checkNotClosed();
        if (channel == null || channel.isEmpty()) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("閫氶亾鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        return new RedisPubSubSubscriber(redisTemplate, channel, this, config);
    }

    @Override
    public String[] getChannels() {
        return subscribers.keySet().toArray(new String[0]);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isClosed() {
        return closed;
    }

    @Override
    public String getType() {
        return "Redis-PubSub";
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        if (closed) {
            return;
        }
        synchronized (this) {
            if (closed) {
                return;
            }
            closed = true;
            shutdownSubscribers();
            // RedisTemplate 鐢?remi-comm-redis 绠＄悊锛屾棤闇€鍏抽棴
            log.info("[Redis-PubSub] 闃熷垪宸插叧闂?);
        }
    }

    public void registerSubscriber(String subscriberId, RedisPubSubSubscriber subscriber) {
        if (subscriberId != null && subscriber != null) {
            subscribers.put(subscriberId, subscriber);
            log.debug("[RedisPubSubMQ] 璁㈤槄鑰呭凡娉ㄥ唽锛宻ubscriberId={}", subscriberId);
        }
    }

    public void removeSubscriber(String subscriberId) {
        if (subscriberId != null) {
            subscribers.remove(subscriberId);
            log.debug("[RedisPubSubMQ] 璁㈤槄鑰呭凡绉婚櫎锛宻ubscriberId={}", subscriberId);
        }
    }

    private void shutdownSubscribers() {
        for (Map.Entry<String, RedisPubSubSubscriber> entry : subscribers.entrySet()) {
            try {
                entry.getValue().shutdown();
                log.debug("[RedisPubSubMQ] 璁㈤槄鑰呭凡鍏抽棴锛宻ubscriberId={}", entry.getKey());
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.warn("[RedisPubSubMQ] 鍏抽棴璁㈤槄鑰呮椂鍙戠敓寮傚父锛宻ubscriberId={}", entry.getKey(), e);
            }
        }
        subscribers.clear();
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\queue\RedisStreamMQ.java ===

import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessagePublisher;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessageSubscriber;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.impl.RedisStreamPublisher;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.impl.RedisStreamSubscriber;
import com.remisoft.comm.redis.service.RedisService;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisTemplate;

import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;

/**
 * 鍩轰簬 Redis Stream 瀹炵幇鐨勬秷鎭槦鍒?
 *
 * <p>Redis Stream 鏄?Redis 5.0 寮曞叆鐨勬暟鎹粨鏋勶紝鎻愪緵浜嗘洿寮哄ぇ鐨勬秷鎭寔涔呭寲鍜屾秷璐圭粍鍔熻兘銆?
 * 瀹冩槸 List 鐨勫寮虹増锛屾敮鎸佹秷鎭‘璁ゃ€佹秷璐圭粍銆佹秷鎭疘D鎺掑簭绛夐珮绾х壒鎬с€?
 *
 * <p><b>杩炴帴澶嶇敤锛?/b>閫氳繃 {@link RedisService} 澶嶇敤 remi-comm-redis 鐨勮繛鎺ャ€?
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
public class RedisStreamMQ implements IMessageQueue {

    private final QueueProperties queueProperties;
    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final ExecutorService consumerExecutor;
    private volatile boolean closed = false;

    /**
     * 鍩轰簬 RedisService 鏋勯€狅紙澶嶇敤 remi-comm-redis 杩炴帴锛屾帹鑽愶級
     *
     * @param redisService     Redis 鏈嶅姟
     * @param config           闃熷垪閰嶇疆
     * @param consumerExecutor 寮傛娑堣垂鑰呯嚎绋嬫睜锛堝彲涓?null锛屽皢閫€鍖栧埌瑁哥嚎绋嬶紝涓嶆帹鑽愶級
     */
    public RedisStreamMQ(RedisService redisService, QueueProperties config, ExecutorService consumerExecutor) {
        if (config == null) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("闃熷垪閰嶇疆涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        this.queueProperties = config;
        this.redisTemplate = redisService.getRedisTemplate();
        this.consumerExecutor = consumerExecutor;
        log.info("[RedisStreamMQ] 鍒濆鍖栨垚鍔燂紙澶嶇敤 remi-comm-redis 杩炴帴锛夛紝娑堣垂鑰呯粍: {}",
                config.resolvedStreamGroup());
    }

    @Override
    public IMessagePublisher createPublisher(String channel) {
        checkNotClosed();
        if (channel == null || channel.isEmpty()) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("閫氶亾鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        return new RedisStreamPublisher(redisTemplate, channel);
    }

    @Override
    public IMessageSubscriber createSubscriber(String channel) {
        checkNotClosed();
        if (channel == null || channel.isEmpty()) {
            throw BusinessException.builder().key("閫氶亾鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖").build();
        }
        return new RedisStreamSubscriber(redisTemplate, channel, queueProperties, consumerExecutor);
    }

    @Override
    public String[] getChannels() {
        return new String[0];
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isClosed() {
        return closed;
    }

    @Override
    public String getType() {
        return "Redis-Stream";
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        if (closed) {
            return;
        }
        synchronized (this) {
            if (closed) {
                return;
            }
            closed = true;
            // RedisTemplate 鐢?remi-comm-redis 绠＄悊锛屾棤闇€鍏抽棴
            log.info("[Redis-Stream] 闃熷垪宸插叧闂?);
        }
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\rate\ConsumerRateLimiter.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.rate;

import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;

import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantLock;

/**
 * 鍩轰簬浠ょ墝妗剁殑娑堣垂鑰呴檺娴佸櫒
 *
 * <p>浣跨敤浠ょ墝妗剁畻娉曟帶鍒舵秷鎭秷璐归€熺巼锛岄槻姝笅娓哥郴缁熻绐佸彂娴侀噺鍑诲灝銆?
 * 姣忎釜娑堣垂鑰呭疄渚嬫寔鏈夌嫭绔嬬殑闄愭祦鍣紝鏀寔鍔ㄦ€佽皟鏁撮檺娴侀€熺巼銆?
 *
 * <p><b>绠楁硶鍘熺悊锛?/b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>浠ょ墝浠ュ浐瀹氶€熺巼鐢熸垚骞舵斁鍏ユ《涓?/li>
 *   <li>姣忔潯娑堟伅娑堣垂鍓嶉渶鑾峰彇涓€涓护鐗?/li>
 *   <li>妗舵弧鏃跺浣欑殑浠ょ墝琚涪寮?/li>
 *   <li>妗剁┖鏃舵秷璐硅€呯瓑寰呯洿鍒版湁浠ょ墝鍙敤</li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>浣跨敤绀轰緥锛?/b>
 * <pre>{@code
 * ConsumerRateLimiter limiter = new ConsumerRateLimiter(100); // 姣忕 100 鏉?
 * // 鍦ㄦ秷璐瑰惊鐜腑
 * limiter.acquire(); // 闃诲鐩村埌鑾峰彇浠ょ墝
 * processMessage(message);
 * }</pre>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
public class ConsumerRateLimiter {

    /**
     * 闄愭祦閫熺巼锛堟瘡绉掍护鐗屾暟锛?
     */
    private final double permitsPerSecond;

    /**
     * 妗跺閲忥紙鏈€澶т护鐗屾暟锛?
     */
    private final double maxTokens;

    /**
     * 褰撳墠浠ょ墝鏁?
     */
    private double tokens;

    /**
     * 涓婃琛ュ厖浠ょ墝鐨勬椂闂存埑锛堢撼绉掞級
     */
    private long lastRefillTime;

    /**
     * 绾跨▼瀹夊叏閿?
     */
    private final ReentrantLock lock = new ReentrantLock();

    /**
     * 鏄惁鍚敤闄愭祦
     */
    private final boolean enabled;

    /**
     * 鍒涘缓闄愭祦鍣?
     *
     * @param permitsPerSecond 姣忕鍏佽閫氳繃鐨勪护鐗屾暟锛?= 0 琛ㄧず涓嶉檺娴?
     */
    public ConsumerRateLimiter(int permitsPerSecond) {
        if (permitsPerSecond <= 0) {
            this.permitsPerSecond = 0;
            this.maxTokens = 0;
            this.tokens = 0;
            this.enabled = false;
        } else {
            this.permitsPerSecond = permitsPerSecond;
            // 妗跺閲?= 姣忕閫熺巼锛屽厑璁哥煭鏃堕棿鍐呯殑绐佸彂
            this.maxTokens = permitsPerSecond;
            this.tokens = maxTokens;
            this.lastRefillTime = System.nanoTime();
            this.enabled = true;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇浠ょ墝锛屽鏋滄《涓病鏈変护鐗屽垯闃诲绛夊緟
     *
     * <p>姝ゆ柟娉曚細闃诲褰撳墠绾跨▼鐩村埌鑾峰彇鍒颁竴涓护鐗屼负姝€?
     * 濡傛灉闄愭祦鍣ㄦ湭鍚敤锛坧ermitsPerSecond <= 0锛夛紝鍒欑珛鍗宠繑鍥炪€?
     *
     * <p><b>绾跨▼涓柇澶勭悊锛?/b>濡傛灉绛夊緟鏈熼棿绾跨▼琚腑鏂紝浼氳缃腑鏂爣蹇楀苟绔嬪嵆杩斿洖锛?
     * 涓嶄細鎶涘嚭 IllegalMonitorStateException銆?
     */
    public void acquire() {
        if (!enabled) {
            return;
        }
        lock.lock();
        try {
            while (true) {
                refillTokens();
                if (tokens >= 1.0) {
                    tokens -= 1.0;
                    return;
                }
                // 璁＄畻闇€瑕佺瓑寰呯殑鏃堕棿
                double deficit = 1.0 - tokens;
                long waitNanos = (long) (deficit / permitsPerSecond * TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(1));
                if (waitNanos <= 0) {
                    waitNanos = 1;
                }
                // 閲婃斁閿佸悗 sleep锛岄伩鍏嶆寔鏈夐攣闃诲鍏朵粬绾跨▼
                lock.unlock();
                try {
                    Thread.sleep(TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.toMillis(waitNanos),
                            (int) (waitNanos % TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS.toNanos(1)));
                } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                    // sleep 琚腑鏂椂锛屽綋鍓嶇嚎绋嬪凡涓嶆寔鏈夐攣锛岀洿鎺ヨ缃腑鏂爣蹇楀苟杩斿洖
                    // 涓嶄細杩涘叆 finally 鍧楃殑 lock.unlock()锛堝洜涓洪攣宸插湪涓婇潰閲婃斁锛?
                    Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
                    // 閲嶆柊鑾峰彇閿佷互杩涘叆 finally 鍧楋紝閬垮厤 finally 涓?unlock 鎶?IllegalMonitorStateException
                    lock.lock();
                    return;
                }
                // sleep 缁撴潫鍚庨噸鏂拌幏鍙栭攣缁х画寰幆
                lock.lock();
            }
        } finally {
            // 鍙湁鍦ㄦ寔鏈夐攣鏃舵墠閲婃斁锛岄伩鍏?IllegalMonitorStateException
            if (lock.isHeldByCurrentThread()) {
                lock.unlock();
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * 灏濊瘯鑾峰彇浠ょ墝锛屼笉闃诲
     *
     * @return true 琛ㄧず鑾峰彇鎴愬姛锛宖alse 琛ㄧず褰撳墠鏃犲彲鐢ㄤ护鐗?
     */
    public boolean tryAcquire() {
        if (!enabled) {
            return true;
        }
        lock.lock();
        try {
            refillTokens();
            if (tokens >= 1.0) {
                tokens -= 1.0;
                return true;
            }
            return false;
        } finally {
            lock.unlock();
        }
    }

    /**
     * 琛ュ厖浠ょ墝
     *
     * 鏍规嵁褰撳墠鏃堕棿涓庝笂娆¤ˉ鍏呮椂闂寸殑宸€硷紝璁＄畻搴旇鏂板鐨勪护鐗屾暟銆?
     */
    private void refillTokens() {
        long now = System.nanoTime();
        long elapsedNanos = now - lastRefillTime;
        double tokensToAdd = elapsedNanos * permitsPerSecond / TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(1);
        if (tokensToAdd > 0) {
            tokens = Math.min(maxTokens, tokens + tokensToAdd);
            lastRefillTime = now;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇褰撳墠鍙敤浠ょ墝鏁帮紙浠呯敤浜庣洃鎺э級
     *
     * @return 鍙敤浠ょ墝鏁?
     */
    public double getAvailableTokens() {
        lock.lock();
        try {
            refillTokens();
            return tokens;
        } finally {
            lock.unlock();
        }
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇閰嶇疆鐨勯檺娴侀€熺巼
     *
     * @return 姣忕浠ょ墝鏁?
     */
    public double getPermitsPerSecond() {
        return permitsPerSecond;
    }

    /**
     * 鍒ゆ柇闄愭祦鍣ㄦ槸鍚﹀惎鐢?
     *
     * @return 鏄惁鍚敤
     */
    public boolean isEnabled() {
        return enabled;
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\recovery\ConsumerThreadGuard.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.recovery;

import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;

import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Future;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;

/**
 * 娑堣垂绾跨▼宕╂簝鎭㈠瀹堝崼
 *
 * <p>鐩戞帶娑堣垂鑰呬换鍔¤繍琛岀姸鎬侊紝褰撲换鍔″洜寮傚父缁堟鏃惰嚜鍔ㄩ噸鍚€?
 * 鏀寔鏈€澶ч噸鍚鏁伴檺鍒讹紝瓒呰繃闃堝€煎悗瑙﹀彂鍛婅骞跺仠姝㈡仮澶嶃€?
 *
 * <p><b>绾跨▼姹犳ā寮忥紙鎺ㄨ崘锛夛細</b>
 * 褰撲紶鍏?{@link ExecutorService} 鏃讹紝浠诲姟鐢?Spring 绠＄悊鐨勭嚎绋嬫睜鎵ц锛?
 * 閬垮厤鐩存帴鍒涘缓瑁哥嚎绋嬶紝鏀寔浼橀泤鍋滄満銆?
 *
 * <p><b>瑁哥嚎绋嬫ā寮忥紙宸插純鐢級锛?/b>
 * 褰撴湭浼犲叆 {@link ExecutorService} 鏃讹紝鍐呴儴浼氬垱寤哄畧鎶ょ嚎绋嬫墽琛屼换鍔★紝
 * 浠呯敤浜庡悜鍚庡吋瀹癸紝涓嶆帹鑽愬湪鏂颁唬鐮佷腑浣跨敤銆?
 *
 * <p><b>浣跨敤绀轰緥锛?/b>
 * <pre>{@code
 * ConsumerThreadGuard guard = new ConsumerThreadGuard("redis-stream", 10, executor);
 * guard.start(() -> consumeLoop(handler));
 *
 * guard.stop();
 * }</pre>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
public class ConsumerThreadGuard {

    private static final long DEFAULT_RESTART_BACKOFF_MS = 2000L;
    private static final long MAX_BACKOFF_MS = 30000L;

    private final String name;
    private final int maxRestarts;
    private final ExecutorService executor;
    private final AtomicBoolean running;
    private final AtomicInteger restartCount;
    private volatile Future<?> currentFuture;
    private volatile Thread fallbackThread;

    /**
     * 鍒涘缓娑堣垂鑰呯嚎绋嬪畧鍗紙鎺ㄨ崘锛屼娇鐢?Spring 绠＄悊鐨勭嚎绋嬫睜锛?
     *
     * @param name         瀹堝崼鍚嶇О锛岀敤浜庢棩蹇楁爣璇?
     * @param maxRestarts  鏈€澶ч噸鍚鏁?
     * @param executor     寮傛浠诲姟鎵ц鍣?
     */
    public ConsumerThreadGuard(String name, int maxRestarts, ExecutorService executor) {
        if (name == null || name.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("瀹堝崼鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        if (maxRestarts <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("鏈€澶ч噸鍚鏁板繀椤诲ぇ浜?");
        }
        this.name = name;
        this.maxRestarts = maxRestarts;
        this.executor = executor;
        this.running = new AtomicBoolean(false);
        this.restartCount = new AtomicInteger(0);
    }

    /**
     * 鍚姩娑堣垂鑰呬换鍔″苟寮€鍚洃鎺?
     *
     * @param task 娑堣垂鑰呬换鍔★紝閫氬父鍐呴儴鍖呭惈寰幆娑堣垂閫昏緫
     */
    public void start(Runnable task) {
        if (!running.compareAndSet(false, true)) {
            log.warn("[ConsumerGuard] 娑堣垂鑰呭凡鍦ㄨ繍琛岋紝name={}", name);
            return;
        }
        restartCount.set(0);
        launch(task);
    }

    /**
     * 鍋滄娑堣垂鑰呬换鍔?
     */
    public void stop() {
        if (!running.compareAndSet(true, false)) {
            return;
        }
        if (currentFuture != null) {
            currentFuture.cancel(true);
        }
        if (fallbackThread != null && fallbackThread.isAlive()) {
            fallbackThread.interrupt();
        }
        log.info("[ConsumerGuard] 娑堣垂鑰呭凡鍋滄锛宯ame={}, totalRestarts={}", name, restartCount.get());
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇褰撳墠杩愯鐘舵€?
     */
    public boolean isRunning() {
        return running.get();
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇宸查噸鍚鏁?
     */
    public int getRestartCount() {
        return restartCount.get();
    }

    private void launch(Runnable task) {
        if (executor != null) {
            currentFuture = executor.submit(() -> runWithRecovery(task));
            log.info("[ConsumerGuard] 娑堣垂鑰呬换鍔″凡鎻愪氦鍒扮嚎绋嬫睜锛宯ame={}", name);
        } else {
            Thread t = new Thread(() -> runWithRecovery(task), "remi-queue-guard-" + name);
            t.setDaemon(true);
            fallbackThread = t;
            t.start();
            log.warn("[ConsumerGuard] 娑堣垂鑰呬换鍔′娇鐢ㄨ８绾跨▼杩愯锛宯ame={}锛堜笉鎺ㄨ崘锛?, name);
        }
    }

    private void runWithRecovery(Runnable task) {
        while (running.get()) {
            try {
                task.run();

                if (!running.get()) {
                    return;
                }

                // 浠诲姟姝ｅ父缁撴潫浣嗕粛鍦ㄨ繍琛岋紝鎸夊紓甯搁€€鍑哄鐞嗗苟閲嶅惎
                int count = restartCount.incrementAndGet();
                if (count > maxRestarts) {
                    log.error("[ConsumerGuard] 瓒呰繃鏈€澶ч噸鍚鏁帮紝鍋滄鎭㈠锛宯ame={}, maxRestarts={}", name, maxRestarts);
                    running.set(false);
                    return;
                }

                long backoff = calcBackoff(count);
                log.warn("[ConsumerGuard] 娑堣垂鑰呬换鍔℃甯搁€€鍑猴紝鍑嗗绗瑊}娆￠噸鍚紝name={}, 寤惰繜={}ms", count, name, backoff);
                sleepQuietly(backoff);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                if (!running.get()) {
                    return;
                }

                log.error("[ConsumerGuard] 娑堣垂鑰呬换鍔″紓甯革紝name={}", name, e);
                int count = restartCount.incrementAndGet();
                if (count > maxRestarts) {
                    log.error("[ConsumerGuard] 瓒呰繃鏈€澶ч噸鍚鏁帮紝鍋滄鎭㈠锛宯ame={}, maxRestarts={}", name, maxRestarts);
                    running.set(false);
                    return;
                }

                long backoff = calcBackoff(count);
                log.warn("[ConsumerGuard] 娑堣垂鑰呬换鍔″紓甯革紝鍑嗗绗瑊}娆￠噸鍚紝name={}, 寤惰繜={}ms", count, name, backoff);
                sleepQuietly(backoff);
            }
        }
    }

    private long calcBackoff(int restartCount) {
        long backoff = DEFAULT_RESTART_BACKOFF_MS * (long) Math.pow(2, Math.min(restartCount - 1, 5));
        return Math.min(backoff, MAX_BACKOFF_MS);
    }

    private void sleepQuietly(long millis) {
        try {
            Thread.sleep(millis);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\retry\RetryPolicy.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.retry;

/**
 * 缁熶竴閲嶈瘯绛栫暐鎺ュ彛
 *
 * <p>瀹氫箟娑堟伅闃熷垪娑堣垂澶辫触鏃剁殑閲嶈瘯绛栫暐銆?
 * 鏀寔鎸囨暟閫€閬裤€佸浐瀹氶棿闅斻€佺嚎鎬у闀跨瓑澶氱閲嶈瘯绛栫暐銆?
 *
 * <p><b>浣跨敤绀轰緥锛?/b>
 * <pre>{@code
 * // 鎸囨暟閫€閬块噸璇曪紙鏈€澶?3 娆★紝鍒濆 1s锛屾渶澶?30s锛?
 * RetryPolicy policy = RetryPolicy.exponentialBackoff(3, 1000, 30000);
 *
 * // 鍥哄畾闂撮殧閲嶈瘯锛堟渶澶?5 娆★紝姣忔闂撮殧 2s锛?
 * RetryPolicy policy = RetryPolicy.fixedInterval(5, 2000);
 *
 * // 妫€鏌ユ槸鍚﹀彲浠ラ噸璇?
 * if (policy.canRetry(attempt)) {
 *     long delay = policy.getDelayMillis(attempt);
 *     Thread.sleep(delay);
 * }
 * }</pre>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
public interface RetryPolicy {

    /**
     * 鍒ゆ柇鏄惁鍙互缁х画閲嶈瘯
     *
     * @param attempt 褰撳墠閲嶈瘯娆℃暟锛堜粠 0 寮€濮嬶紝0 琛ㄧず棣栨璋冪敤锛?
     * @return true 琛ㄧず鍙互缁х画閲嶈瘯锛宖alse 琛ㄧず宸茶揪鏈€澶ч噸璇曟鏁?
     */
    boolean canRetry(int attempt);

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇涓嬫閲嶈瘯鐨勫欢杩熸椂闂达紙姣锛?
     *
     * @param attempt 褰撳墠閲嶈瘯娆℃暟锛堜粠 0 寮€濮嬶級
     * @return 寤惰繜鏃堕棿锛堟绉掞級
     */
    long getDelayMillis(int attempt);

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇鏈€澶ч噸璇曟鏁?
     *
     * @return 鏈€澶ч噸璇曟鏁?
     */
    int getMaxAttempts();

    /**
     * 鍒涘缓鎸囨暟閫€閬块噸璇曠瓥鐣?
     *
     * @param maxAttempts     鏈€澶ч噸璇曟鏁?
     * @param initialDelayMs  鍒濆寤惰繜锛堟绉掞級
     * @param maxDelayMs      鏈€澶у欢杩燂紙姣锛?
     * @return 鎸囨暟閫€閬块噸璇曠瓥鐣?
     */
    static RetryPolicy exponentialBackoff(int maxAttempts, long initialDelayMs, long maxDelayMs) {
        return new ExponentialBackoffRetryPolicy(maxAttempts, initialDelayMs, maxDelayMs);
    }

    /**
     * 鍒涘缓鍥哄畾闂撮殧閲嶈瘯绛栫暐
     *
     * @param maxAttempts 鏈€澶ч噸璇曟鏁?
     * @param intervalMs  鍥哄畾闂撮殧锛堟绉掞級
     * @return 鍥哄畾闂撮殧閲嶈瘯绛栫暐
     */
    static RetryPolicy fixedInterval(int maxAttempts, long intervalMs) {
        return new FixedIntervalRetryPolicy(maxAttempts, intervalMs);
    }

    /**
     * 鎸囨暟閫€閬块噸璇曠瓥鐣ュ疄鐜?
     */
    class ExponentialBackoffRetryPolicy implements RetryPolicy {
        private final int maxAttempts;
        private final long initialDelayMs;
        private final long maxDelayMs;

        ExponentialBackoffRetryPolicy(int maxAttempts, long initialDelayMs, long maxDelayMs) {
            if (maxAttempts < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("鏈€澶ч噸璇曟鏁板繀椤?>= 0");
            if (initialDelayMs <= 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("鍒濆寤惰繜蹇呴』 > 0");
            if (maxDelayMs < initialDelayMs) throw new IllegalArgumentException("鏈€澶у欢杩熷繀椤?>= 鍒濆寤惰繜");
            this.maxAttempts = maxAttempts;
            this.initialDelayMs = initialDelayMs;
            this.maxDelayMs = maxDelayMs;
        }

        @Override
        public boolean canRetry(int attempt) {
            return attempt >= 0 && attempt < maxAttempts;
        }

        @Override
        public long getDelayMillis(int attempt) {
            long delay = initialDelayMs * (1L << attempt);
            return Math.min(delay, maxDelayMs);
        }

        @Override
        public int getMaxAttempts() {
            return maxAttempts;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 鍥哄畾闂撮殧閲嶈瘯绛栫暐瀹炵幇
     */
    class FixedIntervalRetryPolicy implements RetryPolicy {
        private final int maxAttempts;
        private final long intervalMs;

        FixedIntervalRetryPolicy(int maxAttempts, long intervalMs) {
            if (maxAttempts < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("鏈€澶ч噸璇曟鏁板繀椤?>= 0");
            if (intervalMs <= 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("闂撮殧鏃堕棿蹇呴』 > 0");
            this.maxAttempts = maxAttempts;
            this.intervalMs = intervalMs;
        }

        @Override
        public boolean canRetry(int attempt) {
            return attempt >= 0 && attempt < maxAttempts;
        }

        @Override
        public long getDelayMillis(int attempt) {
            return intervalMs;
        }

        @Override
        public int getMaxAttempts() {
            return maxAttempts;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 姣忔潯娑堟伅鐨勯噸璇曠姸鎬佽窡韪櫒
     *
     * 灏佽浜嗘秷鎭骇鍒殑閲嶈瘯璁℃暟鍣紝閬垮厤璋冪敤鏂规墜鍔ㄧ鐞嗛噸璇曟鏁般€?
     * 绾跨▼瀹夊叏锛屾敮鎸佸苟鍙戝満鏅笅鐨勫師瀛愭搷浣溿€?
     *
     * <b>浣跨敤绀轰緥锛?/b>
     * <pre>{@code
     * RetryPolicy policy = RetryPolicy.exponentialBackoff(3, 1000, 30000);
     * RetryState state = policy.createState();
     *
     * while (state.canRetry()) {
     *     try {
     *         processMessage(msg);
     *         state.markSuccess();
     *         break;
     *     } catch (Exception e) {
     *         if (!state.tryIncrement()) {
     *             log.error("娑堟伅閲嶈瘯娆℃暟宸茬敤灏?);
     *             break;
     *         }
     *         Thread.sleep(state.getDelayMillis());
     *     }
     * }
     * }</pre>
     */
    class RetryState {
        private final RetryPolicy policy;
        private final int maxRetries;
        private volatile int attemptCount;
        private volatile boolean success;

        private RetryState(RetryPolicy policy, int maxRetries) {
            this.policy = policy;
            this.maxRetries = maxRetries;
            this.attemptCount = 0;
            this.success = false;
        }

        /**
         * 鍒ゆ柇鏄惁鍙互缁х画閲嶈瘯
         *
         * @return true 琛ㄧず鍙互閲嶈瘯锛宖alse 琛ㄧず宸茶揪鏈€澶ч噸璇曟鏁?
         */
        public boolean canRetry() {
            return !success && attemptCount < maxRetries;
        }

        /**
         * 鍘熷瓙鎬у湴澧炲姞閲嶈瘯璁℃暟
         *
         * @return true 琛ㄧず鎴愬姛閫掑锛堟湭瓒呴檺锛夛紝false 琛ㄧず宸茶揪鏈€澶ч噸璇曟鏁?
         */
        public synchronized boolean tryIncrement() {
            if (attemptCount >= maxRetries) {
                return false;
            }
            attemptCount++;
            return true;
        }

        /**
         * 鑾峰彇褰撳墠閲嶈瘯娆℃暟
         *
         * @return 褰撳墠閲嶈瘯娆℃暟锛堜粠 0 寮€濮嬶級
         */
        public int getAttemptCount() {
            return attemptCount;
        }

        /**
         * 鑾峰彇涓嬫閲嶈瘯鐨勫欢杩熸椂闂达紙姣锛?
         *
         * @return 寤惰繜鏃堕棿
         */
        public long getDelayMillis() {
            return policy.getDelayMillis(attemptCount);
        }

        /**
         * 鏍囪娑堟伅澶勭悊鎴愬姛
         */
        public void markSuccess() {
            this.success = true;
        }

        /**
         * 鏄惁宸插鐞嗘垚鍔?
         *
         * @return true 琛ㄧず鎴愬姛锛宖alse 琛ㄧず浠嶅湪閲嶈瘯鎴栧凡鑰楀敖
         */
        public boolean isSuccess() {
            return success;
        }

        /**
         * 鏄惁宸茶€楀敖鎵€鏈夐噸璇曟鏁?
         *
         * @return true 琛ㄧず宸茶€楀敖锛宖alse 琛ㄧず浠嶆湁閲嶈瘯鏈轰細
         */
        public boolean isExhausted() {
            return attemptCount >= maxRetries && !success;
        }

        /**
         * 鑾峰彇鏈€澶ч噸璇曟鏁?
         *
         * @return 鏈€澶ч噸璇曟鏁?
         */
        public int getMaxRetries() {
            return maxRetries;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 鍒涘缓娑堟伅绾у埆鐨勯噸璇曠姸鎬佽窡韪櫒
     *
     * @return 閲嶈瘯鐘舵€佸疄渚?
     */
    default RetryState createState() {
        return new RetryState(this, getMaxAttempts());
    }
}

package com.remisoft.comm.queue.scheduler;

import com.remisoft.comm.queue.config.QueueProperties;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.DeadLetterQueueService;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;

/**
 * 姝讳俊闃熷垪鑷姩閲嶈瘯璋冨害鍣?
 *
 * <p>瀹氭湡鎵弿姝讳俊闃熷垪骞惰嚜鍔ㄩ噸璇曞け璐ユ秷鎭€傚綋娑堟伅瓒呰繃鏈€澶ч噸璇曟鏁板悗灏嗚姘镐箙鍒犻櫎銆?
 *
 * <p><b>璋冨害绛栫暐锛?/b>
 * <ul>
 *   <li>榛樿姣?60 绉掓墽琛屼竴娆℃壂鎻忥紙鍙€氳繃 deadLetterRetryInterval 閰嶇疆锛?/li>
 *   <li>姣忔鎵弿閬嶅巻鎵€鏈変富棰樼殑姝讳俊娑堟伅</li>
 *   <li>瓒呰繃鏈€澶ч噸璇曟鏁扮殑娑堟伅灏嗚姘镐箙鍒犻櫎</li>
 *   <li>璁板綍姣忔閲嶈瘯鐨勮缁嗘棩蹇?/li>
 * </ul>
 *
 * <p><b>閰嶇疆椤癸細</b>
 * <pre>{@code
 * remi.queue:
 *   deadLetterRetryEnabled: true    # 鏄惁鍚敤鑷姩閲嶈瘯
 *   deadLetterMaxRetries: 3         # 鏈€澶ч噸璇曟鏁?
 *   deadLetterRetryInterval: 60000  # 閲嶈瘯闂撮殧锛堟绉掞級
 * }</pre>
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
public class DeadLetterRetryScheduler {

    private final DeadLetterQueueService deadLetterQueueService;
    private final QueueProperties queueProperties;

    public DeadLetterRetryScheduler(DeadLetterQueueService deadLetterQueueService,
                                    QueueProperties queueProperties) {
        this.deadLetterQueueService = deadLetterQueueService;
        this.queueProperties = queueProperties;
    }

    /**
     * 瀹氭椂鎵弿骞堕噸璇曟淇￠槦鍒椾腑鐨勬秷鎭?
     * <p>鎵ц闂撮殧鐢?queueProperties.deadLetterRetryInterval 閰嶇疆鍐冲畾
     */
    @Scheduled(fixedDelayString = "${remi.queue.deadLetterRetryInterval:60000}",
               initialDelayString = "${remi.queue.deadLetterRetryInterval:60000}")
    public void scanAndRetry() {
        if (!queueProperties.resolvedDeadLetterRetryEnabled()) {
            log.debug("[DeadLetterRetryScheduler] 姝讳俊闃熷垪鑷姩閲嶈瘯宸茬鐢紝璺宠繃鎵弿");
            return;
        }

        try {
            int totalSuccess = deadLetterQueueService.retryAll();
            if (totalSuccess > 0) {
                log.info("[DeadLetterRetryScheduler] 姝讳俊闃熷垪鎵弿瀹屾垚锛屾垚鍔熼噸璇?{} 鏉℃秷鎭?, totalSuccess);
            } else {
                log.debug("[DeadLetterRetryScheduler] 姝讳俊闃熷垪鎵弿瀹屾垚锛屾棤闇€瑕侀噸璇曠殑娑堟伅");
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[DeadLetterRetryScheduler] 姝讳俊闃熷垪鎵弿澶辫触: {}", e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }
}

    List<String> queryDeadLetters(String topic, int limit);

    int retryAll();

import com.remisoft.comm.queue.domain.QueueMessage;

/**
 * 娑堟伅鍙戝竷鑰呮帴鍙?
 *
 * <p>瀹氫箟娑堟伅鍙戝竷鐨勬爣鍑嗘搷浣滐紝鏀寔鍙戝竷瀛楃涓叉秷鎭拰缁熶竴娑堟伅妯″瀷銆?

 * @author Marvin Lee

public interface IMessagePublisher {

    /**
     * 鍙戝竷瀛楃涓叉秷鎭?
     *
     * <p>灏嗗瓧绗︿覆娑堟伅鍙戝竷鍒伴槦鍒楅€氶亾銆傚鏋滄秷鎭牸寮忕鍚?QueueMessage JSON 缁撴瀯锛?
     * 浼氳姝ｇ‘瑙ｆ瀽锛涘惁鍒欎細浣滀负鏅€氭枃鏈鐞嗐€?
     *
     * @param message 寰呭彂甯冪殑瀛楃涓叉秷鎭?
     */
    void publish(String message);

    /**
     * 鍙戝竷 QueueMessage 娑堟伅
     *
     * <p>鍙戝竷缁熶竴娑堟伅妯″瀷锛屾敮鎸佹秷鎭ご銆佽拷韪狪D绛夊厓鏁版嵁銆?
     * 榛樿瀹炵幇浼氬皢娑堟伅搴忓垪鍖栦负 JSON 鍚庤皟鐢?publish(String) 鏂规硶銆?
     *
     * @param message 寰呭彂甯冪殑娑堟伅瀵硅薄
     */
    default void publish(QueueMessage message) {
        publish(QueueMessage.toPayload(message));
    }

    /**
     * 鍙戝竷甯﹀欢杩熺殑娑堟伅
     *
     * <p>鏌愪簺闃熷垪瀹炵幇鏀寔寤惰繜娑堟伅锛岃鏂规硶鐢ㄤ簬鍙戝竷闇€瑕佸欢杩熷鐞嗙殑娑堟伅銆?
     * 榛樿瀹炵幇鐩存帴璋冪敤 publish(message)锛屼笉鏀寔寤惰繜鐨勯槦鍒椾細蹇界暐姝ゅ弬鏁般€?
     *
     * @param message   寰呭彂甯冪殑娑堟伅
     * @param delayMillis 寤惰繜鏃堕棿锛堟绉掞級
     */
    default void publishDelayed(QueueMessage message, long delayMillis) {
        publish(message);
    }

    /**
     * 鍙戝竷椤哄簭娑堟伅
     *
     * <p>椤哄簭娑堟伅淇濊瘉鍚屼竴娑堟伅缁勫唴鐨勬秷鎭寜椤哄簭琚秷璐广€?
     * 鐩稿悓 messageGroupKey 鐨勬秷鎭細琚矾鐢卞埌鍚屼竴闃熷垪鍒嗗尯锛屼繚璇侀『搴忔€с€?
     *
     * <p><b>浣跨敤绀轰緥锛?/b>
     * <pre>{@code
     * // 鍙戝竷椤哄簭娑堟伅
     * QueueMessage message = QueueMessage.of("order data");
     * message.setSequential("order-123", 1L);
     * publisher.publishSequential(message);
     * }</pre>
     *
     * @param message 寰呭彂甯冪殑椤哄簭娑堟伅锛堝繀椤昏缃?messageGroupKey锛?
     */
    default void publishSequential(QueueMessage message) {
        if (message == null || !message.isSequential()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("椤哄簭娑堟伅蹇呴』璁剧疆 messageGroupKey");
        }
        publish(message);
    }

    /**
     * 鎵归噺鍙戝竷娑堟伅
     *
     * <p>灏嗗鏉℃秷鎭壒閲忓彂甯冨埌闃熷垪閫氶亾锛屾彁楂樻壒閲忔搷浣滅殑鏁堢巼銆?
     * 榛樿瀹炵幇鏄惊鐜皟鐢?publish() 鏂规硶锛屽瓙绫诲彲浠ヨ鐩栦互浼樺寲鎬ц兘銆?
     *
     * @param messages 寰呭彂甯冪殑瀛楃涓叉秷鎭暟缁?
     */
    default void publishBatch(String... messages) {
        if (messages == null || messages.length == 0) {
            return;
        }
        for (String message : messages) {
            publish(message);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇鍙戝竷鑰呭叧鑱旂殑閫氶亾鍚嶇О

     */
    default String getChannel() {
        return null;
    }

     * @return true 濡傛灉鍙互姝ｅ父鍙戝竷娑堟伅锛宖alse 鍚﹀垯
     */
    default boolean isActive() {
        return true;
    }

    /**
     * 鍏抽棴鍙戝竷鑰?
     *
     * <p>閲婃斁鍙戝竷鑰呭崰鐢ㄧ殑璧勬簮锛屽鍏抽棴杩炴帴绛夈€?

     */
    default void close() {
    }
}

/**
 * 娑堟伅璁㈤槄鑰呮帴鍙?

 * <p>璇ユ帴鍙ｅ畾涔変簡娑堟伅闃熷垪鐨勬秷璐硅€呰涓猴紝鍖呮嫭鍚屾娑堣垂鍜屽紓姝ユ秷璐逛袱绉嶆ā寮忋€?

public interface IMessageSubscriber {

    /**

     *   <li>闇€瑕佺簿纭帶鍒舵秷璐硅妭濂?/li>
     * </ul>
     *
     * @return 娑堣垂鍒扮殑娑堟伅锛屾棤娑堟伅鏃惰繑鍥?null
     */
    String subscribe();

    /**
     * 鍚屾娑堣垂娑堟伅锛堣繑鍥炵粨鏋勫寲娑堟伅锛?

     * 杩斿洖 {@link QueueMessage} 瀵硅薄锛屽寘鍚秷鎭綋鍙婂厓鏁版嵁銆?
     *

     *   <li>闇€瑕佽闂秷鎭ご銆佷紭鍏堢骇绛夊厓鏁版嵁</li>

    default QueueMessage subscribeMessage() {
        String message = subscribe();
        return message != null ? QueueMessage.fromPayload(message) : null;
    }

    /**

    default String subscribeOnce(IMessageHandler handler) {
        QueueMessage message = subscribeMessage();
        if (message == null || handler == null) {
            return null;
        }
        try {
            handler.onMessage(message);
            return message.getTraceId();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new InfrastructureException("娑堟伅澶勭悊澶辫触: " + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }

=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\service\impl\DeadLetterQueueServiceImpl.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.impl;

import com.remisoft.comm.util.json.JsonUtils;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.config.QueueProperties;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.domain.QueueMessage;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.DeadLetterQueueService;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessagePublisher;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.queue.IMessageQueueProvider;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.Cursor;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.ScanOptions;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

/**
 * 姝讳俊闃熷垪鏈嶅姟瀹炵幇绫?
 * <p>鍩轰簬 Redis Hash 瀛樺偍姝讳俊娑堟伅锛屾彁渚涢噸璇曘€佹煡璇㈠拰缁熻鑳藉姏
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
public class DeadLetterQueueServiceImpl implements DeadLetterQueueService {

    private static final String DLQ_KEY_PREFIX = "remi:queue:dlq:";
    private static final String DLQ_RETRY_KEY_PREFIX = "remi:queue:dlq:retry:";
    private static final DateTimeFormatter FORMATTER = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss.SSS");

    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final IMessageQueueProvider queueProvider;
    private final QueueProperties queueProperties;

    public DeadLetterQueueServiceImpl(RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate,
                                      IMessageQueueProvider queueProvider,
                                      QueueProperties queueProperties) {
        this.redisTemplate = redisTemplate;
        this.queueProvider = queueProvider;
        this.queueProperties = queueProperties;
    }

    @Override
    public void sendToDeadLetter(String topic, String messageId, String messageBody, String failureReason) {
        String dlqKey = DLQ_KEY_PREFIX + topic;
        String retryKey = DLQ_RETRY_KEY_PREFIX + topic;

        DeadLetterMessage dlqMessage = new DeadLetterMessage();
        dlqMessage.setMessageId(messageId);
        dlqMessage.setMessageBody(messageBody);
        dlqMessage.setFailureReason(failureReason);
        dlqMessage.setEnterTime(LocalDateTime.now().format(FORMATTER));
        dlqMessage.setRetryCount(0);

        String dlqMessageJson = JsonUtils.toJson(dlqMessage);
        redisTemplate.opsForHash().put(dlqKey, messageId, dlqMessageJson);
        redisTemplate.opsForHash().put(retryKey, messageId, "0");

        // 娉ㄦ剰锛氫笉璁剧疆鏁翠釜 Hash key 鐨?TTL锛屽洜涓烘瘡娆℃柊澧炴秷鎭細閲嶇疆 TTL 瀵艰嚧鏃ф秷鎭案涓嶈繃鏈熴€?
        // 鏀逛负渚濊禆瀹氭椂浠诲姟 cleanExpiredDeadLetters() 娓呯悊瓒呰繃 7 澶╃殑鍗曟潯娑堟伅銆?

        log.info("[DeadLetterQueue] 娑堟伅杩涘叆姝讳俊闃熷垪: topic={}, messageId={}, reason={}",
                topic, messageId, failureReason);
    }

    @Override
    public List<String> queryDeadLetters(String topic, int limit) {
        String dlqKey = DLQ_KEY_PREFIX + topic;
        List<Object> values = redisTemplate.opsForHash().values(dlqKey);
        if (values == null || values.isEmpty()) {
            return Collections.emptyList();
        }
        return values.stream()
                .limit(limit)
                .map(Object::toString)
                .toList();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean retry(String topic, String messageId) {
        String dlqKey = DLQ_KEY_PREFIX + topic;
        String retryKey = DLQ_RETRY_KEY_PREFIX + topic;

        Object dlqMessageObj = redisTemplate.opsForHash().get(dlqKey, messageId);
        if (dlqMessageObj == null) {
            log.warn("[DeadLetterQueue] 閲嶈瘯澶辫触锛屾秷鎭笉瀛樺湪: topic={}, messageId={}", topic, messageId);
            return false;
        }

        int maxRetries = queueProperties.resolvedDeadLetterMaxRetries();
        int currentRetryCount = getRetryCount(topic, messageId);

        if (currentRetryCount >= maxRetries) {
            log.warn("[DeadLetterQueue] 娑堟伅宸茶揪鍒版渶澶ч噸璇曟鏁帮紝姘镐箙鍒犻櫎: topic={}, messageId={}, retries={}",
                    topic, messageId, currentRetryCount);
            removeDeadLetter(topic, messageId);
            return false;
        }

        DeadLetterMessage dlqMessage = JsonUtils.fromJson(dlqMessageObj.toString(), DeadLetterMessage.class);
        QueueMessage queueMessage = QueueMessage.fromPayload(dlqMessage.getMessageBody());
        if (queueMessage != null) {
            queueMessage.setRetryCount(currentRetryCount + 1);
        }

        try {
            IMessagePublisher publisher = queueProvider.createMessageQueue(queueProperties.resolvedType())
                    .createPublisher(topic);
            publisher.publish(queueMessage != null ? queueMessage : QueueMessage.of(dlqMessage.getMessageBody()));

            // 閲嶈瘯鎴愬姛鍚庝粠姝讳俊闃熷垪褰诲簳绉婚櫎锛堜笉鍐嶄繚鐣?retryCount锛?
            redisTemplate.opsForHash().delete(dlqKey, messageId);
            redisTemplate.opsForHash().delete(retryKey, messageId);

            log.info("[DeadLetterQueue] 娑堟伅閲嶈瘯鎴愬姛: topic={}, messageId={}, retryCount={}",
                    topic, messageId, currentRetryCount + 1);
            return true;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            // 閲嶈瘯澶辫触锛氭洿鏂?retryCount锛屼繚鐣欏湪姝讳俊闃熷垪涓瓑寰呬笅娆￠噸璇?
            redisTemplate.opsForHash().put(retryKey, messageId, String.valueOf(currentRetryCount + 1));
            log.error("[DeadLetterQueue] 娑堟伅閲嶈瘯澶辫触: topic={}, messageId={}, error={}",
                    topic, messageId, e.getMessage(), e);
            return false;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 娓呯悊杩囨湡鐨勬淇℃秷鎭€?
     *
     * <p>閬嶅巻鎵€鏈夋淇￠槦鍒楋紝鍒犻櫎杩涘叆鏃堕棿瓒呰繃 7 澶╃殑娑堟伅銆?
     * 搴旂敱瀹氭椂浠诲姟锛堝 DeadLetterRetryScheduler锛夊畾鏈熻皟鐢ㄣ€?
     *
     * @return 娓呯悊鐨勬秷鎭暟閲?
     */
    public int cleanExpiredDeadLetters() {
        int cleanedCount = 0;
        long maxAgeMillis = TimeUnit.DAYS.toMillis(7);
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        DateTimeFormatter formatter = FORMATTER;

        ScanOptions scanOptions = ScanOptions.scanOptions().match(DLQ_KEY_PREFIX + "*").count(100).build();
        try (Cursor<String> cursor = redisTemplate.scan(scanOptions)) {
            while (cursor.hasNext()) {
                String dlqKey = cursor.next();
                // 璺宠繃 retry key
                if (dlqKey.startsWith(DLQ_RETRY_KEY_PREFIX)) {
                    continue;
                }
                String topic = dlqKey.substring(DLQ_KEY_PREFIX.length());
                Map<Object, Object> entries = redisTemplate.opsForHash().entries(dlqKey);
                if (entries == null || entries.isEmpty()) {
                    continue;
                }

                for (Map.Entry<Object, Object> entry : entries.entrySet()) {
                    String messageId = entry.getKey().toString();
                    try {
                        DeadLetterMessage msg = JsonUtils.fromJson(entry.getValue().toString(), DeadLetterMessage.class);
                        if (msg != null && msg.getEnterTime() != null) {
                            LocalDateTime enterTime = LocalDateTime.parse(msg.getEnterTime(), formatter);
                            long ageMillis = now - enterTime.atZone(java.time.ZoneId.systemDefault()).toInstant().toEpochMilli();
                            if (ageMillis > maxAgeMillis) {
                                redisTemplate.opsForHash().delete(dlqKey, messageId);
                                redisTemplate.opsForHash().delete(DLQ_RETRY_KEY_PREFIX + topic, messageId);
                                cleanedCount++;
                            }
                        }
                    } catch (Exception e) {
                        log.debug("[DeadLetterQueue] 娓呯悊杩囨湡娑堟伅瑙ｆ瀽澶辫触: topic={}, messageId={}", topic, messageId);
                    }
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[DeadLetterQueue] 娓呯悊杩囨湡姝讳俊娑堟伅澶辫触: {}", e.getMessage(), e);
        }

        if (cleanedCount > 0) {
            log.info("[DeadLetterQueue] 娓呯悊杩囨湡姝讳俊娑堟伅瀹屾垚: cleanedCount={}", cleanedCount);
        }
        return cleanedCount;
    }

    @Override
    public int retryAll() {
        int successCount = 0;

        // 浣跨敤 SCAN 鏇夸唬 KEYS锛岄伩鍏嶉樆濉?Redis
        ScanOptions scanOptions = ScanOptions.scanOptions().match(DLQ_KEY_PREFIX + "*").count(100).build();
        try (Cursor<String> cursor = redisTemplate.scan(scanOptions)) {
            while (cursor.hasNext()) {
                String dlqKey = cursor.next();
                String topic = dlqKey.substring(DLQ_KEY_PREFIX.length());
                Map<Object, Object> entries = redisTemplate.opsForHash().entries(dlqKey);
                if (entries == null || entries.isEmpty()) {
                    continue;
                }

                for (Object messageIdObj : entries.keySet()) {
                    String messageId = messageIdObj.toString();
                    if (retry(topic, messageId)) {
                        successCount++;
                    }
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[DeadLetterQueue] 鎵归噺閲嶈瘯澶辫触: {}", e.getMessage(), e);
        }

        log.info("[DeadLetterQueue] 鎵归噺閲嶈瘯瀹屾垚: successCount={}", successCount);
        return successCount;
    }

    @Override
    public int getDeadLetterCount(String topic) {
        String dlqKey = DLQ_KEY_PREFIX + topic;
        Long size = redisTemplate.opsForHash().size(dlqKey);
        return size != null ? size.intValue() : 0;
    }

    @Override
    public int getRetryCount(String topic, String messageId) {
        String retryKey = DLQ_RETRY_KEY_PREFIX + topic;
        Object countObj = redisTemplate.opsForHash().get(retryKey, messageId);
        if (countObj == null) {
            return 0;
        }
        try {
            return Integer.parseInt(countObj.toString());
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            return 0;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 浠庢淇￠槦鍒椾腑绉婚櫎鎸囧畾娑堟伅
     *
     * @param topic     涓婚
     * @param messageId 娑堟伅 ID
     */
    private void removeDeadLetter(String topic, String messageId) {
        String dlqKey = DLQ_KEY_PREFIX + topic;
        String retryKey = DLQ_RETRY_KEY_PREFIX + topic;
        redisTemplate.opsForHash().delete(dlqKey, messageId);
        redisTemplate.opsForHash().delete(retryKey, messageId);
        log.info("[DeadLetterQueue] 娑堟伅宸蹭粠姝讳俊闃熷垪姘镐箙鍒犻櫎: topic={}, messageId={}", topic, messageId);
    }

    /**
     * 姝讳俊娑堟伅鍐呴儴妯″瀷
     */
    @SuppressWarnings("unused")
    private static class DeadLetterMessage {
        private String messageId;
        private String messageBody;
        private String failureReason;
        private String enterTime;
        private int retryCount;

        public String getMessageId() {
            return messageId;
        }

        public void setMessageId(String messageId) {
            this.messageId = messageId;
        }

        public String getMessageBody() {
            return messageBody;
        }

        public void setMessageBody(String messageBody) {
            this.messageBody = messageBody;
        }

        public String getFailureReason() {
            return failureReason;
        }

        public void setFailureReason(String failureReason) {
            this.failureReason = failureReason;
        }

        public String getEnterTime() {
            return enterTime;
        }

        public void setEnterTime(String enterTime) {
            this.enterTime = enterTime;
        }

        public int getRetryCount() {
            return retryCount;
        }

        public void setRetryCount(int retryCount) {
            this.retryCount = retryCount;
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\service\impl\RedisListPublisher.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.impl;

import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessagePublisher;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisTemplate;

/**
 * 鍩轰簬 Redis List 鐨勬秷鎭彂甯冭€?
 *
 * <p>浣跨敤 Redis RPUSH 鍛戒护灏嗘秷鎭啓鍏?List锛屽疄鐜板厛杩涘厛鍑猴紙FIFO锛夌殑娑堟伅鍙戝竷銆?
 * 閫氳繃 {@link RedisTemplate} 澶嶇敤 remi-comm-redis 鐨勮繛鎺ャ€?
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
public class RedisListPublisher implements IMessagePublisher {

    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final String channel;

    public RedisListPublisher(RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate, String channel) {
        if (redisTemplate == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("RedisTemplate 涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        if (channel == null || channel.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("閫氶亾鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        this.redisTemplate = redisTemplate;
        this.channel = channel;
    }

    @Override
    public void publish(String message) {
        if (message == null) {
            return;
        }
        redisTemplate.opsForList().rightPush(channel, message);
    }

    @Override
    public String getChannel() {
        return channel;
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\service\impl\RedisListSubscriber.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.impl;

import com.remisoft.comm.queue.config.QueueProperties;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.domain.QueueMessage;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.rate.ConsumerRateLimiter;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.recovery.ConsumerThreadGuard;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessageHandler;
import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessageSubscriber;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisTemplate;

import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference;

/**
 * 鍩轰簬 Redis List 鐨勬秷鎭闃呰€?
 *
 * <p>浣跨敤 Redis BRPOPLPUSH 鍘熷瓙鎿嶄綔浠庝富闃熷垪寮瑰嚭娑堟伅骞舵帹鍏ュ浠介槦鍒楋紝
 * 澶勭悊瀹屾垚鍚庡啀浠庡浠介槦鍒楀垹闄わ紝纭繚娑堟伅澶勭悊澶辫触鏃朵笉浼氫涪澶便€?
 * 閫氳繃 {@link RedisTemplate} 澶嶇敤 remi-comm-redis 鐨勮繛鎺ャ€?
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
@Slf4j
public class RedisListSubscriber implements IMessageSubscriber {

    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final String channel;
    private final String backupChannel;
    private final int blockTimeoutSeconds;

    /**
     * 澶囦唤闃熷垪鏈€澶ч暱搴﹂檺鍒讹紝瓒呰繃姝ゅ€兼椂瑙﹀彂娓呯悊鍜屽憡璀?
     * 榛樿 10000 鏉?
     */
    private final int maxBackupQueueSize;

    private final AtomicBoolean running;
    private final AtomicLong consumedCount;
    private final AtomicLong trimmedCount;
    private final AtomicReference<Throwable> lastError;
    private final ConsumerRateLimiter rateLimiter;
    private final ExecutorService consumerExecutor;

    private volatile Thread consumerThread;
    private volatile ConsumerThreadGuard threadGuard;

    public RedisListSubscriber(RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate, String channel,
                               int blockTimeoutSeconds, ExecutorService consumerExecutor) {
        this(redisTemplate, channel, blockTimeoutSeconds, 10000,
                new ConsumerRateLimiter(0), consumerExecutor);
    }

    public RedisListSubscriber(RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate, String channel,
                               int blockTimeoutSeconds, int maxBackupQueueSize, ExecutorService consumerExecutor) {
        this(redisTemplate, channel, blockTimeoutSeconds, maxBackupQueueSize,
                new ConsumerRateLimiter(0), consumerExecutor);
    }

    public RedisListSubscriber(RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate, String channel,
                               int blockTimeoutSeconds, int maxBackupQueueSize,
                               QueueProperties queueProperties, ExecutorService consumerExecutor) {
        this(redisTemplate, channel, blockTimeoutSeconds, maxBackupQueueSize,
                queueProperties != null ? queueProperties.createRateLimiter() : new ConsumerRateLimiter(0),
                consumerExecutor);
    }

    private RedisListSubscriber(RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate, String channel,
                                int blockTimeoutSeconds, int maxBackupQueueSize,
                                ConsumerRateLimiter rateLimiter, ExecutorService consumerExecutor) {
        if (redisTemplate == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("RedisTemplate 涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        if (channel == null || channel.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("閫氶亾鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        if (maxBackupQueueSize <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("澶囦唤闃熷垪鏈€澶ч暱搴﹀繀椤诲ぇ浜?0");
        }
        this.redisTemplate = redisTemplate;
        this.channel = channel;
        this.backupChannel = channel + ":backup";
        this.blockTimeoutSeconds = blockTimeoutSeconds;
        this.maxBackupQueueSize = maxBackupQueueSize;
        this.running = new AtomicBoolean(false);
        this.consumedCount = new AtomicLong(0);
        this.trimmedCount = new AtomicLong(0);
        this.lastError = new AtomicReference<>();
        this.rateLimiter = rateLimiter != null ? rateLimiter : new ConsumerRateLimiter(0);
        this.consumerExecutor = consumerExecutor;
    }

    @Override
    public String subscribe() {
        try {
            if (blockTimeoutSeconds > 0) {
                // 浣跨敤 BRPOPLPUSH 鍘熷瓙鎿嶄綔锛氫粠 channel 寮瑰嚭娑堟伅骞舵帹鍏?backupChannel
                // 杩斿洖娑堟伅鍐呭锛堜笉鍚?key 鍚嶏級
                Object message = redisTemplate.execute(connection -> {
                    byte[] result = connection.listCommands()
                            .bRPopLPush(blockTimeoutSeconds, channel.getBytes(), backupChannel.getBytes());
                    return result;
                }, true);
                if (message != null) {
                    return new String((byte[]) message);
                }
                return null;
            } else {
                Object value = redisTemplate.opsForList().rightPop(channel);
                return value != null ? String.valueOf(value) : null;
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            lastError.set(e);
            log.error("[RedisList] 鎷夊彇娑堟伅寮傚父锛宑hannel={}", channel, e);
            return null;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public String subscribeAsync(IMessageHandler handler) {
        if (!running.compareAndSet(false, true)) {
            log.warn("[RedisList] 璁㈤槄鑰呭凡鍦ㄨ繍琛屼腑锛宑hannel={}", channel);
            return channel;
        }
        threadGuard = new ConsumerThreadGuard("redis-list-" + channel, 10, consumerExecutor);
        threadGuard.start(() -> consumeLoop(handler));
        log.info("[RedisList] 寮傛娑堣垂宸插惎鍔紙ConsumerThreadGuard瀹堟姢锛夛紝channel={}", channel);
        return channel;
    }

    @Override
    public void stop() {
        if (!running.compareAndSet(true, false)) {
            return;
        }
        if (threadGuard != null) {
            threadGuard.stop();
        }
        log.info("[RedisList] 鏀跺埌鍋滄淇″彿锛宑hannel={}", channel);
        if (consumerThread != null) {
            consumerThread.interrupt();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isRunning() {
        return running.get();
    }

    @Override
    public Object getChannel() {
        return channel;
    }

    @Override
    public String getConsumerId() {
        return channel;
    }

    @Override
    public int getConsumedCount() {
        return (int) consumedCount.get();
    }

    @Override
    public Throwable getLastError() {
        return lastError.get();
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇澶囦唤闃熷垪褰撳墠闀垮害
     *
     * @return 澶囦唤闃熷垪闀垮害
     */
    public long getBackupQueueSize() {
        try {
            Long size = redisTemplate.opsForList().size(backupChannel);
            return size != null ? size : 0;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return 0;
        }
    }

    /**
     * 鑾峰彇宸叉竻鐞嗙殑澶囦唤闃熷垪娑堟伅鎬绘暟
     *
     * @return 娓呯悊鏁伴噺
     */
    public long getTrimmedCount() {
        return trimmedCount.get();
    }

    private void consumeLoop(IMessageHandler handler) {
        try {
            while (running.get()) {
                try {
                    // 姣忔娑堣垂鍓嶆鏌ュ浠介槦鍒楅暱搴?
                    trimBackupQueueIfExceeded();

                    String message = subscribe();
                    if (message != null) {
                        rateLimiter.acquire();
                        processMessage(message, handler);
                    }
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    lastError.set(e);
                    log.error("[RedisList] 娑堣垂寰幆寮傚父锛宑hannel={}", channel, e);
                    sleepQuietly(1000);
                }
            }
        } finally {
            running.set(false);
            log.info("[RedisList] 娑堣垂寰幆宸查€€鍑猴紝channel={}", channel);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 妫€鏌ュ苟娓呯悊澶囦唤闃熷垪锛堣秴杩囨渶澶ч暱搴︽椂浠庡乏渚хЩ闄ゆ渶鏃ф秷鎭級
     */
    private void trimBackupQueueIfExceeded() {
        try {
            Long size = redisTemplate.opsForList().size(backupChannel);
            if (size != null && size > maxBackupQueueSize) {
                long trimCount = size - maxBackupQueueSize;
                redisTemplate.opsForList().trim(backupChannel, trimCount, -1);
                trimmedCount.addAndGet(trimCount);
                log.warn("[RedisList] 澶囦唤闃熷垪闀垮害瓒呴檺({}>{})锛屽凡娓呯悊 {} 鏉℃渶鏃ф秷鎭紝channel={}",
                        size, maxBackupQueueSize, trimCount, channel);
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.warn("[RedisList] 澶囦唤闃熷垪娓呯悊澶辫触锛宑hannel={}", channel, e);
        }
    }

    private void processMessage(String message, IMessageHandler handler) {
        if (message == null) {
            return;
        }
        boolean ackSuccess = false;
        try {
            QueueMessage queueMessage = QueueMessage.fromPayload(message);
            if (queueMessage == null) {
                queueMessage = QueueMessage.of(message);
            }
            if (handler != null) {
                handler.onMessage(queueMessage);
            }
            ackSuccess = true;
            consumedCount.incrementAndGet();
            lastError.set(null);
            log.debug("[RedisList] 娑堟伅澶勭悊鎴愬姛锛宑hannel={}, traceId={}", channel, queueMessage.getTraceId());
        } catch (Exception e) {
            lastError.set(e);
            log.error("[RedisList] 娑堟伅澶勭悊寮傚父锛宑hannel={}锛屾秷鎭繚鐣欏湪澶囦唤闃熷垪绛夊緟鎭㈠", channel, e);
        } finally {
            // 澶勭悊鎴愬姛鍚庝粠澶囦唤闃熷垪鍒犻櫎娑堟伅锛涘鐞嗗け璐ュ垯淇濈暀鍦ㄥ浠介槦鍒?
            if (ackSuccess) {
                try {
                    redisTemplate.opsForList().remove(backupChannel, 1, message);
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    log.warn("[RedisList] 浠庡浠介槦鍒楀垹闄ゆ秷鎭け璐ワ紝channel={}", channel, e);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private void sleepQuietly(long millis) {
        try {
            Thread.sleep(millis);
        } catch (InterruptedException ignored) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
    }
}


=== FILE: com\remisoft\comm\queue\service\impl\RedisPubSubPublisher.java ===
package com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.impl;

import com.remisoft.comm.queue.service.IMessagePublisher;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisTemplate;

/**
 * 鍩轰簬 Redis PubSub 鐨勬秷鎭彂甯冭€?
 *
 * <p>浣跨敤 Redis PUBLISH 鍛戒护灏嗘秷鎭箍鎾埌鎸囧畾棰戦亾銆?
 * 閫氳繃 {@link RedisTemplate} 澶嶇敤 remi-comm-redis 鐨勮繛鎺ャ€?
 *
 * @author Marvin Lee
 * @email limw1888@126.com
 * @version 3.5.0
 * @since 1.0.0
 */
public class RedisPubSubPublisher implements IMessagePublisher {

    private final RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate;
    private final String channel;

    public RedisPubSubPublisher(RedisTemplate<String, Object> redisTemplate, String channel) {
        if (redisTemplate == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("RedisTemplate 涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        if (channel == null || channel.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("閫氶亾鍚嶇О涓嶈兘涓虹┖");
        }
        this.redisTemplate = redisTemplate;
        this.channel = channel;
    }

    @Override
    public void publish(String message) {
        if (message == null) {
            return;
        }
        redisTemplate.convertAndSend(channel, message);
    }

    @Override
    public String getChannel() {
        return channel;
    }
